# 第 20 章　欢迎来到乐园

*4.0 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Welcome to Arcadia　·　欢迎来到乐园

## 166　Phantasmoon, the Merciful Queen　幻月是慈悲的女王

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Party Car　|　角色：Trailblazer; Pom-Pom; Dan Heng; March 7th; Himeko; Welt; Jade; Sunday; Black Swan; Evanescia; Pearl　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Fallen Petals, Leave Fading Traces of Fragrance'' completed

### Story Recap

To address the fuel shortage, the Astral Express followed Miss Black Swan's suggestion to travel to Amphoreus, a place known as "The Eternal Land." However, soon after the arrival, March suddenly fell unconscious, and the landing that Dan Heng and I made on Amphoreus was anything but smooth.

After surviving the crash, the Trailblaze Squad met the local heroes known as the Chrysos Heirs. From them, they learned that the Titans sustaining the world were beginning to decay, and that Amphoreus itself was scarred and broken. Thus began the prologue of the Flame-Chase Journey...

At the end of this epic adventure, we uncovered the truth: Amphoreus was trapped within the "Eternal Recurrence" created by Phainon and Cyrene. Their goal was simple: to defy the Aeon and rewrite the world's fated end of Destruction.

Carrying on Phainon's legacy, we stepped once more into the cycle, determined to find a way to break free from fate. After thwarting the schemes of Zandar, the First Genius, and Evernight, we finally gathered together to face the Lord Ravager Irontomb.

The battle was so immense that it even drew the gaze of Nous. Herta took Irontomb's place, completing her self-coronation as the Lord Ravager fell. And Cyrene, the Child of Remembrance, gave her life so that the seeds of a new world could take root among the stars...

...And so, the epic of Amphoreus came to an unprecedented and romantic close. In Nous's extrapolations, the "Fourth Instant" had already begun its countdown...

With the spirit of Trailblaze shining in full, we brought this journey to a close in the purest, most poetic way imaginable. And as we bid farewell to the Eternal Land, the Astral Express set off once more toward its next destination.

**Himeko:** Every stop the Express makes is decided by all passengers together. Madam Jade, please give us some time.

**Jade:** Of course. Then I shall wait with anticipation.

> **苏醒的少女：**我说的没错吧，隔着重重帷幕窥视我等的…观测者？

**Awakening Girl:** I've been rambling on since the show just started... You must be totally lost, aren't you? Then just forget all that nonsense I was spouting.

> **苏醒的少女：**戏才开场，我就自顾自说了这么多…你一定一头雾水吧？那就忘掉我刚才的胡言乱语。

[Recap] The Astral Express entered Planarcadia and came to a stop at a platform. Phantasmoon's gaze followed the train, welcoming your arrival with a smile.

*(Cutscene plays)*

**Awakening Girl:** Anyway, stop looking here.

> **苏醒的少女：**总之，先别看这儿了。

**Awakening Girl:** What you should be focusing on... is them.

> **苏醒的少女：**你该关注的是…他们。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Pearl, CEO of Planarcadia, sent her greetings to the Express Crew, welcoming Navigator Himeko back home. Pearl mentioned that as the Phantasmoon waxed full once more, the Phantasmoon Games would begin as scheduled. Many distinguished cosmic guests would visit during this time, including envoys from the Xianzhou Yuque.

**March 7th:** So we're not just stopping in low orbit this time, but making a direct landing?

> **三月七：**这次竟然不是停靠在近地轨道，而是直接登陆吗？

**Himeko:** According to the comms, the officials have prepared a dedicated platform for the Astral Express to dock.

> **姬子：**按照通信说法，官方准备了一座站台，专供星穹列车停靠。

**March 7th:** A whole platform? Just for us? Is this some kind of VIP treatment for coming home? Talk about rolling out the red carpet!

> **三月七：**一整座站台？只给我们停靠？这就是回乡的待遇吗？真有面子！

**Pom-Pom:** The Express has arrived at the station. Connecting to local communications...

> **帕姆：**列车已入站。即将接入本地通讯——

**???:** From what I see, the Astral Express is considered a legend across the cosmos, so preparing a fitting gift would be more than appropriate.

> **？？？：**依我之见：星穹列车是银河中的传奇，准备一份恰如其分的礼物，合乎情理。

**Pearl:** Allow me to introduce myself. I'm Pearl from the IPC's Strategic Investment Department.

> **真珠：**初次见面，鄙人真珠，隶属公司战略投资部。

**Pearl:** Now, in my capacity as the legal representative and CEO of this arcadia, I welcome the Express Navigator, Miss Himeko, back home.

> **真珠：**如今，我以这座乐园法人代表与首席执行官的身份：欢迎列车领航员姬子小姐荣归故里。

**Himeko:** Hello, Miss Pearl. This is news to me. I wasn't aware that the Strategic Investment Department had taken over administration of Planarcadia.

> **姬子：**真珠小姐，你好。没想到…如今是战略投资部接管了「二相乐园」。

**Pearl:** As its governing official, I am still in the process of learning. If I can help this planet grow more prosperous, it would be a profound honor.

> **真珠：**作为治理者，我正在学习。若能令这颗星球更加繁荣，我将深感荣幸。

**Himeko:** We would be even more honored to have a face-to-face conversation with you.

> **姬子：**如果能与您当面交流，我们会更加荣幸。

**Pearl:** That's exactly what I wanted to say: While the Express is stationed here, the IPC will reasonably accommodate all your requests, including the one you just mentioned.

> **真珠：**这正是我想说的：列车停留期间，各位的要求，只要在合理范围内，公司会一应照办。包括你提出的这条。

**Pearl:** Pardon my choice of words, but I sincerely hope that you enjoy your time in Planarcadia so much you never want to leave.

> **真珠：**说法不太妥帖，但我衷心希望：二相乐园能让各位流连忘返。

**Himeko:** In that case, I'd like to know about recent events in this world. I heard from Madam Jade that it's once again the time of the full moon?

> **姬子：**既然如此，我想了解这个世界的近况。听翡翠女士说，又到月圆的时节了？

**Pearl:** Indeed. I believe it's also welcoming the Express.

> **真珠：**正是。我认为：它也在对列车表示欢迎。

**Pearl:** After fifteen years, now that the Phantasmoon of Elation is full again, the Phantasmoon Games will begin as scheduled.

> **真珠：**时隔十五年，随着「欢愉」的幻月再次满盈，「幻月游戏」总会如期开始。

**Pearl:** The more lively, the better! During these divine games, distinguished guests from across the cosmos will be stopping by.

> **真珠：**热热闹闹总是好的。这场神赐游戏进行期间，银河贵客们也会一一到访。

**Pearl:** This time, along with the Express, a delegate from the Xianzhou Alliance has also arrived. Don't be surprised if you happen to run into them.

> **真珠：**这次与列车一同抵达的，还有仙舟联盟的使节。如果各位偶然遇见，请不要意外。

**Dan Heng:** A Xianzhou envoy?

> **丹恒：**仙舟使节？

**Pearl:** That's right. A big shot from the Yuque.

> **真珠：**没错。「玉阙」的大人物。

**Pearl:** Since you've just arrived, I won't keep you any longer. I've arranged for staff to wait for you at the platform. Feel free to ask them for anything you need.

> **真珠：**各位方才抵达，便不多叨扰了。我安排了接待人员在站台上等候，尽可随意吩咐。

**Pearl:** All of this is a token of appreciation from the Strategic Investment Department to our friends. We hope you'll enjoy yourselves to the fullest.

> **真珠：**上述一切，都是战略投资部对朋友的谢礼：祝愿各位能尽情享受。

[Recap] The Express Crew split up to tackle different tasks. Himeko and Welt paid Pearl a visit, while Dan Heng and Sunday asked around about the Xianzhou envoys. Your mission with March 7th was to get some well-earned rest... but Himeko had a favor to ask of you two.

**Sunday:** Madam Pearl's style... is different from Aventurine's, but her methods are cut from the same cloth.

> **星期日：**这位女士…风格和砂金不同，手段却如出一辙。

**Welt:** Straight to the point, clean and decisive. It's obvious that "the Express not going to Astropolis" was part of their plan all along.

> **瓦尔特：**开门见山，干脆利落。看得出来，「列车不去千星城」早在他们的计划中。

**Himeko:** This could go on forever. They're really pulling out all the stops to form this "Anti-Destruction Alliance."

> **姬子：**这样下去没完没了。为促成「反毁灭同盟」，真是不惜血本啊。

**Himeko:** Looks like we need to pay them a visit, whether to understand the CEO's intentions or to firmly reject their proposal.

> **姬子：**看来，有必要登门拜访一次，不管是了解这位首席执行官的目的，还是旗帜鲜明地拒绝他们。

**Himeko:** At least in my homeland, we have more room to maneuver. Welt, care to join me?

> **姬子：**至少在我的故乡，我们有更多回旋的余地。瓦尔特，和我一起？

**Welt:** Looks like we don't get a vacation after all.

> **瓦尔特：**结果还是闲不下来啊。

**Himeko:** As for everyone else, you're free to do as you please. My suggestion is: Why not take a look around and get to know this world better?

> **姬子：**至于其他人，可以先自由行动。我的建议是：不妨四处逛逛，多了解下这个世界。

- ▸ This is also a stop on our Trailblaze journey.

> ▸ 这也是「开拓」的一站。

- ▸ I want to join the negotiations too.

> ▸ 我也想一起去谈判。

**Himeko:** I appreciate your enthusiasm, really. But I have something else I'd like you to help me with.

> **姬子：**难得你有这份心意，我心领了。但我有别的事想拜托你。

**Dan Heng:** In that case, Sunday and I will go gather some intel about the Xianzhou envoy.

> **丹恒：**既然这样，我和星期日去打听下仙舟使节的消息。

**March 7th:** Huh? Why you two?

> **三月七：**咦，为什么是你俩？

**Sunday:** I asked Dan Heng to bring me along.

> **星期日：**是我拜托丹恒带上我的。

**Sunday:** I can't appear in public given my identity. But I have a feeling that the Express might need to make some tough decisions in these complicated circumstances.

> **星期日：**碍于身份，我不便在公众场合露面。但我有种预感，列车或许要在复杂的形势下做出一些抉择。

**Sunday:** Understanding the Xianzhou Alliance's stance might help us on our journey.

> **星期日：**如果能弄清仙舟联盟的态度，也许会对旅程有所帮助。

**Dan Heng:** (Trailblazer), you had the toughest time in Amphoreus. Since we're splitting up, why not make your mission "take a break with March"?

> **丹恒：**开拓者，在翁法罗斯，你一直是最辛苦的那个。既然分头行动，不妨将自己的任务定为「陪三月好好休息」吧。

**Dan Heng:** I'm saying this from the bottom of my heart: I'm counting on you.

> **丹恒：**我的心里话：交给你了。

**Dan Heng:** Same goes for you, March.

> **丹恒：**你也一样，三月。

**March 7th:** Ugh, now we'll look like two immature kids just loafing around.

> **三月七：**哎呀，这下倒显得咱们两个不懂事的小娃娃游手好闲了。

- ▸ I'm already a grown-up.

> ▸ 我已经是大人了。

**March 7th:** Wow, you grew up pretty fast then!

> **三月七：**那你长得还挺快吼。

- ▸ I'm still just a kid.

> ▸ 我还是个孩子啊。

**March 7th:** Pretty proud of yourself, huh?

> **三月七：**你还挺得意哦？

**Himeko:** Don't worry, you two. Just make sure to come see me in the Parlor Car before you leave.

> **姬子：**两位小朋友也不用烦恼。出发前，记得来观景车厢找我一趟。

**Himeko:** I have a favor to ask of you both.

> **姬子：**我有件事要拜托你们。

### Head to the Parlor Car and hear Himeko's request

> *Planarcadia, a world blessed by Aha, Himeko's homeland, and now, the Astral Express's next stop. Ready to embark on this journey of Elation, (Trailblazer)?*

*(Talk to Dan Heng & Sunday, optional)*

**Dan Heng:** I've made contact with the Xianzhou delegation. Sunday, let's head out.

**Sunday:** If there is any etiquette I need to follow when meeting Xianzhou people, please let me know in advance.

[Recap] Himeko told you about the Phantasmoon Games held whenever the Phantasmoon waxed full: Participants compete for the chance to have an audience with Aha, the Aeon of Elation. Aha will hear the victor's wish and share THEIR authority for one minute. Himeko asked you to Trailblaze your own path where the Express set sail anew, and decide whether — and how — to throw yourselves into this grand Phantasmoon Games.

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**Himeko:** (Trailblazer), March, you're here.

> **姬子：**开拓者，小三月，你们来了。

**March 7th:** So, what kind of mission do you have for us?

> **三月七：**所以，是要给咱们什么任务吗？

**Himeko:** It's a long story, but what if I told you there was a "game" being played in a corner of the cosmos...

> **姬子：**说来话长，如果我告诉你们，在银河的角落有一场「游戏」……

**Himeko:** And the winner might "become an Aeon"? Would that pique your interest?

> **姬子：**而胜出者有可能「成为星神」，你们会不会感兴趣？

- ▸ Wait, really?

> ▸ 还有这种事？

- ▸ Really? There's no catch?

> ▸ 还有这种好事？

**Himeko:** You two saw that special moon in the sky, didn't you?

> **姬子：**你们也瞧见天上那轮特别的月亮了，对吧？

**Himeko:** That's a piece of "artwork." Here, a ceremony called the "Phantasmoon Games" is held every full moon.

> **姬子：**那是一幅「画作」。在这里，每逢幻月满盈之时，名为「幻月游戏」的神赐仪式就会展开。

**Himeko:** And that's what I was talking about, the game where the winner "becomes an Aeon."

> **姬子：**它就是我说的，让胜出者「成为星神」的游戏。

**March 7th:** Pearl mentioned that name too. Just what kind of ceremony could be so mystical?

> **三月七：**真珠也提到了这个名字，到底什么仪式这么神奇？

**Himeko:** Historically, the origin of the "Phantasmoon Games" is tied to a group of Mourning Actors.

> **姬子：**在历史上，「幻月游戏」的起源和一群悲悼伶人有关。

**Himeko:** Legend has it that they offered heartfelt stories to the moon, hoping for a chance to have an audience with an Aeon.

> **姬子：**据说，他们对着月亮献上打动人心的故事，以求得到谒见星神的机会。

- ▸ Is meeting an Aeon really that special?

> ▸ 谒见星神很稀罕吗？

**Himeko:** Not really. As far as I know, many factions have their own ways of gaining an audience with THEM.

> **姬子：**并不。据我所知，许多派系都有「觐见」的手段。

**Himeko:** Like the IPC's tourist package "One-Day Glory Tour of the Amber Lord." Pay 1.8 billion credits, and you can get a close-up view of Qlipoth from an orbital ship at Pier Point.

> **姬子：**比如公司的旅游项目「琥珀王荣光一日游」。支付十八亿信用点，就可以在庇尔波因特的轨道飞船上近距离瞻仰克里珀。

**Himeko:** But the Phantasmoon Games are different. They're one of the few well-documented ways where you can actually see an Aeon and receive THEIR response.

> **姬子：**但「幻月游戏」不同，它是少数有明确记载，能见到星神并得到回应的方式。

- ▸ Could this Aeon be...

> ▸ 这位星神难道是…

**Himeko:** Aha the Elation. Legend has it that THEY personally started the Phantasmoon Games, though THEIR motives remain a subject of much speculation.

> **姬子：**「欢愉」阿哈。传说「幻月游戏」由祂亲手开启，而祂的目的众说纷纭。

**Himeko:** Aha is also one of the Aeons "closest" to humans. THEY have left behind countless pranks, with this miracle of Ahatopia being one of them.

> **姬子：**在星神中，阿哈也是最「亲人」的那一类。祂留下的恶作剧数不胜数，哈托彼亚的神迹也是其中之一。

- ▸ What's the deal with "becoming an Aeon"?

> ▸ 「成为星神」是怎么回事？

**Himeko:** According to historical records, when a Supplicant rises above the rest, the Elation will manifest, listen to the victor's wishes, and share THEIR authority... for one minute.

> **姬子：**史料记载，从谒者中脱颖而出，「欢愉」便会垂迹，聆听胜者所求，并分享其权柄…一分钟。

**Himeko:** Considering this Aeon once injected all THEIR power into a Noblesse Worm, it's not surprising THEY would do something like this.

> **姬子：**考虑到这位星神曾将全部力量注入一只诺布莱斯虫，会这么做也不意外。

**March 7th:** What does it mean... to become an Aeon for a minute?

> **三月七：**成为一分钟星神…意味着什么？

**Himeko:** Among past winners, some sought ultimate power, some yearned to erase their past regrets, and someone even stored this planet within a scroll painting.

> **姬子：**历代胜者，有人追求极致的力量，有人渴望抹消过去的悔恨，甚至有人将这颗星球一并收入画卷中保存。

**Himeko:** But how their wishes were granted, and what became of them... only rumors remain, and no one knows the truth.

> **姬子：**但他们的愿望如何被满足，结果又如何…只有流言蜚语，无人知晓真相。

- ▸ So, what are we going to do?

> ▸ 所以，我们要做什么？

**Himeko:** The first signs of the war among the Aeons are beginning to show. I believe this is why the IPC has been positioning itself here for years.

> **姬子：**列神之战初见端倪。我想，这也是公司在此处布局多年的原因。

**Himeko:** When I saw the Phantasmoon at its fullest, I became even more certain. This ceremony to commune with the Aeon, whether genuine or not, could be the turning point that determines the destiny of the cosmos.

> **姬子：**看到幻月满盈时，我更确信了这点。觐见星神的仪式，无论真伪，都有可能成为左右银河命运的契机。

**Himeko:** With all eyes on these games, the IPC won't be the only ones with ulterior motives. The road ahead will likely be fraught with hidden dangers.

> **姬子：**瞩目这场游戏，有所企图的人，不会只有公司一派。接下来的路，恐怕暗流涌动。

**Himeko:** But what's important is that you make your own decisions. Such as... whether you'll take part in these grand games, and if so, how.

> **姬子：**但重要的是，你们应当作出自己的决定。比如…是否，以及如何投身这场盛大的游戏。

**Himeko:** I have only one mission for you both: Explore and understand this world in your own way, as the place where the Trailblaze began anew, and my homeland.

> **姬子：**我只有一项任务交给两位：以你们自己的方式，去见识、了解这个世界——「开拓」重新出发的起点，我的故乡。

**Himeko:** You might hear completely different versions of what I just said from others. Trust what you see with your own eyes, and believe in what you choose to believe.

> **姬子：**上面这些话，你们或许会在他人口中听见截然不同的说法。相信你们看见的，选择你们相信的。

- ▸ I trust our Navigator's guidance.

> ▸ 我相信领航员的指引。

**March 7th:** Me too! I won't let (Trailblazer) walk ahead alone.

> **三月七：**我也是！我可不会让开拓者一个人走在前面。

- ▸ Are the Phantasmoon Games really just games?

> ▸ 幻月游戏，真的只是游戏吗？

**March 7th:** That puzzles me too. The winner gets to share an Aeon's authority. This can't be just a simple game, can it?

> **三月七：**对啊。胜者竟然可以分享星神的权柄，这真的只是一个简单的游戏吗？

- ▸ Whatever happens, I'm okay with it.

> ▸ 发生什么，我都无所谓。

**March 7th:** Stop acting all cool! If you don't have any thoughts about the games, I'm gonna act on my own!

> **三月七：**装什么酷哇，你对这场游戏没什么想法的话，我可就行动啦！

**Himeko:** I see you already have your own thoughts on this. In that case, everyone's got a mission to take care of now.

> **姬子：**看来，你们已经有自己的见解了。这样所有人都有任务在身啦。

**Himeko:** Welt and I are going to meet Pearl. We'll see what kind of surprise she has in store for us.

> **姬子：**我和瓦尔特也准备去会会真珠了。看看她打算为我们呈上怎样的惊喜。

*(Talk to Himeko & Welt, optional)*

**Himeko:** Ready to head out? Have fun out there.

> **姬子：**打算出发了？祝你们玩得愉快。

**Welt:** I heard there are giant mechs all over Planarcadia. I'm quite jealous you'll get to see them in person.

> **瓦尔特：**听说二相乐园各地都有巨型机甲，真羡慕你们能一睹为快啊。

**Himeko:** Isn't Miss Pearl, who we're going to meet, an Intellitron herself?

> **姬子：**我们要见的真珠小姐，不也是一位智械吗？

**Welt:** You don't get it. There's a huge difference between a "robot" and a "GIANT robot."

> **瓦尔特：**你不懂，「机器人」和「大机器人」是两码事。

*(Talk to Pom-Pom, optional)*

**Pom-Pom:** Hmm hmm~ ♬

> **帕姆：**嗯哼哼~♬

Perhaps it's the familiar comfort of returning to an old haunt, or the buoyant air suffused with joy... whatever the reason, the conductor seems to be in high spirits.

### Talk to March 7th and prepare to disembark

> *Planarcadia, a world blessed by Aha, Himeko's homeland, and now, the Astral Express's next stop. Ready to embark on this journey of Elation, (Trailblazer)?*

[Recap] A welcoming announcement echoed through the station. March 7th invited you to get off the train and pay attention to the first person you meet at this stop.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Train Station Broadcast:** The Astral Express has arrived. Welcome!

> **车站广播：**欢迎星穹列车，欢迎星穹列车。

**March 7th:** Now this is what I call a grand welcome! (Trailblazer), let's get off the train.

> **三月七：**这回真是，隆重的接待感扑面而来！开拓者，咱们快下车吧。

**March 7th:** Oh right, after a few Trailblazing expeditions, I've totally figured it out...

> **三月七：**对了，几趟「开拓」之旅下来，本姑娘已经完全理解了……

**March 7th:** The first person we meet at every stop is somehow involved in some earth-shattering secret.

> **三月七：**每一站，我们遇见的第一个人多少沾点惊天大秘密。

**March 7th:** So when the door opens, keep your eyes peeled!

> **三月七：**所以，开门的瞬间，一定要多多留意啊！

## 167　Topological Structure of an Imagenated City　一座幻造城市的拓扑学结构

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Duomension City　|　角色：Trailblazer; Lygus; Sparkle; March 7th; Aeon ★ Aha; Meowton; Furbulitzer; Sparxie; Kuchiba; Skott; Detective Croc; Loftin; Oris　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Phantasmoon, the Merciful Queen'' completed

### Go to Duomension City and continue the adventure

> *You've finally set foot on Planarcadia, and with the whole Express Crew no less! What an unprecedentedly promising start. Who will be the first person we come across this time?*

[Recap] "Lygus" greeted you and March 7th, welcoming you both. Sparkle, disguised as "Lygus," then revealed herself and said she was commissioned by Pearl to serve as the Express Crew's tour guide.

- ▸ Keep your eyes peeled...
- ▸ Don't let your guard down...

**???:** In the name of Erudition, traverse the cosmos.

> **？？？：**以「智识」之名，横渡银河——

**???:** We meet again, Deliverer from beyond the sky.

> **？？？：**我们再度重逢，天外的救世主。

- ▸ What the heck!?
- ▸ Just as I expected...

**"Zandar"?:** I am not the Theoros you know, but the Masked Fool, Zandar Two Kuwabara.

> **「赞达尔」？：**我并非您熟知的神礼观众，而是假面愚者，赞达尔•贰•桑原。

**"Zandar"?:** This time, I choose to walk the path of Elation, and I shall defeat Nous with my sense of humor.

> **「赞达尔」？：**这一次，我选择走上「欢愉」，以我的幽默感击破博识尊。

- ▸ That's somehow both hilarious and infuriating.
- ▸ I think I've figured out who you are...

**"Zandar"?:** Hah, after all, I did say before...

> **？？？：**持有面具的人才算得上谒者，而你，不过是这片土地上，成千上万个自称「想要加入幻月游戏」却借机闹事的暴乱分子之一。

**???:** Planarcadia is a society governed by law. Any act that violates the law, even if done in the name of the Phantasmoon Games, will be severely punished by the Department of Aberration Defense.

> **？？？：**二相乐园是法治社会，任何违背法律的行为，就算打着「幻月游戏」的名义行动，「异常防御部」也会严惩不贷。

**Sparxie:** Severely punished? Haha, you're just a lapdog of the IPC, yet you're still so full of pride. That's very funny.

> **火花：**什么严惩不贷？哈哈，不过是一条公司的哈巴狗，居然有这么强的自豪感？真是太好笑了。

**Sparxie:** That's exactly what I'm saying. Planarcadia under the IPC's rule is boring as hell~ Even the Phantasmoon Games have turned into some kiddie play-date for all the goody-two-shoes.

> **火花：**所以我才说嘛，公司治下的「二相乐园」，无聊到爆~连幻月游戏都变成了乖宝宝们才能参加的过家家。

**Sparxie:** Nameless, you're free to go for now. But I'll be keeping my eyes on you.

> **火花：**无名客，你们暂时自由了。不过，我会盯着你们的。

**Sparxie:** Next time we meet, I'll turn you into devoted fans. I'll make you... mine~♥

> **火花：**下次见面时，我一定要把你们变成我的忠实粉丝，变成…我的东西~♥

**???:** Hmph... escaped, huh?

> **？？？：**哼…逃走了吗？

**???:** Excuse me, I'd like to have a word with you two.

> **？？？：**两位，借过一边，我想和你们聊两句。

### Head to the back alley and talk to the security officer

> *Finally, some Planarcadia officials stepped in to handle this mess. The internet is never some lawless wasteland. Someone's gotta give Sparxie a good smackdown.*

**???:** Thank goodness we made it in time. Otherwise, things could've turned out pretty badly.

> **？？？：**幸亏及时赶来，不然后果不堪设想。

**(Trailblazer):** Don't worry, March and I don't get hurt that easily.

> **开拓者：**放心吧，我和三月不会轻易受伤的。

**???:** By saying things could've turned out pretty badly, I meant you and Miss March knocking out all of her fans.

> **？？？：**不堪设想指的是，你和三月小姐把她的粉丝统统撂倒。

*(Attempt to leave the area, optional)*

**Security Woofae:** Woof, hello! We're making sure Sparxie's fans leave in an orderly fashion, so please stay away from the plaza for now.

[Recap] Security Officer Kuchiba greeted you, explaining that specialist Skott called the Department of Aberration Defense. Kuchiba suggested grabbing something to eat nearby while you waited for the rest of the Express Crew.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Kuchiba:** Let me introduce myself again. I'm Kuchiba, an officer from the Department of Aberration Defense.

> **朽叶：**再次自我介绍，我是朽叶，「异常防御部」的治安官。

**March 7th:** We don't need to introduce ourselves, do we? Miss Kuchiba, you probably know who we are.

> **三月七：**咱们就不用自我介绍了吧？朽叶小姐应该认识我俩。

**Kuchiba:** Of course. The newspapers, TV channels... they've all been reporting on you non-stop.

> **朽叶：**当然。报纸也好，电视新闻也罢，一直在报道呢。

- ▸ I thought you were a police dog trainer.

**Kuchiba:** Police dog? Oh, you mean those "woofae." They're legitimate law enforcement officers, and some of them have even more experience than I do.

> **朽叶：**警犬？哦，你是说那些「犬灵」吧。人家可是正儿八经的警方执法人员，有些资历比我还深呢。

**Kuchiba:** However, as a staff member of the Department of Aberration Defense, I have some special privileges that allow me to request their assistance.

> **朽叶：**不过，作为「异常防御部」的工作人员，我有一些小小特权可以借调他们为我办事。

- ▸ Department of Aberration Defense?

**Kuchiba:** Mm-hmm. As you both know, Planarcadia is quite a fascinating world. Creatures made real by artists' brushstrokes, objects that unexpectedly come to life, strange dimensions hidden within paintings, and even the ground beneath our feet that occasionally trembles...

> **朽叶：**嗯，如二位所知，二相乐园是个奇妙的世界。被绘师画出的幻造生物，意外获得了生命的物品，画卷深处的异空间，还有我们脚下偶尔颤动的大地……

**Kuchiba:** This is what the Department of Aberration Defense exists for: everything from managing supernatural anomalies to registering imagenae and ensuring their continued existence.

> **朽叶：**这些都是「异常防御部」存在的意义：从管理超自然异常，到注册幻造种、确保他们的存续。

**Kuchiba:** And of course, there's the Phantasmoon Games that Sparxie just mentioned.

> **朽叶：**当然，还有火花刚刚提到的「幻月游戏」。

- ▸ So, who sent you to rescue us?

**March 7th:** If I'm not mistaken, was it Mr. Skott who sent you here?

> **三月七：**我猜的没错的话，应该是斯科特先生请你来的吧？

**Kuchiba:** So that's the name of that high and mighty IPC Specialist. This... Mr. Skott believed he wasn't dealing with ordinary criminals, but with Masked Fools, which required decisive action. So a phone call was immediately made to the Department of Aberration Defense.

> **朽叶：**原来那位盛气凌人的公司专员叫这个名字。这位…斯科特先生觉得对手不是普通的罪犯，是假面愚者，必须重拳出击，所以一个电话拍到了「异常防御部」。

**March 7th:** Well, it's not surprising... but what do we do now? Our guide ran off, and we got into a fight with Sparxie's fans... I guess our shopping plans for today are down the drain?

> **三月七：**倒也不出所料…那接下来我们怎么办？导游跑了，和火花的粉丝打了一架…今天的逛街计划是不是泡汤了？

**March 7th:** Should we give Himeko a call and fill her in on what's going on?

> **三月七：**咱们是不是该打个电话给姬子姐，跟她通报一下情况？

- ▸ Isn't she in a meeting right now?
- ▸ Wouldn't that disturb her?

**March 7th:** I guess you're right.

> **三月七：**说的是呐。

**Kuchiba:** No need to rush, you two. Oh! I bet you haven't tried the local delicacies yet, have you?

> **朽叶：**二位倒也不用着急。对了，我猜你们应该还没尝过这儿的点心吧？

**Kuchiba:** How about this? I'll take you both to grab a bite nearby. That way you can kill some time while waiting for your friend.

> **朽叶：**这样吧，我带两位去附近先吃点东西，你们也好慢慢消磨时间，等待你们的朋友。

**March 7th:** Yay! My stomach's been growling like crazy!

> **三月七：**好耶，我肚子早打雷了！

Switching to Himeko's POV...

While (Trailblazer)'s group explores Duomension City, two of the Express Crew are being received by Pearl...

## 168　Artwork in the Age of Mechanical Reproduction　机械复制时代的艺术作品

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Duomension City　|　角色：Pearl; Jade; Mia; Himeko; Welt; Dan Heng; March 7th; Trailblazer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Topological Structure of an Imagenated City'' completed

### Go to Duomension City and continue the adventure

> *Forming the Anti-Destruction Alliance will shape the future of the cosmos and redefine the Astral Express's ties with other factions. Better pay Pearl a visit to figure out what the IPC is up to, and show them where the Crew stands.*

[Recap] Pearl contacted Jade that there seemed to be some kind of rift within the Ten Stonehearts, but Pearl chose to fulfill her duty of overseeing the Phantasmoon Games' progress and remained neutral.

Not long ago, somewhere in Duomension City...

**Pearl:** Color reproduction: 96.2%. Brushstroke reproduction: 93.6%. Conclusion: On the physical level, my reproduction has reached the appraisal threshold.

> **真珠：**色彩还原：96.2%。笔触还原：93.6%。结论：在物理层面，我的临摹已达到鉴定临界值。

**Pearl:** This forgery can now be considered the authentic piece.

> **真珠：**这幅赝作，足以被判定为真迹。

**Jade:** Yet you still seem dissatisfied.

> **翡翠：**可你似乎仍不满意。

**Jade:** Tell me, what troubles you? Is it that less than 4% margin of error? Or something beyond the numbers?

> **翡翠：**告诉我，你在为什么烦恼？那不到4%的误差，还是数字外的什么？

**Pearl:** I'm grappling with a quality that defies measurement: artistry. It is the prerequisite for becoming the steward of this arcadia.

> **真珠：**我在处理一项难以量化的属性：艺术性。它是成为这片乐园世界管理者的前提。

**Jade:** But you already are its steward. "Diamond" will remember that you only took six months to claim that title.

> **翡翠：**可你已经是了。「钻石」会记得，你只用六个月就坐上了这个位置。

**Pearl:** It was five months and fifteen days. Empty titles mean nothing. In Planarcadia, political influence carries limited weight.

> **真珠：**是五个月又十五天。虚名没有意义，在「二相乐园」，政治的影响力有限。

**Pearl:** To add a stroke to this artwork, one must first understand art.

> **真珠：**要为这幅画作添笔，理解艺术是必要条件。

**Jade:** I know you. This is the conclusion, not the cause.

> **翡翠：**我了解你。这是结论，不是原因。

**Jade:** The piece before you, "Worldbreaker Waves," is a relic from one of the civilizations in Ahatopia's history, the final work left behind by Graphia, the ancestral painter of ancient Benzaitengoku. Legend says she performed a miracle, conjuring an entire plane of existence and sealing the whole world within her scroll.

> **翡翠：**你面前这幅《覆世沧浪图》，是哈托彼亚这颗星球历史中的文明…古弁才天国画师之祖「绘世」所留的遗作。传言她施展神迹，幻造出了一个完整的位面，将整个世界收入画卷中。

**Jade:** Do you want to know how she did it?

> **翡翠：**你想知道，她是如何办到的？

**Pearl:** I will not deny it. People call her achievements a "miracle," meaning its true nature remains unknown. In other words, it is an unexplored market.

> **真珠：**我不否认。人们称之为「神迹」，代表无人知晓本质——换言之，是一片蓝海。

**Pearl:** Understanding comes first, then investment. That is the natural order.

> **真珠：**先有理解，再有投资。理所应当。

**Jade:** Perhaps this is why "Diamond" chose you. An Intellitron devoted to results and efficiency, cast into a realm sustained by imagination.

> **翡翠：**也许，这就是「钻石」选择你的原因。一名结果和效率至上的智械，被投入一座靠想象运转的国度。

**Jade:** The "Ten Stonehearts" await a metamorphosis. May you find your own "Pearl" in this strange and dazzling sea.

> **翡翠：**「石心十人」期待一场蜕变。祝你在光怪陆离的海洋里，收获自己的「真珠」。

**Pearl:** Thank you. But my mission is to oversee the progress of the Phantasmoon Games. Personal growth is not part of the task.

> **真珠：**谢谢。但我的任务是：把控「幻月游戏」的进程。自我成长不在其列。

**Pearl:** Jade, allow me to point this out: The Ten Stonehearts are ten independent decision-makers. Your attempts to bind people together will only prove counterproductive and bring needless loss.

> **真珠：**翡翠，容我指出：「石心十人」是十位独立的决策者。你试图将人们捆绑，只会适得其反，致使不必要的折损。

**Pearl:** As always, I follow the optimal choice: neutrality. That is also my answer to Obsidian.

> **真珠：**一如往常，我遵循最优决策：保持中立。这也是我对龙晶的回答。

**Jade:** A flawless reply. Your cool logic is always captivating.

> **翡翠：**滴水不漏的答复。你的理性美总是令人着迷。

**Mia:** Lady Pearl, that Express Navigator has come to see you.

> **美亚：**真珠大人，那位列车领航员来拜访您了。

**Jade:** It seems we no longer have the leisure to speak of art.

> **翡翠：**看来，不会再有奢谈艺术的闲暇了。

**Pearl:** This is all I have to say. Tell Diamond I will fulfill my duty of strategic investment.

> **真珠：**言尽于此。请告诉「钻石」，我会履行自己的职责：战略投资。

**Pearl:** Send the guests in.

> **真珠：**请他们进来。

[Recap] Himeko and Welt visited Pearl on behalf of the Express, declining to form an alliance and emphasizing that the Trailblaze must remain independent. Still, they expressed a willingness to serve as a sail, traveling and collaborating alongside them. Himeko suspected that the IPC intended to use the Phantasmoon Games to curry favor with an Aeon, and Pearl provided further information.

**Mia:** Both of you, please, come in.

> **美亚：**两位，请进吧。

**Himeko:** Forgive the sudden visit, and thank you for receiving us.

> **姬子：**贸然拜会，感谢接见。

**Pearl:** It is not sudden. In my deductions, "a visit from the Express Crew" was an optimal choice for achieving effective communication. I am glad: Everything is proceeding smoothly.

> **真珠：**并非贸然。在推演中，「列车组上门拜访」是达成有效沟通的优质选项。我很高兴：一切顺利。

**Himeko:** To skip the formalities would be best. I imagine that, at your "core," you're already well aware of our purposes?

> **姬子：**能免去寒暄再好不过。我们的目的，你想必也了然于「芯」了？

**Pearl:** I know that you have come for the "Anti-Destruction Alliance"...

> **真珠：**我知道，二位是为「反毁灭同盟」而来——

**Pearl:** The probability of refusing the proposal stands at 91.2%. Can you tell me: Why?

> **真珠：**拒绝的概率是：91.2%。可否告诉我：为什么？

**Welt:** The Express Crew bore witness to the resolve of many factions during the battle of Irontomb. We do not oppose the vision of an "alliance," but the Trailblaze must remain independent.

> **瓦尔特：**对抗铁墓一役，列车组见证了众多派系的决心。我们不抵触「同盟」的愿景，但「开拓」必须自立。

**Welt:** From our perspective, the IPC already has the "Marketing Development Department," and every world it drew into the cosmic trade map eventually became a cornerstone for the Preservation.

> **瓦尔特：**在我们眼里，公司旗下已有「市场开拓部」。而那些被它纳入贸易版图的世界，最后都成了「存护」的基石。

**Pearl:** That is a reasonable concern. Allow me to present data that is often overlooked. In the past two Eras, 1263 planets have joined the Pan-Cosmic Trade System, and 63% of them have preserved their native civilizations. This is the result of the Strategic Investment Department's work.

> **真珠：**合理的担忧。我可以提供易被忽视的数据：近两纪，加入泛银河贸易体系的星球共1263颗，其中63%保留了原生文明形态，背后是战略投资部的努力。

**Pearl:** Planarcadia is one such world. On this land, the Preservation and the Elation co-exist in harmony.

> **真珠：**「二相乐园」也是其中之一，「存护」和「欢愉」在这片土地上融洽共存。

**Himeko:** That remains open to debate.

> **姬子：**这点还有待商榷。

**Pearl:** I recognize that throughout this "war against the Destruction," every participant may experience moments of doubt or retreat. But...

> **真珠：**我理解，在此过程中，每一位参与这场「反毁灭之战」的人都可能陷入犹豫、退缩的情绪。但——

**Pearl:** Based on extrapolations, we believe by following the route of the Trailblaze and building upon the framework of the Preservation, we shall gather the manpower of the Harmony, the computational strength of the Erudition, and the military might of the Hunt... The threat of the Destruction will ultimately be eliminated.

> **真珠：**基于演算，我们认为沿着「开拓」的路径，基于「存护」的框架，整合「同谐」的人力、「智识」的算力、「巡猎」的军力——「毁灭」的威胁终将被弭平。

**Pearl:** Hesitation breeds instability, and instability makes us vulnerable, and our enemies will exploit every crack to break us apart.

> **真珠：**犹豫等于动摇，动摇意味着被动——我们的敌人将会利用这些裂痕逐个击破、摧毁我们。

**Himeko:** Your point is valid, but allow me to note one error. That number, 91.2%, is inaccurate.

> **姬子：**你的观点不无道理。但请容我指出一处误差：91.2%，这个数字并不准确。

**Pearl:** Then, is the probability of rejection 100% in your eyes?

> **真珠：**在你看来，拒绝的概率是100%。

**Himeko:** No, it is zero percent. Interestingly, the difference between "zero" and "one" is also only a matter of perspective.

> **姬子：**不，是0%。很有趣，「零」或「一」也只在角度差别。

**Himeko:** We did not come to refuse the notion. On the contrary, the Astral Express seeks to work with you.

> **姬子：**我们前来，不是为了拒绝。相反，星穹列车想要和你合作。

**Pearl:** This outcome... exceeds my extrapolations. Tell me, was it a change in emotion that led you both to shift your decision?

> **真珠：**这一点…超出我的推演。请问：是情绪变动，令二位改变了想法？

**Himeko:** No. Actually, your earlier perspective inspired me.

> **姬子：**不，是你刚才的观点启发了我。

**Himeko:** The IPC deals with powers that command billions of lives in this cosmic game of cards. The Express had always been insignificant compared to them.

> **姬子：**在银河这张牌桌上，公司结交的是以百亿人为计的势力。列车与他们相比，原本不值一提。

**Himeko:** We've simply been earnestly trailblazing and exploring all this time, trying to help worlds in crisis.

> **姬子：**一直以来，我们只是真诚地开拓、探索，设法帮助那些陷入危机的世界。

**Himeko:** Now, it is this "earnestness" that has drawn many to journey with the Express, while the Nameless' simplicity assures our friends that they will not be consumed and lose their sense of self.

> **姬子：**而如今，正是这份「真诚」有幸让许多人愿意和列车同行，而无名客的朴素让我们的朋友感到安心，不必担忧自己被吞没。

**Welt:** Indeed. If the Trailblaze has any outstanding strength, it is the track of "trust" that we walk.

> **瓦尔特：**没错，要说「开拓」有什么过人之处，只有脚下这条「信任」的轨道。

**Himeko:** That is why we came to you, Miss Pearl. Please set aside your role as one of the Ten Stonehearts and imagine this possibility...

> **姬子：**所以，我们才会来找你，真珠小姐。请你放下石心十人的身份，设想这样一种可能——

**Himeko:** To fight against the Destruction, there is no need for an alliance. What we need is to stand by each other.

> **姬子：**对抗「毁灭」无需同盟，只需同行。

**Himeko:** The IPC may not stand as an ally of the Express, nor might the Strategic Investment Department... But "you" can.

> **姬子：**公司未必是列车的盟友，战略投资部也一样——但「你」可以是。

**Himeko:** Work with the Harmony, calculate alongside the Erudition, help aim the arrows of the Hunt, and build the steadfast shield of the Preservation.

> **姬子：**与「同谐」合力，和「智识」一同数算，校准「巡猎」的锋镝，筑起「存护」的坚盾。

**Pearl:** Then, what part does the Trailblaze wish to play in all this?

> **真珠：**那么，「开拓」想在其中扮演什么角色？

**Himeko:** We shall become the sail.

> **姬子：**风帆。

**Pearl:** The ship's sail? That sounds no different from my former proposal.

> **真珠：**舰船的旗帜？似乎和我先前的提案，并无不同。

**Welt:** It is different. A sail that cuts through the storm does not care for its own welfare, nor does it try to command the ship's course.

> **瓦尔特：**当然有。穿越风暴的帆，不会在意自身的完整，也绝不试图掌控船的走向。

**Welt:** But it will help all who wish to fight against the Destruction to traverse safely through the storm.

> **瓦尔特：**但它帮助所有有志对抗「毁灭」的人，穿过这场风暴。

**Pearl:** Interesting. Logic reminds me that those words you spoke are little more than rhetoric. However...

> **真珠：**有趣。逻辑在提醒我：方才的表述只是文字游戏。但……

**Pearl:** I will follow the optimal decision: neutrality.

> **真珠：**我遵循最优决策：保持中立。

**Himeko:** You mentioned that the IPC has never changed Planarcadia, and it still follows the Path of Elation.

> **姬子：**你刚才提到，公司从未改变二相乐园，它依然行走在「欢愉」的命途上。

**Himeko:** But in my eyes, Planarcadia has not been altered because it still has value, just like the Astral Express.

> **姬子：**但在我看来，与星穹列车一样，只是因为「二相乐园」尚有价值，才没有被改变。

**Himeko:** The IPC intends to use the Phantasmoon Games to reach out to an Aeon and draw THEM in. Am I right?

> **姬子：**公司想利用「幻月游戏」，接触并拉拢一位星神——我说的对吗？

**Pearl:** That involves classified information. I have no comments.

> **真珠：**事关机密，我不便回应。

**Pearl:** However, since Miss Himeko is open to cooperation, I can share more within reasonable limits.

> **真珠：**但，既然姬子女士有意合作，在合理范围内，我可以提供更多信息。

**Pearl:** This way, guests.

> **真珠：**两位，这边请。

[Recap] Himeko and Welt followed Pearl to the Phantasmoon Courtyard, the ceremonial hub of the Phantasmoon Games. Pearl explained that once the eight Supplicants donned the ancient Masks, the Phantasmoon Games would begin. The Graphia family, of which Himeko is a part, had passed down a "Mask of Elation" through the generations. Pearl proposed that the Phantasmoon Games served as a microcosm of the cosmic situation: to see what role the Express would play within it.

*(Automatically teleports to Phantasmoon Courtyard)*

**Welt:** Where's this...?

> **瓦尔特：**这里是……？

**Himeko:** A place in the World in Canvas, called the "Phantasmoon Courtyard."

> **姬子：**画中世界的某处，被称为「幻月秘庭」的地点。

**Welt:** This ancient tree is breathtaking.

> **瓦尔特：**这株古树，真美。

**Pearl:** This ancient tree has stood for a thousand years, blooming only on the eve of the games.

> **真珠：**这株古木千年不朽，只在游戏开始前盛放。

**Pearl:** According to ancient texts, this is the heart of the "Phantasmoon Games" ceremony.

> **真珠：**依照古籍记载，此地是「幻月游戏」的仪式中枢。

**Pearl:** Eight masks. Eight Supplicants. When people don the ancient masks in the near future, the altar will also reveal the masks' forms. Then, the games shall begin.

> **真珠：**八张面具，八位谒者。不久之后，当人们佩戴上古老的面具，神坛也将显现它们的形貌——然后，游戏便将开启。

**Pearl:** I've been told, Miss Himeko, that Graphia's blood courses through your veins...

> **真珠：**我听闻：姬子小姐，您身上流淌着绘世的血脉……

**Pearl:** The messenger of the Hunt is here as well at this moment. This Ceremony to compete for the Elation is but a reflection of cosmic politics.

> **真珠：**眼下，「巡猎」的使者也一同在场。这场竞逐「欢愉」的仪式，便是银河形势的缩影。

**Pearl:** In that case, why not use the result of the Phantasmoon Games to decide whether the Express will become a "sail" or a "banner"?

> **真珠：**既然如此，不妨就用「幻月游戏」的结果，来定夺列车成为「风帆」还是「旗帜」？

**Himeko:** We'll consider your proposal.

> **姬子：**我们会考虑你的提议。

[Recap] Pearl held three masks and attempted to use them to regulate the Phantasmoon Games. Himeko warned that the Aeon of Elation was fickle and unpredictable, urging Pearl to exercise extreme caution. Meanwhile, news arrived of Masked Fools causing trouble.

**Himeko:** However, to take part in the games, one must have a "Mask of Elation." I assume the IPC has prepared for that.

> **姬子：**不过，参加游戏需要「欢愉假面」，想必公司已经有所准备了？

**Pearl:** As of now, the number of masks I possess is: three. With more time, I could obtain even more.

> **真珠：**截至目前，为我所有的面具共：3张。如果时间再充裕些，还可以掌握更多。

**Pearl:** In all of history, no one has conceived of a unified command of all Supplicants and masks, let alone accomplished that feat.

> **真珠：**在历史上，从未有人想过，或是做到将谒者与面具统一调配。

**Pearl:** But this time, the IPC intends to "regulate" the games with our resources and influence.

> **真珠：**但这次，公司将凭借资源和影响力，尝试「调控」这场游戏。

**Himeko:** It is not unusual for visitors from beyond the sky to join the Phantasmoon Games, but the IPC... actually seeks to control the entire thing?

> **姬子：**天外来客加入「幻月游戏」倒也不稀奇，但公司…竟然想掌控整个游戏？

**Himeko:** The Aeon of Elation is famed for unpredictability. Miss Pearl, you must take care. This is a word of caution.

> **姬子：**「欢愉」星神向来以变化无常著称，真珠小姐，请务必小心。这是善意的提醒。

**Pearl:** It is precisely because Aha is so mercurial that such an "elated" attempt has a chance to succeed.

> **真珠：**正因阿哈变化无常，如此「欢愉」的尝试才有成功的可能。

**Mia:** Pardon the interruption, Madam Pearl.

> **美亚：**打扰您会谈了，真珠女士。

**Mia:** The guide you dispatched has sent word. The Masked Fools have stirred up trouble, attacking him and the Express guests. They were separated in the chaos.

> **美亚：**您派出的导游发来消息，说是假面愚者闹事，袭击了他和列车的客人，几个人走散了。

**Pearl:** That is not surprising. Those Masked Fools have long kept the IPC in their sights.

> **真珠：**不意外。那些愚者一直对公司虎视眈眈。

**Pearl:** It seems the Phantasmoon Games have begun, regardless of the Express's choice.

> **真珠：**看来，无论列车如何选择，「幻月游戏」已经开始了。

**Himeko:** I'll get in touch with those two kids.

> **姬子：**我来联系那两个孩子吧。

The three of them returned to the reception room.

[Recap] Pearl asked Himeko to stay behind to discuss the art history of ancient Benzaitengoku, and gifted Welt a super-articulated mechatron model. Welt thanked Pearl for the gift, then headed off to meet up with you and March 7th.

**The Poetic Eye:** The first line and the last line are both "gaze upon the roaring Dovebrook rapids." Repetition makes the act of gazing change its meaning entirely after the person witnesses such grandeur. What's left unsaid speaks volumes!

- ▸ I long to hold you close.

**The Poetic Eye:** A true poet's talent doesn't lie in fancy words. The beauty lies in turning the simple into something heartfelt, which is exactly what this poem conveys.

**The Poetic Eye:** After facing grandeur, the poet does not indulge in the grand narrative, yet turns inward, craving the warmth of an embrace. Perfect!

[Recap] General Yao Guang of the Xianzhou Yuque visited Pearl to discuss an alliance. She brought intel that Blade had arrived in Planarcadia, hired by a local gang under the moniker "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin."

**Himeko:** The hour grows late, Miss Pearl. Matters regarding the Express demand our attention...

> **姬子：**时候不早了，真珠小姐，列车这边另有事需要处理……

**Pearl:** Be it the alliance or the Phantasmoon Games, if you seek deeper cooperation, my doors remain open.

> **真珠：**无论是同盟，还是「幻月游戏」，如果您有更进一步合作的意愿，我随时敞开大门。

**Himeko:** Then, I shall take my leave.

> **姬子：**那么，告辞了。

**Pearl:** How intricate human social interaction models are. My extrapolation still cannot account for the full strategy tree of the Astral Express.

> **真珠：**人类的社交模型，真是复杂。我的推演依旧未能覆盖星穹列车全部的策略树。

**Mia:** Master, General Yao Guang of the Xianzhou Yuque is here to see you. Shall we have her wait?

> **美亚：**大人，玉阙仙舟的爻光将军前来拜访。需要让她等候吗？

**Pearl:** No. The Xianzhou will also become a key ally. Only through enough contact and emulation can we hope to comprehend their methods and strategies.

> **真珠：**不，仙舟也将成为重要盟友。必须有足够的接触与模仿，才能理解他们的行动策略。

**Pearl:** Conclusion: I don't think I'll be able to keep copying the painting for the rest of today. Let General Yao Guang in.

> **真珠：**结论：今天剩下的时间，应该无法临摹作画了。请爻光将军进来吧。

**Yao Guang:** I am the honorary dean of the Omniscience Sapientia Academe, master of the Deca-Light Reflection Barrier, bearer of the Xianzhou's intangible cultural heritage "the sparrow-shaped fist," and the Seer Strategist— General Yao Guang.

> **爻光：**鄙人是遍智格物院荣誉院长、十方光映法界之主、仙舟非物质文化遗产雀形拳传人、戎韬将军——爻光。

**Yao Guang:** Thank you for seeing me despite your busy schedule, Madam Pearl.

> **爻光：**感谢您的拨冗接见，真珠女士。

**Pearl:** For a moment, I nearly mistook your words as a fault in language processing: I believed you had brought the entire delegation inside.

> **真珠：**差一点误判为语言处理进程出错：以为您将整个使团的人带进了房间。

**Yao Guang:** Few Intellitrons in the cosmos have a sense of humor. I have heard that, like that genius, you are one of the many wonders wrought by the Celestial Differentiator of Planet Screwllum. Meeting you in person far outshines your reputation.

> **爻光：**银河中有幽默感的智械可不多。我听说您和那位天才一样，是由螺丝星的星体差分机所造就的诸多奇迹之一。果然闻名不如见面。

**Yao Guang:** Madam Pearl, seeing how you have a "calculating" look, and with your heart pristine and your outer form so dazzling, I could not help but cast a divination for you. The hexagram lies between Fire and Heaven, demonstrating a bountiful harvest with your fortunes lying at the zenith or even beyond.

> **爻光：**我观真珠女士「机」人天相，内涵冰清，外彰玉润，忍不住给您算了一卦。卦象涨落于离、乾之间，是大有之相，爻动于六五。

**Pearl:** I have no training in divination. General, may I ask you to interpret it?

> **真珠：**我未曾学习占卦，还请将军为我解读一二。

**Yao Guang:** Ah, then let me speak in terms even an Intellitron can follow. I extrapolated your fortune using the Xianzhou's predictive algorithms. The result is 101111, a sign of great blessing. If you treat others with sincerity, abundance will follow.

> **爻光：**啊，换成智械也能听懂的语言，我以仙舟的预测算法推演您的运势，得卦为101111，是个大吉大利的征兆——若以诚信待人，将大有收获。

**Pearl:** I have long heard of the Arbiter Generals' legends: the strategy of General Jing Yuan, the courage of General Feixiao, the ingenuity of General Huaiyan...

> **真珠：**帝弓天将的传奇，我闻名已久：景元将军的智谋、飞霄将军的骁勇、怀炎将军的工巧……

**Pearl:** Often, I have wondered: what kind of figures are the Xianzhou generals who remain away from the public eye?

> **真珠：**我偶尔猜想：那些不常现身人前的仙舟将军会是怎样的人物？

**Pearl:** Meeting General Yao Guang today, I cannot help but think that: with such beauty, you should step into the light more often.

> **真珠：**今日与爻光将军相逢，不免觉得：以您的美丽，应该时常抛头露面。

**Yao Guang:** Looks like you've studied the words of the Xianzhou well. You've even learned the art of "provocation."

> **爻光：**看来您学习了不少仙舟的语料——甚至晓得如何「激将」。

**Yao Guang:** You praised the others for their individual and unique talents, yet when it comes to me, the only thing you had to say was "beauty"?

> **爻光：**前面几位被您夸赞各擅胜场，到了我这儿，怎么偏偏只剩下一句「美丽」？

**Pearl:** Do not misunderstand me, general. To me, beauty is the rarest of treasures and the most fragile to preserve.

> **真珠：**将军万勿多心。在我看来：美丽之物最为珍贵，维持其存在也最为不易。

**Pearl:** Besides, intel on you is truly scarce. It is quite difficult for me to find ways to compliment you.

> **真珠：**何况，关于您的情报实在太少，如何恭维是一道难解之题。

**Pearl:** But what I do know is: The Yuque is the think tank of the Xianzhou ships, and its general is the master of all diviners. Speaking purely of deciding the course of affairs, no one is better suited than you to cast the Xianzhou Alliance's vote on whether to join the Anti-Destruction Alliance.

> **真珠：**不过，我知道：「玉阙」是众仙舟之智囊，玉阙的将军是卜者之尊。仅以决断局势而论——「是否加入反毁灭同盟」一事，没人比您更适合投出仙舟联盟的一票。

**Pearl:** So, general, what is your decision in this?

> **真珠：**那么，对于这一议题，您意下如何？

**Yao Guang:** This topic is far too great for me to decide on a whim. But as a gift and to complement the respective gaps in our knowledge, allow me first to offer you a piece of vital intelligence...

> **爻光：**这议题如此宏大，我又怎能轻言决断？不过，我想先送一条重要的情报给你，以作互通有无的见面礼……

**Yao Guang:** And with it, I ask the IPC to show sincerity beyond empty words.

> **爻光：**也借此有请公司，亮明口头空谈以外的诚意。

**Pearl:** I do not follow your logic: with the threat of the Destruction looming, what need is there for further reasons to cooperate?

> **真珠：**我不太明白您的逻辑：「毁灭」的威胁临近，合作似乎无需更多的理由。

**Yao Guang:** Set a mighty foe as a common goal, convince former enemies to turn allies, then devour them in turn... Such feats are hardly rare in the IPC's annals of glory.

> **爻光：**树立一位大敌为目标，说服过去的对手成为盟友，再借机蚕食——公司的丰功伟绩里，这类例子不算少。

**Pearl:** I understand. The Xianzhou questions the IPC's sincerity.

> **真珠：**我理解：仙舟质疑公司的诚意。

**Yao Guang:** It would be better to say we ask the IPC to demonstrate its sincerity. Courtesy demands reciprocity, and the intel I now offer you is both urgent and weighty.

> **爻光：**不如说我们想请公司展示诚意——礼尚往来，我先送上的这条情报非同小可，耽误不得。

**Pearl:** Question: what could be more important at this moment than forming the Anti-Destruction Alliance?

> **真珠：**提问：眼下还有什么事情比结成「反毁灭同盟」更重要？

**Yao Guang:** Nanook won't erase the universe in the next few days, but someone else might destroy Planarcadia within that time.

> **爻光：**纳努克不会在这几天里毁掉整个宇宙，但有个家伙倒有可能在这几天里毁掉这「二相乐园」。

**Yao Guang:** You have heard of Blade, the Stellaron Hunter, right?

> **爻光：**您听说过「星核猎手」的成员，刃，对吧？

**Pearl:** The IPC has long placed a great bounty on his head, though I have never laid eyes on him.

> **真珠：**公司一直重金悬赏，但我未曾见过。

**Yao Guang:** The Yuque recently intercepted a report. Styling himself as "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin," this man accepted a contract from the Synwish Syndicate, a local organized violence group.

> **爻光：**就在最近，玉阙截获了一条情报。这个男人以所谓「仙舟第一杀手」的名义接下了当地帮派「共愿帮」的雇佣。

**Yao Guang:** I do not know his exact aim here, but he is a major criminal of the Xianzhou. He is guilty of the Ten Unpardonable Sins and is a formidable fighter, with few able to match him. As General of the Yuque, I will take it upon myself to bring him in before he can cause disaster here.

> **爻光：**他来这儿要做什么，我不得而知。但此人是仙舟重犯，十恶大逆，本领高强，等闲难有敌手。我作为玉阙将军，会一手担负起将之捉拿归案的义务，以免他在此生出事端。

**Pearl:** This planet, Ahatopia, is part of the Pan-Cosmic Trade System, and Blade is a wanted criminal of the IPC. Since he has set foot here, he falls under its law.

> **真珠：**哈托彼亚这颗星球也是泛银河贸易体系的一员，刃是公司通缉的重犯。既然他踏入这里，就在当地的法治管辖中。

**Pearl:** General Yao Guang, is the sincerity you seek for us to capture this man and deliver him into the hands of the Xianzhou?

> **真珠：**爻光将军，您想要的诚意，是希望由我们捉拿此人，移交仙舟？

**Yao Guang:** I hear the Phantasmoon Games are about to begin, with the entire nation awaiting it with bated breath. I imagine you will also find it hard to extract yourself from the festivities. If this cursed man were to quietly "depart on his own" at this time... would that not be a perfect outcome for all?

> **爻光：**我听闻「幻月游戏」开启在即，举世翘首期盼，您恐怕也难以从中抽身。如果这个灾星能悄无声息地「自行离去」，岂不是皆大欢喜？

**Pearl:** This is my understanding: you wish for the right to act freely within Planarcadia.

> **真珠：**我的理解：您想要在「二相乐园」之上行事自由的权利。

**Yao Guang:** Apart from this cooperation, what better proof of sincerity could there be before we join forces against the Destruction?

> **爻光：**在一同对抗「毁灭」之前，还有什么比这次合作更能展示我们彼此诚意的？

**Pearl:** ...

> **真珠：**……

**Pearl:** Deal. What I do not doubt: the might of an Arbiter General. However, I do doubt: will "the Xianzhou's Number One Assassin" truly "leave" without a commotion?

> **真珠：**成交。我不怀疑：帝弓天将的实力。但，我怀疑：「仙舟第一杀手」真的会悄无声息地「离去」吗？

**Pearl:** By my extrapolations, even without accounting for the guidance of Destiny's Slave, the probability of capturing a single Stellaron Hunter is less than 9.8 percent.

> **真珠：**按照我的推演，即便不考虑「命运的奴隶」对他们的指引，单论逮捕星核猎手个体，其成功率也不足9.8%。

**Yao Guang:** Not even ten percent? As a diviner, that's not how I see probability.

> **爻光：**遇事不决时，你可以打开我交给你的三枚锦囊，其中书写着我占卜得来的应对方略，一定能解开困局，对你的行动大有裨益。

**Yao Guang:** Even if the chance of success is only a coin toss, once I enter the fray myself... I always treat it as a "one-hundred-percent certainty."

Switching to Fugue's POV...

As (Trailblazer's) group deals with Sparxie, Tingyun must head to Dovebrook District for a high-stakes mission...

## 170　Killers Don't Get Vacations　杀手们没有假期

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Dovebrook District　|　角色：Fugue; Yao Guang; Dan Heng; Sunday; Caustide; Boss Polwave　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Fundamentals of Imagenesis'' completed

### Head to Dovebrook District

> *("Impersonating the Xianzhou's Number One Assassin"... Madam Yao, you really stuck me with a "wonderful task.") I wonder what these gang members are plotting, hiring such skilled hands?*

[Recap] Dispatched by Yao Guang, Tingyun went undercover as "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin" to make contact with the Synwish Syndicate. By underworld custom, she must first "prove her worth."

**Tingyun:** Is that even a question? "One hundred percent," of course.

> **停云：**（该办正事了。共愿帮盘踞在哪里呢？）

**Tingyun:** In other words, there will be... no survivors.

> **停云：**（共愿帮这边的接头人，应该很显眼才是。）

**Tingyun:** Anyone who recognizes my face... I will not let them off even if they hide at the ends of the world.

> **停云：**这里应该和爻老板的任务无关，去别处看看吧？

**Tingyun:** I was willing to make an exception when I was hired by your gang's leader, but I see you folks are not interested in accepting my good will.

**Ferocious Ploo in a Suit:** Not that I don't trust you, but you know the rules of the trade.

**Ferocious Ploo in a Suit:** When you hire someone to do a job, you gotta see what they're made of first.

**Tingyun:** People say that the lords of hell are easy to deal with, but their underlings are the real trouble. If a group's underlings don't know their place, even their masters may not be safe.

**Tingyun:** So, since the mighty Synwish Syndicate gang actually had the guts to hire the Xianzhou's Number One Assassin...

**Tingyun:** I guess you are also ready for "destruction."

**Tingyun:** (Years of wandering have given me nothing but exhaustion, and I have finally come to know how harsh the road of life truly is...)

**Tingyun:** ("Impersonating the Xianzhou's Number One Assassin"... Madam Yao, you really stuck me with a "wonderful task.")

[Recap] Yao Guang's divination revealed that the impersonation mission was critically important, and that only Tingyun could carry it out. She warned Tingyun to be cautious, and told her to rely on the three tactical pouches she'd been given if she found herself in a bind.

Not long ago...

**Yao Guang:** Please take great care during this mission, Miss Tingyun. If any misfortune should befall you, I would have no way to answer to your Helm Master Yukong.

**Tingyun:** You worry too much, Madam Yao. When I pledged my resolve to Madam Yukong and departed for the Yuque, I had already steeled myself for the worst.

**Tingyun:** But... may I ask: Does this task of "misfortune" really have to fall upon me?

**Yao Guang:** I cast a divination today. This task lacks the fire element. Without the Whistling Flames Amicassador's knack for diplomacy, this task cannot succeed.

**Yao Guang:** The place you're heading to is crawling with gang members. If they pick a fight, you can try to drop the name "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin" and they might step aside.

**Tingyun:** Do not worry, general. I have journeyed far and wide with the merchant guilds for years and faced no shortage of danger. A handful of gangsters is hardly difficult to deal with.

**Yao Guang:** If you're not afraid of danger, then why have you not set out yet? Shall I read your fortune and give you a little peace of mind?

**Tingyun:** What I fear is that "fortune-telling" of yours... No, what I really fear is you, Madam Yao.

**Yao Guang:** Afraid of me? Am I so notorious on the Xianzhou Luofu?

**Tingyun:** I've heard many tales about you. After the Battle of the Fanghu, you willingly surrendered your command token and became a "general in name only," with neither troops nor soldiers. They also say...

**Tingyun:** That the Seer Strategist, for the sake of a tiny chance at victory, stood by as her mentor went to a noble death, treated the countless lives of dead Verdant Knights as nothing more than numbers consumed in her divinations, and even "doomed" an Arbiter-General

**Yao Guang:** Are you afraid that I, the "cold-blooded diviner," might send you to your "doom" here in Planarcadia? Bold words for a little fox!

**Tingyun:** It's just a bit of my curiosity about you, general. I hoped you could clear up a few of my doubts.

**Yao Guang:** Hahaha, seems like I wasn't wrong about you. Few can face a Xianzhou general with such composure and dignity.

**Yao Guang:** No wonder you could make such a bold statement: "The gods use humans as pawns in their cosmic game, sacrificing countless innocent souls... Have you ever thought about making THEM pay?"

**Yao Guang:** They say you survived a Lord Ravager's deadly clutches thanks to luck, but I believe it was your unbreakable resilience that carried you through.

**Tingyun:** You flatter me, Madam Yao. One must glean some wisdom to be worthy of enduring such a life-and-death ordeal.

**Tingyun:** If we surpass the gods only in cruelty and heartlessness, then that would be our complete failure in my eyes.

**Yao Guang:** ...Excellent, I couldn't agree more! Miss Tingyun, you've passed the interview.

**Tingyun:** Ever since I escaped death, I swore never again to be a pawn for heartless chess players. And Madam Yao, you've passed my test, too.

**Yao Guang:** I know you're not interested in empty talk, so let's get down to business. Miss Tingyun, our Yuque business delegations are treated much better than what you get on the Luofu when they set out on official missions! They get five meals a day...

**Tingyun:** General, I really don't have such a large appetite.

**Yao Guang:** For the duration of the delegation's mission, you'll receive six times the usual allowance, and all expenses will be fully reimbursed.

**Tingyun:** Such a generous offer? To be honest, that makes me even more nervous...

**Tingyun:** Head to Dovebrook District, locate the Synwish Syndicate contact, use the passphrase you gave me to establish contact, and do not reveal my true name.

**Tingyun:** If there's nothing more to it, is this task really worth six times the pay?

**Yao Guang:** You're the kind of talent worthy of such a reward. After all, we diviners are no different from you merchants. Even when we hit a dead end, we still have to bargain and carve out the faintest chance.

**Yao Guang:** Merchants strive for profitable opportunities, while diviners reach for a sliver of luck.

**Tingyun:** Looks like my mission bears significant weight in Madam Yao's reading of fate. In that case, I shall depart.

**Yao Guang:** When in doubt, open the three strategy pouches I entrusted to you. Inside are tactics I divined, certain to break any deadlock and aid your journey.

**Yao Guang:** Go, "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin."

**Happy Magic Wand:** Join now and receive our Golden Rules of Life's Happiness book series along with a Fulwish TV membership. You can watch every program free for a whole year~

> **无奈的欧克壮汉：**按照《灵物保护法》的相关法规规定，幽灵不等同于生前个体。换句话说，你只是个刚满三个月的新生幻造种。

> **无奈的欧克壮汉：**你听不明白吗？过去的你，死了，连犯罪记录也一笔勾销了。老大的意思是，有洗白上岸的机会，就别再来混帮派啦——
