**Dan Heng:** When Sunday and I contacted the Xianzhou delegation, they asked for our help. They claimed someone from their group had lost their way here in a foreign land and was captured by a local gang, and they wanted us to help.

> **丹恒：**我和星期日与仙舟的使团搭上线时，收到他们的请求，说使团中有人异乡迷途，不慎陷落当地帮派之手，希望我们伸出援手。

**Dan Heng:** It was clear there was more to the story, but we came anyway.

> **丹恒：**虽说一听就是另有隐情，但我们还是来了。

**Sunday:** Who would've thought... it turned out to be true.

> **星期日：**谁知道…竟然确有其事。

**Tingyun:** "Lost"? "Captured"? There must be some misunderstanding... Alas, this rumor was likely a "destiny" arranged by Madam Yao.

> **停云：**「迷途陷落」？当中有些误会——唉，两位这则流言，恐怕是爻老板精心安排的「命数」吧。

**Dan Heng:** Did you just say "Madam Yao"? Do you mean the Seer Strategist from the Xianzhou Yuque?

> **丹恒：**你刚才提到「爻老板」，莫非是玉阙仙舟的戎韬将军？

**Sunday:** I happened to hear of her back in Penacony too. They say she is the diviner general, one who lives in seclusion. Did the Alliance send her this time?

> **星期日：**我在匹诺康尼时也偶有耳闻，听说她是位深居简出的卜者将军。这次，联盟派了她前来？

**Sunday:** Lady Tingyun, you stepped into such danger yourself... do you have a vital mission?

> **星期日：**停云女士亲涉险地，莫非是要务在身？

**Tingyun:** You two are no strangers... so I will speak plainly. She's the one who sent me here on this mission.

> **停云：**两位不是外人，我就不瞒二位了，就是她差我来此执行任务。

**Tingyun:** The Synwish Syndicate invited the notorious "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin" with the intention of hiring this person to participate in the Phantasmoon Games. But the delegation's intelligence revealed that the person was none other than the Stellaron Hunter, Blade.

> **停云：**共愿帮请来「仙舟第一杀手」，想雇佣这位凶名卓著的人物参与「幻月游戏」。但按使团所获情报，那人的真实身份是星核猎手，刃。

**Dan Heng:** ...Him?

> **丹恒：**…是他？

**Tingyun:** I assumed the title to come here to uncover why the Stellaron Hunters are involved... and how he will enter the game.

> **停云：**我冒用这一名号前来此地，就是为了探明星核猎手为何，以及如何入局。

**Sunday:** So how's it going so far?

> **星期日：**眼下进展如何？

**Tingyun:** By sheer twists of fate, everything has gone smoothly so far. But as for what comes next... I still don't know anything either.

> **停云：**虽说阴差阳错之下，一切都很顺利，但接下来该如何做，小女子也还蒙在鼓里。

**Tingyun:** Still, Madam Yao entrusted me with three pouches before I departed, each said to hold a stratagem. Well... let us call them stratagems for now. And at this moment, it is time to open the second.

> **停云：**不过，临行前爻老板曾交予我三只锦囊，内藏妙计…嗯，姑且先当作妙计吧。眼下，也到了拆开第二只的时候。

**Sunday:** ...A strategy pouch?

> **星期日：**…锦囊？

Yao Guang's Strategy Pouch: "So you revealed my identity after all? No worries! I've sent you some backup. Use them however you like. Open the third pouch only after you've met the Synwish Syndicate's boss. b（￣▽￣）"

**Tingyun:** ...

> **停云：**……

**Tingyun:** No wonder they also call Madam Yao the "Crosstalk General"...

> **停云：**爻老板，难怪您也有「相声将军」的名头呐……

**Dan Heng:** The what general?

> **丹恒：**怎么说？

**Tingyun:** It says the two of you will help me clear the way, so I can safely meet the Synwish Syndicate's boss. What do you say?

> **停云：**上面说，两位会为我解围，让我安心去见共愿帮的头目——两位意下如何？

**Dan Heng:** We...

> **丹恒：**我们……

**Sunday:** This place is perilous. Of course we will accompany you, Miss Tingyun.

> **星期日：**这里万分凶险，我们当然会与停云小姐随行。

**Sunday:** As for the Stellaron Hunters... I, too, find them intriguing.

> **星期日：**至于星核猎手，我同样对他们很感兴趣。

**Sunday:** Dan Heng, will you help me gather a bit more information?

> **星期日：**丹恒，能再陪我打听些消息吗？

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** Of course... thank you, Sunday

> **丹恒：**当然…谢谢你，星期日。

### Discuss "business" with the leader of the Synwish Syndicate

*(Attempt to leave the area, optional)*

**Caustide:** "Number One Assassins," where are you going? The boss is waiting upstairs!

*(Reach the top floor)*

**Tingyun:** I gotta say, this place is decorated with a touch of style.

> **停云：**看不出来，这儿装修得颇有品味。

**Sunday:** A gang does not always flaunt brute force. More often, they need to flaunt wealth.

> **星期日：**暴力组织不是总得展示暴力的，更多时候，他们需要展示财力。

[Recap] Polwave produced the Mask of Elation, stating his desire to sponsor a Supplicant in the name of the Synwish Syndicate. By drawing attention and Wishpower, the syndicate hoped to improve its chances of survival and continue on.

*(Approach Boss Polwave)*

**Boss Polwave:** Before I even realized, the scenery of Dovebrook became unrecognizable.

> **池波老大：**一不留神，鸽川的风景就变得面目全非了。

**Boss Polwave:** Back then, who would have imagined iron hulks running wild on the streets, let alone us gang members being treated like vermin?

> **池波老大：**以前哪儿会有铁坨子满街跑呢？更别提把我们这些帮派分子当成害虫处理了。

**Tingyun:** You didn't call us here just to reminisce, did you?

> **停云：**您找我们来，显然不是来怀旧的吧？

**Boss Polwave:** Haha, of course not! Please forgive Caustide's rude little test.

> **池波老大：**哈哈，当然不是！三位，请原谅恶水这家伙无礼的试探行为。

**Boss Polwave:** That narrow-minded fellow never understands why young people flock to things that come from other planets, like mechatrons, masked heroes, and the Astral Express...

> **池波老大：**这只井底之蛙，总是搞不明白年轻人为什么喜欢从银河里传来的东西。机铠、蒙面英雄、星穹列车……

**Boss Polwave:** What he does not see is that while cosmic novelties are more enticing, big shots that hail from the cosmos are even more fearsome.

> **池波老大：**他不明白，银河里来的东西更新鲜，银河里来的大人物也更可怕。

**Boss Polwave:** Wouldn't you agree?

> **池波老大：**几位，我说的没错吧？

**Tingyun:** You're a clever one, so there's no point in keeping up this charade. We're not "Xianzhou's Number One Assassins." You knew that already, didn't you?

> **停云：**您是聪明人，再装下去不过是自讨没趣。我们并非「仙舟第一杀手」，这点您应该心知肚明？

**Boss Polwave:** I may not live as long as those Xianzhou folks, but I've got a decent eye for sizing people up. You might not be the number one assassins, but your skills surpass even such assassins.

> **池波老大：**我虽然不像仙舟人那样长寿，但好歹有些看人的眼光。你们不是第一杀手，但本领胜似第一杀手。

**Boss Polwave:** So I don't care who you are. I paid good money for a "sharp blade"...but I don't give a fig about its brand.

> **池波老大：**所以，我不在乎你们是谁。抛出大价钱请来了「快刀」，又何必管它是什么牌子？

**Tingyun:** And where do you intend to use this blade?

> **停云：**那这把快刀，您是想用在哪儿？

**Boss Polwave:** On the Synwish Syndicate.

> **池波老大：**共愿帮。

**Dan Heng:** What?

> **丹恒：**什么？

**Boss Polwave:** Don't get me wrong. I am not looking for bloodshed. Instead, I intend to perform surgery on the Synwish Syndicate. Look here...

> **池波老大：**别误会，我不是想看到血流成河，而是想给共愿帮动一场手术。三位请看——

**Boss Polwave:** Through certain connections, I secured this "Mask of Elation," the entry pass to the Aeonic game. Some covet this treasure, while others fear it a curse.

> **池波老大：**靠着一些关系，我有幸得到了这张可以加入星神游戏的「欢愉假面」，有人对这宝贝趋之若鹜，也有人担心它是祸根。

**Boss Polwave:** Now, you claimed the name of "Xianzhou's Number One Assassins," so surely you came for this. You want the god's reward, don't you?

> **池波老大：**你们冒认「仙舟第一杀手」，应该是为它而来的吧？你们想争夺神明的奖赏，对吗？

**Dan Heng:** You are our employer. Therefore, the festival's prize should rightfully be yours. What do you want from the Aeon, Mr. Polwave?

> **丹恒：**您是雇主，仪式的奖赏本该由您拿下才对。池波先生打算向星神索取什么？

**Boss Polwave:** The Aeon? I harbor no such unrealistic desire.

> **池波老大：**星神？我可没有那样不切实际的奢望。

**Boss Polwave:** I'll leave this prize for you to claim as your reward. I'm happy enough to share in a bit of your glory...

> **池波老大：**这份索取的机会，我把它当成给三位的报酬。我只想沾沾光——

**Boss Polwave:** Whether you win or lose, all I ask is that when the press comes knocking, you say this: You were sponsored by the Synwish Syndicate of Dovebrook. That is enough for me.

> **池波老大：**无论成败，我只希望几位被采访时广而告之，你们是由鸽川的「共愿帮」资助，这样就好。

**Tingyun:** So this is a sponsorship deal? The Synwish Syndicate only wants fame?

> **停云：**原来这是一场赞助生意？共愿帮是想要借此扬名？

**Dan Heng:** Since we are not truly in your employ, are you not afraid we might break our promise and ignore your request?

> **丹恒：**我们既然不是您雇佣的人，您不担心我们出尔反尔？无视您的要求？

**Boss Polwave:** If you truly cared nothing for what I asked, why even raise the question? As I said before, I may not have the long life of the Xianzhou people, but I do know how to judge people.

> **池波老大：**你们真要对我的诉求漠不关心，又何必问呢？我刚才说过，本人虽不像仙舟人那样长寿，但好歹有些看人的眼光。

**Tingyun:** One more question from me, please. Why are you so set on chasing fame?

> **停云：**容小女子再多问一嘴，您执着扬名，是为了什么？

**Boss Polwave:** In Planarcadia, imagenae exist due to Wishpower, and that Wishpower comes from the attention, curiosity, and demands humans place upon these imagenae.

> **池波老大：**在二相乐园，人们投注在幻造种身上的目光，对他们的好奇和诉求，就是令他们得以存在的愿力。

**Boss Polwave:** The Synwish Syndicate was founded to provide for those imagenae who were ignored, had no Wishpower, and were not needed by humanity.

> **池波老大：**共愿帮的存在，最初是为了供养那些不被关注、没有愿力、也无人需要的幻造种。

**Boss Polwave:** Unfortunately, ever since that seashell woman rose to power, our path of blood and violence had run its course.

> **池波老大：**可惜，自打那个贝壳女士上台后，我们打打杀杀的路要到头了。

**Boss Polwave:** That's why I want the Synwish Syndicate to take on a new form, one that will draw more attention than ever before!

> **池波老大：**所以，我想让「共愿帮」换个形式存在，让它拥有比过去多得多的关注！

**Boss Polwave:** The Synwish Strangeling Pension Foundation. What do you think of the name?

> **池波老大：**「共愿妖怪养老基金会」，你们觉得这个名字怎么样？

**Sunday:** ...It's a cozy and heartwarming name.

> **星期日：**…是个温情脉脉的好名字。

**Boss Polwave:** Hahaha! So, will the three of you accept this job?

> **池波老大：**哈哈哈哈，那么，三位是否打算接下这单生意？

### Leave Synwish Syndicate headquarters

> *A mask, a dangerous criminal, a sponsorship deal, a long-held wish. This business for the Synwish Syndicate is weighing heavy indeed. With Phantasmoon Games involved, I must report back to Madam Yao for her careful judgment.*

[Recap] Tingyun, Dan Heng, and Sunday discussed their next move. Tingyun opened the third strategy pouch: "Take it all." Yao Guang arrived, explaining that Jing Yuan was originally meant to undertake this mission, but a divination foretold that one of the Reignbow Arbiter-Generals would fall in Planarcadia, prompting her to step in instead. Yao Guang asked Tingyun for the mask, intending to wear it herself.

A moment later, somewhere in Dovebrook District

**Sunday:** ...Mr. Polwave's goal is surprisingly grounded.

> **星期日：**…这位池波先生的目标，倒是意外地脚踏实地。

**Sunday:** A gang leader won't survive this long by relying on bodyguards. It's about having a sharp instinct for survival.

> **星期日：**能活到这把年纪的帮派首领，靠的不是保镖，而是生存智慧。

**Tingyun:** He wants to launder his gang clean, but that will not be easy. In my view, the underboss may not see eye to eye with his boss.

> **停云：**他想让组织洗白，只怕也没那么容易。依小女子愚见，那位二当家就未必会和他的老大同心同德。

**Tingyun:** My thanks to you both. With your protection, I managed to leave without harm.

> **停云：**多谢二位的保护，我能有惊无险地离开。

**Tingyun:** Yet, we carried off the mask meant for the so-called "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin"... when I think of it that way, this mask feels thorny in my hands.

> **停云：**只是，带走了本该属于「仙舟第一杀手」的面具…这么一想，这副面具都有些扎手了。

**Dan Heng:** That man... He seeks to enter the Phantasmoon Games? What does he plan to do?

> **丹恒：**那个男人…想要加入幻月游戏？他想做什么？

**Tingyun:** Whatever his purpose, this matter is grave. I'm afraid I must take my leave for now and report to General Yao Guang before making any decisions.

> **停云：**无论他为何而来，兹事体大，恐怕小女子得先失陪了，待到回禀爻光将军后再作决断。

**Yao Guang:** No need for that. I've been waiting here for you all for some time.

> **爻光：**不必了，我在此等各位已有一阵子了。

**Tingyun:** Madam Yao, you came in person?

> **停云：**爻老板，你竟然亲自来了？

**Yao Guang:** Well, you see, I must admit I passed false information to the two behind you, sending them on a wild goose chase. I should personally ask for their forgiveness. As for why I am here... Miss Tingyun, you still have not opened the third pouch, have you?

> **爻光：**这不，冒昧给你身后两位递上了假消息，致二位一番奔波，还是得老老实实来请罪啊。至于为何到此…停云姑娘，第三只锦囊，你怕是还没拆开吧？

**Tingyun:** That's true...

> **停云：**这倒是……

Yao Guang's Strategy Pouch: "Whatever they give you, just take it all and bring them to me. ˶╹ᵕ╹˶ಣ"

**Tingyun:** What kind of after-the-fact strategy is this?

> **停云：**这是什么马后炮锦囊？

**Dan Heng:** General, had you spoken plainly, Sunday and I would never have refused you. Why did you lie to us and claim that someone was "lost and captured"?

> **丹恒：**将军，您若是开口讲明，我和星期日断不会拒绝。为何却要骗我们「使团有人迷途失陷」？

**Yao Guang:** I didn't lie.

> **爻光：**我可没有骗人。

**Yao Guang:** If not for the appearance of the "former Vidyadhara high elder" and the "runaway master of Penacony," Miss Tingyun would still be trapped in those brutes' endless probing. Her being "lost and captured" was exactly the future I divined.

> **爻光：**若没有「前任龙尊」和「匹诺康尼在逃主公」两位现身，停云姑娘恐怕仍要困在那群莽夫无休止的试探中。「迷途失陷」，正是我卜测所见的未来。

**Yao Guang:** I also foresaw bloodshed awaiting the Synwish Syndicate. Had it come true, it would have been because Miss Tingyun failed to restrain her strength during the clash. Yet with you two here, the gangsters' lives were spared.

> **爻光：**我还算到共愿帮要遭血光之灾，若此事成真，多半是因为武力纠缠间，停云姑娘没能留住力。而两位一来，共愿帮也保住了性命。

**Tingyun:** You even foresaw the anomaly with my body.

> **停云：**您果然算到了我身体的异状。

**Yao Guang:** Sometimes, even when fate is foreseen, it cannot be changed. Fortunately, things did not come to the worst.

> **爻光：**有时候纵然算到了命数，未必就能改得了。万幸，一切没往最糟的结果发展。

**Yao Guang:** As for Mr. Sunday, I've heard so much about you. With my thousand feathered eyes, I had the privilege of watching the broadcast of your entire god-ascending feat in Penacony from far away. Truly magnificent!

> **爻光：**星期日先生，久仰大名！你在匹诺康尼的登神壮举，本座有幸借着千只翎眼遥遥看了全程直播，堪称壮丽绝伦！

**Sunday:** Let's not speak of that misstep anymore, general.

> **星期日：**行差踏错的一步，将军还是不要再提了。

**Sunday:** Rather, we have brought back the mask as you asked. How do you intend to use it? Surely not just to hold it and block the Stellaron Hunter's entry?

> **星期日：**倒是我们如您所愿带回了面具。您打算如何用它？不只想单单握在手中，以绝星核猎手入局的心思吧。

**Yao Guang:** That was the plan, but from one cause spring a hundred outcomes. Now that the mask is in my hands, there are many possibilities.

> **爻光：**本该如此，但是一因生百果，面具既然到了我手上，能有的花样多着呢。

**Yao Guang:** What do you say about using it to lure Blade here and strike him down together? Or perhaps...

> **爻光：**借它将刃诱来，合力围杀，你觉得如何？又或者——

**Yao Guang:** I could wear the mask myself and personally compete in the Phantasmoon Games. That'd be interesting, right?

> **爻光：**本座亲自戴上这副假面，逐鹿「幻月游戏」，倒也有趣？

**Yao Guang:** Each mask used is gone for good. The Stellaron Hunters will be left with a real headache. Hahaha, that'd be truly killing two birds with one stone.

> **爻光：**这面具戴一副少一副，星核猎手也会为此头疼，哈哈，真是一举两得。

**Tingyun:** Madam Yao, this is deadly business. It's no laughing matter.

> **停云：**爻老板，这可是要命的麻烦事，说笑不得呀。

**Yao Guang:** "Deadly"... Miss Tingyun, that is exactly why I am here.

> **爻光：**「要命」…停云姑娘，本座正是为此而来的呢。

**Yao Guang:** Not to put Blade in his grave, that is. His ties to the Xianzhou have long since frayed. There was no need for me to snatch someone else's assignment and come here only for his sake.

> **爻光：**当然，不是为了刃的命，他与仙舟的因缘多年来早就断得七七八八，倒也不必非得让本座截胡了别人的任命，为他前来此处。

**Tingyun:** ...Snatch someone else's assignment?

**Dan Heng:** So the Xianzhou envoy was supposed to be someone else?

> **星期日：**所以仙舟派来的使节原本另有其人？

**Yao Guang:** To be frank, this journey to Planarcadia was meant for Jing Yuan, the Divine Foresight. The Luofu Cloud Knights recently returned from the great battle against Irontomb, and the honor should have been his.

> **爻光：**不瞒诸位，二相乐园本该由「神策将军」景元出使——罗浮云骑大战铁墓归来，这份荣耀本该归他。

**Yao Guang:** Yet before his departure, I cast several divinations on all parties involved and received this result...

> **爻光：**只是临行前，我为此次出使所涉各方占上几卦，却得到如下结果——

*(Quick Read)*

Yao Guang's Divination

With the Hunt as the primary subject, the divination reads as follows:"The hexagram resolves as Fire. The change occurs at the fourth line.""It comes without warning: Incineration. Death. Abandonment."This is truly ominous... With the Preservation as the primary subject, the divination reads as follows:"The hexagram resolves as Heaven. The change occurs at the fourth line.""One may leap, or remain in the depths, with no fault incurred."Advance and retreat according to circumstances, and you shall remain untroubled. With the Finality as the primary subject, the divination reads as follows:"The hexagram resolves as Earth. The change occurs at the top line.""Dragons clash amongst the wilderness, their blood staining heaven and earth alike."...This is a portent of bloody conflict. Why would Stellaron Hunters be marked by such an omen? Could it be connected to the former high elder? With Planarcadia as the primary subject, the divination reads as follows:"The hexagram fluctuates between Earth and Fire. The change occurs at the top line.""Neither fully luminous nor wholly obscured, it rose to the heavens at first, only to descend back into the earth."What does this even mean? Joy turns to sorrow? With the fate of the next ten days as the primary subject, the divination reads as follows:"The hexagram fluctuates between Heaven and Thunder. The change occurs at the fifth line.""For those who present morals and virtues, no remedy is needed for good fortune to follow."... So there are more big shots to come? Just how many more surprises are waiting for me?

**Tingyun:** Incineration. Death. Abandonment... I may not know divination, but even I can tell that there is no good omen.

> **停云：**焚如，死如，弃如…小女子虽然不懂占卜，但能看得出来，这可不是什么吉兆。

**Yao Guang:** Unfortunately, the two top diviners of the Yuque and the Luofu — in other words, my junior soror Fu Xuan and I — cross-checked each other's extrapolations and came to the same astonishing conclusion...

> **爻光：**很遗憾，玉阙和罗浮两位顶尖卜者，也就是我和我师妹符玄，彼此印证推演后，结论出奇地一致——

**Yao Guang:** An Arbiter General will fall in this arcadia of Elation.

> **爻光：**在这座「欢愉」的乐园中，会有一位帝弓天将陨落。

**Dan Heng:** Even so, you're still taking the mission meant for Jing Yuan?

> **丹恒：**明知如此，你还要代替景元出使？

**Yao Guang:** Yes. I contacted the Marshal and took over the task. I've simply never seen such a bizarre fate and wanted to witness it.

> **爻光：**是，我向元帅进言，接了这份差事。没别的，只是本座从未见过这般离奇的命数，很有兴趣见证一番。

**Sunday:** Even if it means your death?

> **星期日：**哪怕会因此而死？

**Yao Guang:** It's only death.

> **爻光：**不过一死罢了。

**Yao Guang:** If I can die in these Phantasmoon Games to achieve a moment of godhood... Then my death won't have sullied the Xianzhou's honor. Miss Tingyun, hand me the mask.

> **爻光：**若能在这场幻月游戏中为登神片刻而死，也不算辱没仙舟的声名。停云姑娘，把面具给我吧。

[Recap] Yao Guang examined the mask for a moment, pretended to put it on, then whirled around and snapped it onto someone else. Dan Heng nimbly dodged aside, but Sunday was too slow and became a Supplicant.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Yao Guang:** Hehe.

> **爻光：**哼哼。

**Sunday:** ...

**(???):** Well done, you have hit your fitness goal. Another active and fulfilling day!

> **？？？：**您的今日运动目标已达成，又是活力满满的一天！

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Yao Guang verified the mask's authenticity and congratulated Sunday on becoming a Supplicant. Dan Heng questioned her intentions, but Sunday signaled him to stay calm and let him personally handle it.

**Dan Heng:** Umm... sorry, Sunday. My body instinctively sensed danger.

> **丹恒：**这…抱歉，星期日，身体本能地察觉到了危险。

**Yao Guang:** "Once the mask for joining the Phantasmoon Games is worn, it binds tightly to the Supplicant and manifests the shape of the wearer's soul." I read something like this in the Views of the Universe from a Starskiff.

> **爻光：**「加入幻月游戏的假面一旦被佩戴，便会与谒者本人紧密联结在一起——它会显现出佩戴者灵魂的形状。」我在《涯海星槎胜览》中看过类似的记述。

**Yao Guang:** It's confirmed now. This mask is indeed genuine.

> **爻光：**如今验明正身，这幅面具确实是真品。

**Yao Guang:** Congratulations, Mr. Sunday. You're now a Supplicant in the Aeonic game.

> **爻光：**恭喜你啊，星期日先生，你现在已经是这堂堂星神游戏中的谒者了。

**Dan Heng:** What exactly are you trying to do, Seer Strategist? Twice now you've acted without warning. Do you mean to make an enemy out of the Astral Express?

> **丹恒：**戎韬将军，你究竟想做什么？你两次贸然行事，是要与星穹列车交恶吗？

**Sunday:** Dan Heng.

> **星期日：**丹恒。

**Sunday:** Let me take care of this.

> **星期日：**还是我来处理吧。

**Dan Heng:** (Sunday... are you planning to fight?)

> **丹恒：**（星期日，你这是打算动手？）

**Sunday:** (Don't worry. I'm no match for this general.)

> **星期日：**（不要担心。我当然不是这位将军的对手。）

[Recap] Sensing Sunday's desire to wear the mask, Yao Guang offered him this gift. Through her Eye of Īśvara, she saw that as long as Sunday followed his heart, he could bring about change. Yao Guang vowed to do everything she could to help him carve open a sliver of possibility.

**Sunday:** General Yao Guang... How'd you pierce my thoughts? I ran a check with my "tuning," and there were no signs of any outside intrusion.

> **星期日：**爻光将军…你到底是如何窥探到了我的所思所想？我以「调律」检视过了，没有外力侵入的痕迹。

**Yao Guang:** They say a diviner can't read hearts. I call that the excuse of mediocrity. Mr. Sunday, you're fascinated by the Phantasmoon Games. You even wondered "what if I wore this mask," didn't you?

> **爻光：**都说卜者算不准人心，要我说，不过是些庸手的托辞——星期日先生，你对「幻月游戏」很感兴趣，甚至冒出了「若能戴上这副面具又会如何」的念头，对吧？

**Tingyun:** You can even divine thoughts like that?

> **停云：**这种念头，也是能靠卜卦算出来的？

**Yao Guang:** No. I'm simply good at seeing using my "eyes." Though Mr. Sunday never asked to examine the mask, he's glanced at it at least three times since we started the conversation.

> **爻光：**不能，我只是善用「眼睛」看。虽然星期日先生从未向你索要端详面具，但从刚才到现在，他的双眼至少三次看向这件奇物。

**Yao Guang:** Given the character and manners of someone as noble as him, I can't think of any motive for this action but "genuine interest."

> **爻光：**以他这等人物的心性涵养，我实在想不出「感兴趣」之外的第二种解释。

**Dan Heng:** ...Sunday?

> **丹恒：**…星期日？

**Sunday:** Yes. I did think of it. For a moment, I even considered putting it on.

> **星期日：**嗯，我确实有这样的想法，刚才的片刻，我甚至冒出了佩戴它的念头。

**Yao Guang:** So I merely gave you what you desired. This is no trap. It's just an unexpected "gift"...

> **爻光：**所以，我只是将你想要的给了你。这可不是什么突然陷害，不过是给了你一份意想不到的「礼物」罢了——

**Yao Guang:** During the Phantasmoon Games, I'll give every ounce of aid I can to help you seize your chance to have an audience with the Aeon.

> **爻光：**幻月游戏进行期间，本座会提供一切力所能及的援手，助你夺得谒见星神的机会。

**Sunday:** General Yao Guang... As far as I know, throwing out heavy profits first is usually a lure to make others lose.

> **星期日：**爻光将军…就我所知，先一步抛出重利，往往是为了诱人亏输。

**Sunday:** We've only just met. I don't understand why you'd go this far?

> **星期日：**我们不过是初识，我想不明白，是什么让你愿意做到这一步？

**Yao Guang:** Why belittle yourself? Every move I've made is to carve a sliver of "possibility" from "the impossible."

> **爻光：**何必妄自菲薄？本座机关算尽，正是为了自「不可能」中凿开一线「可能」。

**Yao Guang:** With my Eye of Īśvara, I see you're the only one in this land who doesn't need to do a thing for me. Simply acting on your own will can bring change.

> **爻光：**以我「观自在眼」所见，你是这片土地上，唯一不必为我做些什么，只要按自己的心意行事，就能带来变数的人。

**Dan Heng:** "Your own will"...

> **丹恒：**「自己的心意」……

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** Indeed. Dan Heng, I do want to learn more about this world.

> **星期日：**嗯，丹恒，我确实想对这个世界了解得更深入一些。

**Sunday:** Because this world of Elation fascinates me. Is Planarcadia... truly a paradise? An arcadia that its name claims?

> **星期日：**因为这片「欢愉」的世界令我感到好奇，「二相乐园」…真的是乐园吗？

**Sunday:** I once pursued a mistaken vision of paradise with stubborn resolve. Even now, I can't say for sure what a true paradise should look like.

> **星期日：**我曾为了建成乐园，抱着错误的愿景一意孤行，甚至对乐园应当是什么样貌，至今也无法笃定地给出回答。

**Sunday:** That's why I want to see with my own eyes whether this "arcadia" lives up to its name.

> **星期日：**所以，我想亲自验证，这片「乐园」是否真如其所名？

**Tingyun:** So then, as long as Mr. Sunday acts, that "omen of death" Madam Yao foresaw...

> **停云：**这么说来，只要星期日先生行动起来，爻老板，你之前占卦所得的「死兆」……

**Yao Guang:** Of course it would still come to pass! Did you think just a lavish gift could let you stroll out of destiny's shackles alive?

> **爻光：**——当然还是死定啦！难道你指望送出一份厚礼，就能随便逃出生天吗？

**Tingyun:** ...

> **停云：**……

**Tingyun:** Pardon me for butting in... But does the Xianzhou know about this?

> **停云：**容小女子多嘴一句，此事仙舟是否知情？

**Yao Guang:** This is such serious business. I'd never make this decision on my own. I've talked it over with the Six Charioteers of the Yuque. If something happens to me, I'll line up a solid successor.

> **爻光：**事关重大，我怎么会擅作主张？我和玉阙六御商议过了，若我有什么三长两短，我会推荐一位合适人选继任。

**Yao Guang:** She headed to the Yuque on the day I left on this mission, just in case anything went wrong. There, with the other potential successors picked by the Six Charioteers, she'll train at the Omniscience Sapientia Academe in divination arts, war strategies, and civil governance.

> **爻光：**自我出使之日起，为防万一，她将前往玉阙，与玉阙六御所提的其他候选人一同在遍智格物院受训术数、兵法、治平之道。

**Tingyun:** You mean Lady Fu Xuan? She'd take over the Yuque after you?

> **停云：**您是说符玄大人？她会接替您掌握玉阙符节？

**Yao Guang:** Fu Xuan? What Fu Xuan?

> **爻光：**符玄？什么符玄？

**Yao Guang:** If I don't make it back from this trip, maybe Qingque from the Luofu Divination Commission could take the Seer Strategist's position and command the Yuque...

> **爻光：**若是这趟旅程我未能归返，罗浮太卜司的青雀也许有望接过「戎韬将军」的符节，节制「玉阙」……

**Yao Guang:** ...Qingque and Yuque. What a perfectly rhymed match!

> **爻光：**——青雀、玉阙，多衬啊！

**Yao Guang:** As for the details... shall we discuss this somewhere else, you three?

> **爻光：**个中详情…三位，我们换个地方谈谈吧，如何？

Switching to Silver Wolf's POV...

Tingyun takes off with the mask. Shortly after, a poor soul who lost it also finds their way to Dovebrook District...

## 171　Symbolic Exchange and Detection　象征交换与侦探

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Dovebrook District　|　角色：Ashveil; Silver Wolf; Pearl; Blade; "Narrator"; Furbulitzer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Killers Don't Get Vacations'' completed

### Find the Ashen Detective Agency

> *Wait, what's up with the Synwish Syndicate? Who still uses passphrases to make contact nowadays? And they have zero clue about guarding against social engineering attacks? Guess there's no choice but to hire a detective to track down the mask.*

[Recap] Silver Wolf searched for the Ashen Detective Agency and arrived outside a magazine office. Spotting a furbo journalist nearby, she decided to ask for directions.

Some time later, in search of stolen items, an uninvited guest quietly slipped into Dovebrook District.

**Silver Wolf:** Map says the "Ashen Detective Agency" should be right here.

> **银狼：**从地图上看，「不死神探事务所」就在这了。

**Silver Wolf:** ...But why's there just a magazine publisher's office?

> **银狼：**…但怎么只开着一家杂志社？

**Furbobocom Reporter:** Holy furboly! Heard big drama hit Graphia Academy!

> **狸狸通信记者：**狸了个大谱！听说绘世学院那边出了个大新闻！

**Silver Wolf:** A furbo? Taking photos? You really can find anything in the land blessed by Aha. Let's go ask for directions.

### Ask the furbo for directions

*(Attempt to leave the area, optional)*

**Silver Wolf:** Let's get down to business. I can stroll around later.

> **银狼：**狸猫？在拍照？阿哈眷顾的土地上真是什么都有，去问问路吧。

[Recap] Silver Wolf learned that the Ashen Detective Agency was located upstairs. A small monkey named "Narrator" greeted her, claiming to be the detective's assistant.

*(Talk to the Furbobocom Reporter)*

**Silver Wolf:** Hey, little furbo. Know if there's an "Ashen Detective Agency" nearby?

> **银狼：**你好啊，小狸猫，你知道这附近有没有一家「不死神探事务所」？

**Furbobocom Reporter:** ...Beats me. But if I find 10,000 credits on the ground, I might recall the spot?

> **狸狸通信记者：**…不知道哩。但是，如果地上有一万信用点的话，可能会想起来喔？

**Silver Wolf:** Ten thousand for directions? Hmmm... Fine, job first.

> **银狼：**问个路要收一万？算了…办正事要紧。

**Silver Wolf:** Oh wow, looks like 10,000 credits from my account just hopped to end up in yours for no reason. Now, spill?

> **银狼：**啊，天哪，我信用账户里的一万信用点不知怎么竟然跑到了你的账户里。现在，可以告诉我了吗？

**Furbobocom Reporter:** Whoa, generous miss! The place you're looking for is upstairs. That detective's renting my boss's space for his agency.

> **狸狸通信记者：**出手好大方的小姐！你要找的地方就在楼上喔。那位侦探借我们老板的地盘开办事务所。

**Furbobocom Reporter:** But he's zonked out in the day, and his doors are locked at night. No clue how he earns any money. Maybe he's a vampire?

> **狸狸通信记者：**但这家伙白天睡大觉，晚上大门紧锁。谁也不知道他靠什么生活！难道，他是吸血鬼吗？

**Silver Wolf:** So they're closed? Then how do I meet this Ashen Detective?

> **银狼：**这不就是没开门吗？那，我该怎么见到这位不死神探呢？

**Furbobocom Reporter:** ...Beats me. But if I find 50,000 credits on the ground, I might recall that too?

> **狸狸通信记者：**…不知道哩。但是，如果地上有五万信用点的话，可能会想起来喔？

**???:** You stinky furbo, begging for a smack, scamming our guest!

> **？？？：**臭狸猫，你丫真是欠收拾啊，竟敢诈骗咱们的客人！

**Furbobocom Reporter:** Time to go!

> **狸狸通信记者：**溜了溜了！

**"Narrator":** Hi, I'm "Narrator," receptionist at the Ashen Detective Agency, and the assistant of that Mr. Detective.

> **「旁白」：**你好，我是「旁白」，是「不死神探事务所」的接待员，也是那位侦探先生的助手。

**Silver Wolf:** "Narrator"? What sane person gives their pet that name?

> **银狼：**「旁白」？谁家好人会给自己的宠物起这么个名字啊？

**"Narrator":** That would be the dude upstairs. By the way, that mangy furbo didn't fleece you, right?

> **「旁白」：**楼上那位就是了。对了，你没被那只臭狸猫骗了钱吧？

**Silver Wolf:** It did. A full 10,000.

> **银狼：**骗了，足足一万呢。

**"Narrator":** Do you want me to catch that sneaky furbo for you and get your money back?

> **「旁白」：**要不要我替你撵上那只臭狸猫，把钱要回来。

**Silver Wolf:** Nah, save your breath... Got it fixed. Money's back in my account, plus the last 26.5 credits from that guy's account balance.

> **银狼：**就不麻烦你了…好了，钱回来了。连带还有那家伙信用点户头里剩下的二十六块五毛余额。

**"Narrator":** Not bad, Punklordian hacker.

> **「旁白」：**不错嘛，朋克洛德的黑客。

**Silver Wolf:** You seem pretty knowledgeable. So, would you mind showing me the way?

> **银狼：**大叔，你还蛮有见识的。那么，可以麻烦你带个路吗？

### Enter the magazine office through the "side path"

**"Narrator":** This way please, Miss Hacker.

**"Narrator":** Sorry, but you will have to take a "side path." The landlord of this magazine office fled to dodge his debts, and creditors have been storming in one after another. In the end, Mr. Detective sealed the front door entirely.

*(Talk to "Narrator" again, optional)*

**"Narrator":** Sorry, but you will have to take a "side path." The landlord of this magazine office fled to dodge his debts, and creditors have been storming in one after another. In the end, Mr. Detective sealed the front door entirely.

> **「旁白」：**让您见笑，还得走条「小道」。杂志社的房东躲债逃走了，债主轮番上门轰炸，侦探先生干脆把正门给封死了。

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

**Silver Wolf:** So there is even a shooting button? That's interesting.

> **银狼：**还有射击键？有点意思。

**Silver Wolf:** Why are there so many... furbos?

> **银狼：**怎么这么多…狸猫？

**"Narrator":** They're all employees of this magazine company.

> **「旁白」：**都是这间杂志社的员工。

**Silver Wolf:** Why does this scene feel like a punishment round from a game...

> **银狼：**这画面怎么像游戏里的惩罚环节……

**"Narrator":** Office survival's way more brutal than any game.

> **「旁白」：**职场求生，可比游戏残酷多了。

*(Exit Interplanar Jump!)*

### Find the "Ashen Detective"

[Recap] Silver Wolf entered the Ashen Detective Agency. "Narrator" remarked that it is rare to have a client, and scampered off to wake the detective.

**Silver Wolf:** So this is where the detective lives?

> **银狼：**所以，侦探先生就住在这儿？

**"Narrator":** Yes, his office and living quarters are one and the same. It is a little shabby, I admit. At this hour... he is probably fast asleep. Visitors here are rare indeed.

> **「旁白」：**没错，办公居住一体化，确实寒碜了点。这个点，他应该在睡大觉吧，我们这儿也难得来一次客人。

**Silver Wolf:** Visitors being rare... that really does sound a little sad.

> **银狼：**难得来一次客人…听起来真叫人悲伤啊。

**"Narrator":** Please, come with me. I will wake him at once.

> **「旁白」：**您跟我来，我这就把他叫醒。

### Talk to Ashveil about the commission

*(Attempt to leave the area, optional)*

**Silver Wolf:** Let's get down to business. I can stroll around later.

> **银狼：**先办正事吧，晚点再闲逛。

[Recap] The refrigerator door on the floor suddenly swung open. Ashveil rose from within, bleary-eyed. "How many times do I have to say it? Just leave the delivery at the door. Don't disturb my sleep."

*(Approach "Narrator")*

*(Cutscene begins)*

**"Narrator":** Time to get up, Mr. Ashveil!

> **「旁白」：**起床喽，不死途先生！

**Ashveil:** How many times do I have to say it?

> **不死途：**说多少次了。

**Ashveil:** Just leave the delivery at the door.

> **不死途：**外卖放门口就好。

**Ashveil:** Don't disturb my sleep.

> **不死途：**不要吵我睡觉。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Silver Wolf commissioned Ashveil to locate the missing mask, offering to hand her over to the IPC for a bounty as payment. Ashveil accepted the job and later called Pearl, reporting that he had discovered traces of the Stellaron Hunters.

**"Narrator":** Mr. Ashveil, this lady is not here for food delivery.

> **「旁白」：**不死途先生，这位小姐并不是来送外卖的。

**Ashveil:** Oh, then she must be here to kill me?

> **不死途：**哦，那就是来杀我的吧？

**"Narrator":** Sadly, not that either.

> **「旁白」：**很遗憾，也不是。

**Silver Wolf:** What do you mean, "sadly"...?

> **银狼：**到底遗憾在哪里……

**Ashveil:** Not here to deliver food, AND not here to kill me. How strange... Oh! Then you must be a client! You are here to hire me for a case!

> **不死途：**不是送外卖的，也不是来杀我的。真是怪了…噢！难道是，委托人！你是来委托我查案的！

**Silver Wolf:** Your order of guesses is a little heartbreaking. Yes, I am here to commission you. Something has been stolen.

> **银狼：**你的猜测顺序实在是令人有些心疼。没错，我是来委托你查案的，有东西失窃了。

**Ashveil:** Oh? What did you lose?

> **不死途：**哦，你丢了什么东西呢？

**Silver Wolf:** A person, along with an ancient mask that should have been mine.

> **银狼：**丢了人，连带着我本该拿到手的一张古老面具。

**Ashveil:** Heh, interesting. Are you here to join the Phantasmoon Games?

> **不死途：**哼哼，有点意思。你是来加入「幻月游戏」的？

**"Narrator":** At that moment, a cloud of suspicion rises in the detective's mind. Who exactly is this girl?

> **「旁白」：**此刻，侦探的内心升起了一团疑云——她到底是什么来头？

**Ashveil:** Mister N, stop the voiceover when we're talking business.

> **不死途：**老白，谈生意的时候就不要配音了。

**"Narrator":** Huh? Stop? You pay me nine hundred credits an hour plus two bananas just for this, don't you?

> **「旁白」：**哎？要停下来吗？你付我时薪九百信用点外加两根香蕉不就是干这个的吗？

**Ashveil:** Yes, but at least throw me a few clues while you work. Between a detective and their assistant, at least one of them should know a thing or two about deduction, right?

> **不死途：**对，但干活的时候，多少给我提几个词。侦探和助手，两个人中好歹得有一个懂点推理吧？

**"Narrator":** A name flashed through the otherwise empty mind of the detective. This unremarkable-looking girl was in fact one of the top ten most wanted terrorists on the IPC's bounty list, a Stellaron Hunter...

> **「旁白」：**一个名字突然在侦探先生那空空如也的脑袋里升起。这个表面看来不起眼的女孩，却是名列公司通缉榜赏金前十的恐怖分子，星核猎手——

**Ashveil:** ...Kafka!

> **不死途：**——卡芙卡！

**"Narrator":** It's Silver Wolf! She's Silver Wolf! Mr. Ashveil, did you get your head caught in the fridge door or something?

> **「旁白」：**是银狼，她是银狼啊！不死途先生，你的脑袋让冰箱门给挤了吗？

**Silver Wolf:** I feel like I need to wipe my personal data again.

> **银狼：**我感觉需要再销毁一遍自己的信息了。

**Ashveil:** One of the Interastral Peace Corporation's most wanted fugitives with the highest bounties, comes to commission a broke detective... I have seen plenty of stories like this.

> **不死途：**星际和平公司悬赏金额最高的通缉犯，跑来委托一个穷困潦倒的侦探——这类故事我倒是看过不少。

**Ashveil:** So, the next thing would be me getting dragged into a massive conspiracy and forced to run for my life, right?

> **不死途：**下一步，我就该身不由己地被卷入一场大阴谋里，开始逃亡，对吧？

**Silver Wolf:** Drop the paranoia. I came to you because I hacked into the Security Bureau's criminal records system.

> **银狼：**别被害妄想了。我来找你，是因为我挖进治安局的刑事档案记录系统看了看。

**Silver Wolf:** What I found was shocking. In all those bizarre police cases, you were hired again and again.

> **银狼：**不查不知道，一查吓一跳，那些警方奇案怪案的资料中，你被聘用的次数可是多得很呢。

**Silver Wolf:** "The Curious Case of the Harmony Sect Communion," "The Mystery of the Ghost Bullet Wyrm Express," "The Serial Ghost Resurrection Incident," "The Super-Non-Serial Locked-Room Murders at Kooky House"... you helped solve them all.

> **银狼：**「同谐教团圣餐奇案」「幽灵疾锋龙列车案」「幽灵连续复活事件」「奇笑馆超不连续密室杀人案」…都是你帮忙解决的。

**Silver Wolf:** You may not be the most famous, but you're probably the most capable detective around here, right?

> **银狼：**你不是最有名的，但你应该是这里最有能耐的侦探，对吗？

**Ashveil:** Is nothing private anymore... Whatever. Tell me, how did you lose your mask?

> **不死途：**还有没有点隐私啊…算了，说说看，面具是怎么丢的。

**Silver Wolf:** Today was supposed to be our meeting with the Synwish Syndicate...

> **银狼：**今天本该是和共愿帮碰头的日子……

**Ashveil:** Alright, now let's talk about the commission fee.

> **不死途：**好的，那么接下来谈谈委托费的问题吧。

**Silver Wolf:** Excuse me? I haven't even finished my sentence yet...?

> **银狼：**拜托，我一句话都还没说完……？

**Ashveil:** A true great detective can know a case without ever leaving the room.

> **不死途：**真正的名侦探，即便足不出户也能对案情了如指掌。

**"Narrator":** While he sleeps, I gather the intel. We already have the basics about today's fiasco with the Synwish Syndicate, and those three "Xianzhou's Number One Assassins" who scammed the mask.

> **「旁白」：**他睡着的时候，我会负责搜罗情报。关于共愿帮今天的遭遇，还有那三个骗走面具的「仙舟第一杀手」，我们已经大致了解情况了。

**Silver Wolf:** Can you handle it? Money is not a problem. Do you take dirty money?

> **银狼：**能搞定吗？钱不是问题。对了，你们收黑钱吗？

**"Narrator":** Sorry, no dirty money. And please do not withdraw your payment plus the account balance after paying.

> **「旁白」：**不好意思，不收黑钱。也请您务必不要在付款后将报酬连同账户余额一同撤回。

**Silver Wolf:** Oh, then how about this? This is the IPC's turf, right? Turn me in for the bounty.

> **银狼：**哦，那这样吧，这儿不是公司的地盘吗？你把我扭送领赏吧。

**Silver Wolf:** You take the reward, and I bolt afterward. We Stellaron Hunters sometimes use that trick to raise funds.

> **银狼：**你拿完赏金我就跑路。星核猎手偶尔也会用这招赚点经费。

**Ashveil:** ...Or maybe we could discuss the possibility of taking dirty money. Thirty million credits. Pay us after the work's done.

> **不死途：**…要不然我们还是谈谈收黑钱的可能性吧。三千万信用点，事后给就行。

**Silver Wolf:** Deal. I'll try to get that money over the next few days. Here's my contact info. Update me the moment you have any news.

> **银狼：**成交，那我这几天努努力。这是我的联系方式，你们有消息了随时告诉我。

**"Narrator":** Pay us after the work? When did our office have a rule like that?

> **「旁白」：**事后给？咱们事务所什么时候有这个规矩了？

**Ashveil:** That rule got made just now. Oh, my dear Mister N, did you truly want to take dirty money? I have come up with another way to make money. Let me reach out to a certain big shot first.

> **不死途：**就在刚才。老白啊老白，难道你还真想收下一笔黑钱？我想到了另一个来钱的路子。让我先联系一下某位大人物。

**Ashveil:** Madam Pearl, I've obtained solid intel!

> **不死途：**真珠女士，我得到了一个可靠的消息！

**Pearl:** ...Mr. Ashveil? The chance of you initiating a call at this hour is under five percent.

> **真珠：**…不死途先生？你会在这时候主动来电，概率可不到5％。

**Pearl:** Let me guess: Perhaps you are finally ready to consider my offer?

> **真珠：**推测：也许你愿意考虑我的提议了？

**Ashveil:** No, I've found the trail of the Stellaron Hunters! They are here in Planarcadia, and they even plan to crash the Phantasmoon Games!

> **不死途：**不，我发现了星核猎手的踪迹！他们现在就在二相乐园，甚至还准备加入「幻月游戏」搅局！

**Ashveil:** I can send you her coordinates quietly, without anyone noticing...

> **不死途：**我可以把她的坐标给你，神不知鬼不觉……

[Recap] By wiretapping Ashveil's call, Silver Wolf learned that Pearl possessed other masks as well. She contacted Blade to ask about the contents of the script he received. Blade's response: "A completely blank page."

**Silver Wolf:** Hmph. Quietly, without anyone noticing.

> **银狼：**嗯哼，神不知鬼不觉。

**Ashveil:** The Phantasmoon Games... The moment your IPC chose to speedrun it, it was fated to be more than just a game of fun. And you want me to take part? That is asking a bit much of an old man.

> **不死途：**「幻月游戏」…打从贵公司决定速通开始，它就注定不只是一局逗闷子的游戏了。您希望我加入，实在是有些虐待老人了吧。

**"Narrator":** We should've taken that hacker's deal earlier and turned her in for the bounty.

> **「旁白」：**早知道刚才就应该答应那个黑客的要求，把她扭送领赏才对啊。

**Silver Wolf:** An unexpected bonus. So Pearl still has other masks hidden away... That makes things much easier.

> **银狼：**意外之喜。真珠还藏着其他面具啊…那就好办多了。

**Silver Wolf:** Seems that listening in on one's partners after wrapping up business is a habit worth keeping.

> **银狼：**看来谈完生意顺道窃听一下合作伙伴这个好习惯…还得保持。

**Silver Wolf:** Hey Bladie, where are you?

> **银狼：**喂，阿刃，在哪？

**Blade:** Did you find the mask?

> **刃：**面具找到了？

**Silver Wolf:** Not yet. But I've got a new lead. We might've lost one mask, so I just need to find another.

> **银狼：**还没。不过我有了新线索。丢了一张面具，我再找一张不就是了。

**Blade:** Fine. Promise me this: Next time, don't take contracts on the dark web using names like "Xianzhou's Number One Assassin."

> **刃：**好。答应我，下次别再用什么「仙舟第一杀手」的名目在暗网上接活了，好吗？

**Silver Wolf:** Okay, okay. By the way, can you tell me what script Elio gave you this time?

> **银狼：**好好好。对了，你能告诉我吗，这次艾利欧给了你什么剧本？

**Blade:** A completely blank page.

> **刃：**一片空白。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 172　Here's Your Fifteen Minutes of Fame　每个人都能成名十五分钟

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Duomension City　|　角色：Trailblazer; Sparxie; Sparkle; Himeko; Furbulitzer; March 7th; Welt; Paraprince; Dan Heng; Sunday; Fugue; Yao Guang; Joolyon; Furli; Poofy; Snowie　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Symbolic Exchange and Detection'' completed

### Report the situation to the Security Officer

> *March and Mr. Yang were still trapped in the academy under Sparxie's control. The priority was their swift rescue, along with the students taken hostage. But what is Sparxie's true goal, and how can she be stopped?*

[Recap] You reported the incident to the Department of Aberration Defense. However, the security officer told you that during the Phantasmoon Games, they had no authority to interfere with a Supplicant's actions unless Sparxie caused actual harm.

Meanwhile, (Trailblazer), who escaped from school alone, actually called the police...

**Security Officer:** ...So what you're saying is that this Miss Sparxie, who was originally just a virtual avatar, has now crossed into reality and taken an entire school's staff and students hostage?

> **治安官：**…您的意思是，那位火花小姐，原本只是个虚拟形象，现在却来到现实中，劫持了一整个学校的师生？

**Security Officer:** Ah, I get it. So it's just a typical case of the ideogenitive imagenae phenomenon.

> **治安官：**啊，明白了，也就是常见的「缘生幻造种」现象吧。

- ▸ It gained sentience of its will?

> ▸ 成精了？

**Security Officer:** Yeah. You could say that according to the old superstition.

> **治安官：**对，按古老的迷信说法，可以这么理解。

**Security Officer:** Modern science believes that when something receives excessive attention, it would suddenly gain life and becomes an imagenae due to the accumulation of too much Wishpower.

> **治安官：**现代科学认为，受过度关注的某物因为得到了过多的愿力，会突然拥有生命成为幻造种。

**(Trailblazer):** What kind of modern science is that supposed to be?

> **开拓者：**这是哪门子现代科学啊？

**Security Officer:** Planarcadia's modern science, of course.

> **治安官：**二相乐园的现代科学啊。

- ▸ A typical case?

> ▸ 常见？

**Security Officer:** Outworld visitors might find this kind of thing strange... but it's not exactly rare around here.

> **治安官：**各位外星游客可能对此类事件感到陌生…但在本地，这倒也不是什么稀罕事。

**Security Officer:** Certain historical eras had imagenae popping up non-stop like mushrooms after a shower. Things are different now, but if you're not careful, you might still find characters from popular comics suddenly show up in the flesh.

> **治安官：**在过去某些时代，幻造种的速度就像下过雨后角落里的蘑菇一样蹭蹭窜个不停。如今虽然不同了，但稍不留神，还是有些知名动漫里的角色会突然活生生地现世。

- ▸ Ideogenitive?

> ▸ 缘生？

**Security Officer:** Sorry, sorry, used a bit of jargon there. "Ideogenitive" here means something born of chance and circumstance.

> **治安官：**抱歉抱歉，用了复杂的术语。「缘生」的意思，就是因机缘而生。

**Security Officer:** These imagenae arise from incidental events. That's quite different from "progenitive imagenae," which reproduce by having artists draw their offspring.

> **治安官：**这类幻造种的诞生的原因都是些偶发性事件。和那些要靠绘师画出自己后代来繁衍的「谱系幻造种」不太一样。

- ▸ So when are you heading out?

> ▸ 所以你们什么时候出动？

**Security Officer:** Heading out? Where to? You mean going to Graphia Academy to arrest this illegal imagenae?

> **治安官：**出动？去哪儿？您是说去绘世学院逮捕这个非法幻造种吗？

**Security Officer:** *sigh* We can't. I just received notice from the Department of Aberration Defense: The first Supplicant of these Phantasmoon Games has revealed herself, and she's none other than Miss Sparxie, the one you mentioned.

> **治安官：**唉，不行。就在刚才我已得到了「异常防御部」的通知。本次「幻月游戏」的第一位谒者公开了自己的存在——她就是您说的这位火花小姐。

**Security Officer:** Although you're honored guests of the Astral Express, you seem completely unfamiliar with the laws and customs of this world. During the Phantasmoon Games, we do not traditionally interfere with Supplicants' actions. That is their privilege.

> **治安官：**虽然您是星穹列车的贵客，但您完全不了解这个世界的法律和民俗。在「幻月游戏」进行期间，谒者们的行动历来不受干涉——这是他们的特权。

**Security Officer:** In other words, since she's a Supplicant, we security officers can't do anything unless she causes actual harm.

> **治安官：**换言之，作为谒者，在她没有造成任何实质伤害前，我们这些治安官什么也做不了。

- ▸ She took an entire school hostage!

> ▸ 她劫持了一座学校啊！

- ▸ She's super dangerous, okay!?

> ▸ 她超级危险好嘛！

**Security Officer:** You say she took a school hostage, but the intel I have says otherwise.

> **治安官：**你说她劫持了一所学校，但我们得到的消息却完全不是这么回事啊？

**Security Officer:** Oh right, the news broadcast is about to start. Care to watch?

> **治安官：**对了，新闻播报要开始了，您可以看看？

[Recap] According to news reports, the year was 1999 in the Arcadian Calendar. A prophecy once foretold that a great evil would descend from the sky. Now, the first masked individual had appeared, and the Department of Aberration Defense had confirmed that Sparxie indeed became a Supplicant.

**Host on the Left:** Hello everyone, welcome to Fulwish TV's special news segment, Arcadian Morning Report! I'm your host, Poofy!

> **左侧主持人：**大家好，欢迎收看满愿电视台《乐园早知道》特别新闻栏目！我是主持人噗噗！

**Host on the Right:** Hi everyone, I'm your host, Snowie!

> **右侧主持人：**大家好，我是主持人雪雪！

**Poofy:** We've entered Year 1999 of the Arcadian Calendar. What an exciting time! Just one more year until the new millennium!

> **噗噗：**时间跨入乐园历1999年，真是个令人期待的年份！只差一年我们就要迎接千禧年的到来啦！

**Snowie:** Over 500 years ago, a famous prophet made a prediction: "In Year 1999, a terrifying devil will descend from the sky"! Will this prophecy come true? Can Planarcadia safely make it through this crucial year?

> **雪雪：**五百多年前，著名预言家曾发布预言：「一九九九年，恐怖大魔王从天而降」！预言会成真吗？二相乐园到底能否安然无恙地跨越这关键的一年呢？

**Poofy:** You idiot! Are you cursing our viewers? They didn't tune in to hear your fearmongering nonsense! Just read the news, and stick to it!

> **噗噗：**笨蛋，你这是在诅咒观众吗？他们收看这个节目可不是为了来听你说这些危言耸听的事情！新闻，念新闻！

**Snowie:** Y-Yes! Incredibly, after just fifteen years, the Phantasmoon is full again, and the time for the Phantasmoon Games is upon us once more. Surely a blessing from the Master of Elation upon this arcadia!

> **雪雪：**好、好的！不可思议的是，仅仅十五年过去，幻月居然再度满盈，开启「幻月游戏」的时刻又一次降临，这一定是「欢愉」之主福佑乐园！

**Poofy:** Which Supplicants will appear in these Phantasmoon Games, and what mind-blowing miracles will they bring to everyone? With permission from the Department of Aberration Defense, we'll be following and reporting on the Phantasmoon Games from start to finish!

> **噗噗：**本次「幻月游戏」究竟会有哪些谒者现身，他们又会为大家带来怎样震撼人心的奇迹呢？本台已得到「异常防御部」的许可，将全程跟踪本次「幻月游戏」的相关进展！

**Poofy:** Just now, the first masked Supplicant has appeared!

> **噗噗：**就在刚才，第一位佩戴面具的谒者出现了！

**Snowie:** How intriguing! Who are they, and where did they come from? Let's take a look!

> **雪雪：**真是令人好奇啊！这位谒者究竟是谁，又来自何方？让我们一起来看看！

**Snowie:** Wow! Internet-famous streamer Miss Sparxie announced two hours ago that she's donning a mask to become a Supplicant! The Department of Aberration Defense has confirmed the news is authentic!

> **雪雪：**哇喔！风靡网络的主播火花小姐，于两小时前宣告自己佩戴面具，成为谒者！「异常防御部」已确认了本条信息的真实性！

**Poofy:** As an ideogenitive imagenae, Miss Sparxie's birth wasn't approved by the Department of Aberration Defense, but that hardly matters at this point.

> **噗噗：**作为缘生幻造种，火花小姐的诞生并未通过「异常防御部」的批准，但这些都不重要了。

**Poofy:** What sort of miracle does this livestreamer plan to produce during the Phantasmoon Games? Let's switch over to her stream and see!

> **噗噗：**在本次「幻月游戏」期间，这位网络主播想要创造什么样的奇迹呢？下面，让我们切到她的直播间看看！

### Watch the news broadcast

[Recap] At Graphia Academy, Sparxie took March 7th and Welt hostage. Three hours later, she joined the Nameless in presenting a grand livestream event: "SparxiCon."

**Sparxie:** Yoohoo! And we're back from the commercial break! A big thank you to Love Insurance® for being the title sponsor of today's Sparxtacular Arcadian Adventures livestream!

> **火花：**哟吼~广告时间结束！感谢有爱保险®为本次《火花花玩转乐园》的直播企划冠名赞助！

**Sparxie:** One person, a thousand faces! A thousand faces, one person! ♥ Who will Sparxicle become today?

> **火花：**一人千面~千面一人♥今天的火花花又会变成谁呢？

**Sparxie:** Ta-da! Time for the big reveal! Everyone, follow along with Dean Sparxie of Graphia Academy as we check out how our new transfer student is settling in!

> **火花：**锵锵~答案揭晓！屏幕前的大家，快跟上绘世学院校长火花花的脚步，一起来看看新来的转校生适应得怎么样了呢？

**Sparxie:** March, say hi to the camera!

> **火花：**三月同学，向镜头打个招呼吧~

**March 7th:** Sparxie! Don't you dare mess with me! I, March, am not someone you can easily push around!

> **三月七：**火花！你可别惹我！我小三月可不是什么好欺负的善茬！

**March 7th:** I-I'll have you know I'm the disciple of General Jing Yuan of the Luofu and General Huaiyan of the Zhuming! I've undergone rigorous Xianzhou sword training! Others might fear you, but I sure don't!

> **三月七：**我…可是「罗浮」景元将军与「朱明」怀炎将军的徒孙！我受过严格的仙舟剑术训练！别人怕你，我可不怕你！

**Sparxie:** My, March seems quite worked up. Is it because she's a bit nervous about her first live interview?

> **火花：**这位三月同学的情绪有些激昂呢，是第一次接受直播采访太过激动的缘故吗？

**Sparxie:** Alright then, now let me interview my colleague, Mr. Yang!

> **火花：**好的，那么接下来，让我来采访一下我的同事，瓦尔特老师！

**Welt:** Hello everyone, I'm Welt Yang, a history teacher at Graphia Academy. I'm honored to be selected as this year's "Most Popular Male Teacher," and I will continue to strive to nurture even more outstanding talents!

> **瓦尔特：**大家好，我是绘世学院的历史老师瓦尔特•杨。很荣幸能获选本年度的「最受学生欢迎男教师」这一荣誉，接下来我将再接再厉，输送更多优质人才！

**Sparxie:** Alright, that wraps up our interview for now! Let me give you all a sneak peek at what's coming up in our next livestream!

> **火花：**好的，采访暂告一个段落 接下来我要向大家预告下一次直播内容！

**Sparxie:** Thank you all for your wholehearted support! Sparxicle will work hard to repay your kindness! ♥

> **火花：**感谢大家全心全意的支持，火花花也要努力回报大家的心意啦~♥

**Sparxie:** In three hours, I'll be hosting a special livestream performance — SparxiCon — alongside our special guests, the legendary Nameless from the Astral Express who have carved countless legends across the cosmos!

> **火花：**三小时后，我将和本次活动的特邀嘉宾——纵横银河、创下无数传奇的星穹列车的无名客们一起，为大家献上一场直播演出——「火花大会」！

**Sparxie:** Tell your friends, your family... even your enemies! Let's set aside all grudges and meet in the stream to witness the most dazzling sparks light up Planarcadia!

> **火花：**请把消息告诉你的朋友，你的家人，还有你的敌人，让我们一起放下所有恩怨烦恼，准时进入直播间，见证今日的二相乐园绽放最灿烂的火花吧！

[Recap] The Astral Express's fame was about to be turned into Sparxie's spoils during her livestream, while the Department of Aberration Defense and the people of the arcadia remained indifferent. As you struggled to make sense of it all, Sparkle appeared.

**Security Officer:** See? Miss Sparxie was just doing a program with your friend. It's fine, really!

> **治安官：**您看到了吧？火花小姐只是和您的朋友一起在进行一场节目呢。没关系，没关系的！

- ▸ What kind of program is this?

> ▸ 这又是什么节目？

**Security Officer:** Have you seen shows like that before? You know, where celebrities go somewhere to do everyday jobs, like those reality shows where they trade places with regular people.

> **治安官：**不知道你看过类似的节目没？就是明星来到某个地方干些日常工作，和普通人互换人生之类的节目。

**Security Officer:** She picked Graphia Academy as her target? That school had its fair share of trouble fifteen years ago too.

> **治安官：**她居然把目标选在了绘世学院？那座学校十五年前也曾经不太平过啊。

- ▸ Oh, the Astral Express's reputation...

> ▸ 星穹列车的一世英名啊…

- ▸ Oh, the Xianzhou General's honor...

> ▸ 仙舟将军的一世英名啊…

**Livestream Spectator:** Hahaha, so these are the Nameless of the Astral Express?

> **围观直播的民众：**哈哈哈，这就是星穹列车的无名客吗？

**Livestream Spectator:** That's hilarious. I heard they're supposed to be great heroes who saved other worlds, but look at them now, switching to comedy in a heartbeat...

> **围观直播的民众：**好有喜感啊——听说他们是拯救其他世界的大英雄呢，一秒无缝切换喜剧演员啊——

While other spectators in the plaza laugh joyfully, you can't shake off the chill creeping over you.

**Security Officer:** Oh right, please keep this Phantasmoon Games Emergency Response Guide safe. Some sections in it might come in handy.

> **治安官：**对了，请收好这份《幻月游戏突发事件应对指南》，其中有些段落也许能帮上忙。

*(Quick Read)*

During the Phantasmoon Games, numerous unpredictable aberrations and incidents will occur throughout Planarcadia due to factors such as "miracles" offered by Supplicants, oversaturated Wishpower, and concentrated generation of aberrant disasters. All local residents and off-world visitors are advised to remain calm, stay tuned to news reports from the Department of Aberration Defense, steer clear of dangerous zones, and safely enjoy the Phantasmoon Games. Should you encounter any emergencies, please refer to the response guidelines, which can greatly improve your chances of survival. 1. If you find yourself caught in an anomaly triggered by a Supplicant, immediately distance yourself and head toward "home." During this time, do not respond to any voices coming from behind you or from your right. 2. "Home" is an enclosed or semi-enclosed space suitable for human habitation, generally containing objects such as chairs, beds, washbasins, etc. If the "home" you are in does not meet the above definition, immediately read the following: "There are no monkeys dancing on giant teeth, and no black lizards can be seen in the whale's belly." Remain still, and wait for a safety notice from the Department of Aberration Defense. 3. Safety Notices from the Department of Aberration Defense come in several forms: broadcasts, TV News, online streams, and so on. The broadcaster's head should consist of eyes, ears, a mouth, and a nose, not a pineapple or an apple. (Related Report: Masks of Elation were stolen by The Riddlers, nouns and verbs in the cognition of Ternary City citizens have been swapped, causing mass chaos. Perpetrators still at large.) ... 15. If you lose your way and find yourself within a Supplicant's sphere of influence, you can take refuge in a nearby fragment of the World in Canvas. But please note: In the vast majority of cases, canvas fragments won't talk, won't laugh, and have no teeth. 16. The safe zone at the edge of the World in Canvas retains close to reality's three-dimensional coordinate axes. If you notice your body starting to become two-dimensional, immediately turn back the way you came. ... 30. Objects that fall from the sky en masse are usually rain. Rain is a collection of water, not candy, and definitely not vintage flip phones. If you encounter a similar phenomenon, do not answer messages from the candy, and do not eat the phone. 31. A joke is a piece of information designed to induce laughter and does not possess the ability to move. If a joke claims it's heading to its friend the refrigerator's wedding to steal away its electric toothbrush bride, please board the nearest science fiction novel and get out of there. (Related report: A Masked Fool exploited the "Gift of The Laughter" to create infinite improbability phenomena in Rainbow City, and was subsequently taken down in a joint operation by the tavern and the Department of Aberration Defense.) ... 102. If you happen to get trapped inside a television, stand as close to the screen as possible to maximize your on-screen presence. When you hear children's songs being sung in chorus around you, whatever you do, do NOT sing along. 103. When attempting the above, make sure you're inside the TV, not trying to walk into it. (Related Report: Children's program host lures massive audience into a carefree "Fairy Tale Kingdom," now officially classified as a kidnapping case.) ... Friendly reminder: This guide will be updated and revised after each round of Phantasmoon Games. Please ensure you have the latest version published for the current Phantasmoon Games.

[Recap] Dan Heng, Sunday, and Tingyun arrived at the office, and you learned that Sunday had inadvertently become a Supplicant. Yao Guang soon arrived to divine fortune and misfortune for everyone present, though your fate alone could not be read.

After a brief moment, Dan Heng and Sunday arrived.

**Dan Heng:** Miss Himeko, we're here.

> **丹恒：**姬子小姐，我们来了。

**Himeko:** Slower than expected, huh? Looks like you had quite a harvest?

> **姬子：**比预想中慢不少啊——看来有不少收获？

**Dan Heng:** ...That's difficult to say.

> **丹恒：**…难说。

**Tingyun:** Ah, Miss Himeko, and (Trailblazer) is here too! It's been a while. I've missed you all dearly.

> **停云：**见过姬子女士，还有开拓者也在啊。一别多日，小女子甚是挂念几位。

**Himeko:** Miss Tingyun? Are you representing the Xianzhou on this diplomatic mission to Planarcadia?

> **姬子：**停云小姐？这一次是你代表仙舟出使二相乐园吗？

**Tingyun:** This is a matter of great importance. A humble girl like me doesn't have that kind of standing. I'm just tagging along with General Yao Guang of the Yuque to help out with odd jobs. I happened to run into these two on the way.

> **停云：**兹事体大，小女子哪有这样的份量——这趟旅行我只是跟对玉阙仙舟的爻光将军打打杂罢了，在路上碰巧遇见了这二位。

**Himeko:** The Seer Strategist... What happened on this journey?

> **姬子：**「戎韬将军」…这一路上发生什么事了？

**Sunday:** Sorry, there was a bit of a hiccup... I accidentally put on a Mask of Elation. Now, I'm a Supplicant in the Phantasmoon Games.

> **星期日：**抱歉，出了些岔子——我不慎戴上了欢愉假面。眼下，我成了幻月游戏中的一名谒者。

**Himeko:** ...Well, that's quite unexpected.

> **姬子：**…这条消息，确实有些意外了。

**Sunday:** I know the Express has been seeking to join the Phantasmoon Games, and I've been quite curious about that. Sorry... Did I make things more complicated than they needed to be?

> **星期日：**我知道列车在寻求加入幻月游戏的机会，对此一直有些好奇。抱歉，我是不是让事情节外生枝了？

*(If Amphoreus story has been completed)*

**Himeko:** No need to apologize, Sunday. You are a companion of the Trailblaze. No Nameless would ever stop a companion from finding their own path.

*(If Amphoreus story has not been completed)*

**Himeko:** No need to apologize, Sunday. Though you may not be one of the Nameless, you're still a companion on this journey with us. No Nameless would stop a fellow traveler from finding their own path.

> **姬子：**不必道歉，星期日。你是「开拓」的同伴。没有无名客会组织同伴寻找自己的道路。

**Sunday:** ...Many thanks.

> **星期日：**…多谢。

**Himeko:** But wait... did you just say "accidentally"?

> **姬子：**不过，你刚才说…「不慎」？

**Tingyun:** It's a long story... I'm afraid we'll have to wait for General Yao Guang to explain it herself.

> **停云：**说来话长…恐怕要等爻光将军亲自解释了。

**Dan Heng:** She was with us just now, but somehow disappeared without a trace.

> **丹恒：**刚才她一直和我们同行，却不知为何不见了。

**Tingyun:** Madam Yao... Is always like this. She probably disappeared suddenly to deal with something urgent.

> **停云：**爻老板…时常这样，她临时离开，怕是有什么要事吧。

**Himeko:** This Seer Strategist... Is just as elusive as the rumors say. Should we wait for her?

> **姬子：**这位戎韬将军…和传闻中一样神龙见首不见尾。不如我们等等她？

**Yao Guang:** No need. I wouldn't dare impose. The tides of fate shifted. I had to delay my steps to arrive at the proper time. I ask for your understanding.

> **爻光：**不必啦，这怎么好意思。因为命路变迁，我不得不暂时延宕几步，以应时现身，请诸位见谅。

**Yao Guang:** Oh my. Counting me, we make exactly six. Let me do some calculations...

> **爻光：**呀，诸位连我在内正合六爻之数，让我算算……

**Yao Guang:** Your fortune in the coming days looks grim. You'd better watch out.

> **爻光：**近日运势凶危。您可要当心啊。

**Yao Guang:** Weal. Makes sense. You got what you wanted today.

> **爻光：**吉。今日刚好得偿所愿，合理。

**Yao Guang:** The fates spell danger. This matter is most likely connected to that Blade.

> **爻光：**命数凶险。这事儿多半与刃有关了。

**Yao Guang:** The little fox's misfortune has overflowed into good luck. She's blessed with constant good fortune, a true harbinger of prosperity.

> **爻光：**小狐狸否极泰来，吉运常在，是个福将。

**Yao Guang:** That's weird... I can't see yours?

> **爻光：**怪了…看不见？

**Yao Guang:** ...So you're that (Trailblazer) Fu Xuan mentioned?

> **爻光：**…所以，你就是符玄说的开拓者？

[Recap] Yao Guang called you an "īśvara," someone whose mere presence in the game would create variables. She asked Himeko to temporarily assign Sunday to assist her, hoping to prevent Blade from joining the Phantasmoon Games. Then, her form suddenly vanished. Tingyun apologized to the Express Crew on Yao Guang's behalf, saying this happened to her all the time. Himeko assigned Dan Heng and Sunday to accompany Yao Guang and do their best to bring about "variables."

**Himeko:** Your reputation precedes you, Seer Strategist. I'm Himeko, Navigator of the Astral Express. Whatever's on your mind, you can discuss it with me.

> **姬子：**久仰大名，戎韬将军。我是星穹列车的领航员，姬子。有什么事，请和我谈。

**Yao Guang:** Ahh, my sincerest apologies. When a diviner encounters someone whose fate is "unfathomable," we can't help but feel three parts excited, three parts flustered, three parts curious... and one part awestruck.

> **爻光：**啊——实在抱歉，卜者遇上命数「无从卜测」之人，难免就有三分激动、三分失态，三分好奇…还有一分敬畏了。

**Sunday:** What do you mean by... "unfathomable"?

> **星期日：**无从卜测…是什么意思？

**Yao Guang:** My Eye of Īśvara can scan the aura of fortune surrounding each of you, manifesting as Weal or Woe Signs above your foreheads. I'd like to divine your fortunes as a welcome gift.

> **爻光：**我的「观自在眼」能遍览几位身上的气运，于额顶悬作「吉凶签」。我想以此占卜，献给各位作为见面礼。

**Yao Guang:** However, this person, (Trailblazer), really surprised me... I can't see anything from him/her at all.

> **爻光：**但是，这位开拓者却让我颇为意外…在他/她的身上，我什么都瞧不见。

**Yao Guang:** Are you really free to try any choice, to do anything you want? Does an "īśvara" like you actually exist in this world?

> **爻光：**对你而言，什么选择都可去试，什么事都可能去做？世上真的有这样的「自在之人」？

- ▸ I can indeed see the options.

> ▸ 我确实能看到选项。

**Yao Guang:** Everyone has options in life. But some options... they can't help you escape your fate.

> **爻光：**对世人而言，人人皆有可选。只是有的选，却未必能逃得脱命数。

*(Option only available if Memories are the Prelude to Dreams is completed)*

- ▸ I've seen Terminus

> ▸ 我见过末王了。

**Yao Guang:** You mean, you've witnessed the existence of Terminus? But that doesn't mean your life has entered the Finality, does it?

> **爻光：**你是说，你见证过末王的存在？可这不代表你的人生走进「终末」了呀？

- ▸ That's just how we Stellarons are.

> ▸ 我们星核是这样的。

**Yao Guang:** You might be onto something. When it comes to "Aeons," divination often goes haywire and no proper reading can be formed.

> **爻光：**也许你说到点子上了。事涉「星神」，占卜往往出院错乱，不能成卦。

**Dan Heng:** What do you think this means, general?

> **丹恒：**在将军看来，这意味着什么？

**Yao Guang:** As an īśvara, their mere presence in the playfield creates a "variable" in the game. It's both a blessing and a curse.

> **爻光：**身为自在之人，仅凭入局便能成就「变数」。是好事，也是坏事。

**Yao Guang:** As I said before, the weal and woe of us six align with the six lines of divination. If (Trailblazer) represents Woe, then the hexagram falls to Water ☵☵, where dangers lurk above and below, spelling doom.

> **爻光：**刚才我说了，我们六人的吉凶正合六爻之数——若开拓者为凶，则卦象落定于坎☵☵，天险难升，地险难行，凶。

**Yao Guang:** If he/she brings weal, then the hexagram shifts between Wind and Water ☴☵, presenting a great opportunity to strike. Auspicious.

> **爻光：**若她为吉，则卦象涨落于巽坎☴☵，利涉大川，吉。

**Dan Heng:** So, the fate of our journey in Planarcadia rests on (Trailblazer)'s decision?

> **丹恒：**所以，二相乐园之行的吉凶就在开拓者的一念之间？

- ▸ That's way too much responsibility for me to handle.

> ▸ 这责任我可担不起。

**Yao Guang:** It's not as mystical as it sounds. While an īśvara has far greater freedom of choice than ordinary people, they're still caught in the currents of fate. I just want to remind you to choose wisely.

> **爻光：**倒也没有这么玄乎。自在之人虽然拥有远胜凡人的选择权，但依旧身处命数洪流之中。我只想提醒你，慎重选择。

- ▸ What should I do?

> ▸ 我该怎么做？

**Yao Guang:** I am not an īśvara free from fate, so naturally I can't guide those of that disposition.

> **爻光：**我不是自在身，自然也无法指点「自在之人」。

**Himeko:** Back to business. I take it you're not just here to read our fortunes?

> **姬子：**言归正传吧，您这趟来，想必不只是为我们算卦？

**Yao Guang:** I'm here to greet everyone, to apologize to the Express Navigator for my previous "indiscretions," and to borrow someone from you.

> **爻光：**来拜会诸位，则是要为之前的「擅作主张」向列车的领航员致歉，并向您借人。

**Sunday:** (Miss Himeko, now you understand how I ended up wearing this mask?)

> **星期日：**（姬子小姐，你现在明白我是怎么戴上面具了吧？）

**Himeko:** (I get it. She says she wants to borrow you for an operation. I want to hear your thoughts. If you're not comfortable refusing, I can speak up for you...)

> **姬子：**（我懂了。她说想借调你行事。我要听听你的想法，如果你不方便拒绝，可以由我来开口——）

**Yao Guang:** You two, no need for whispers. I can hear you loud and clear!

> **爻光：**两位，还是不要说悄悄话了，这声音大得我都能听见啦！

**Sunday:** How did you... Well, since the general heard us, would you mind telling us your reasons for doing this?

> **星期日：**你是怎么…既然将军听见了，能开诚布公说说您这么做的理由吗？

**Yao Guang:** I'm collecting "variables." During this journey, I'll be acting as unpredictably and irrationally as possible.

> **爻光：**我在收集「变数」，此行中我会尽可能肆意妄为，不按常理行事。

**Yao Guang:** Don't be surprised. Some Arbiter Generals excel in battle with their wit and valor, others master the art of forging weapons, and some guard the netherworld with their lives. I, however, fight for the Alliance by calculating and manipulating fate itself, peeking into the future.

> **爻光：**诸位不必奇怪，帝弓天将或以智勇疆场争胜，或以巧手锻冶兵仗，或以生命坐镇幽冥，而我以卜算为联盟争夺命数，绸缪未来。

**Yao Guang:** I'm here to discuss matters regarding the Anti-Destruction Alliance, and then I'll head to Astropolis for the vote. That's the real business.

> **爻光：**我来，是为磋商「反毁灭同盟」的事宜，之后去往千星城投票，这是正事。

**Yao Guang:** But according to the divination, this arcadian world faces an extraordinary danger. When a bird's nest falls, no egg will remain unbroken.

> **爻光：**但按占卜所示，这座乐园世界有着绝非常情的凶险——一巢倾覆，难有完卵。

**Himeko:** ...Are you saying we'll all perish along with Planarcadia? What's going on here?

> **姬子：**…你是说我们所有人会随二相乐园一起覆灭？这又是怎么回事？

**Yao Guang:** Intel indicates that a serious Xianzhou criminal is attempting to join the Phantasmoon Games. This individual has been infected by the flesh of Shuhu, the Emanator of the Abundance, and is extremely dangerous.

> **爻光：**情报指出，仙舟重犯试图加入幻月游戏——此人身受丰饶令使「倏忽」血肉的侵染，危险至极。

**Yao Guang:** According to my interpretation, his long-suppressed violent nature will eventually become uncontrollable, bringing catastrophic disaster to everyone.

> **爻光：**依照本座解读：他压抑已久的凶性终将难以遏制，会为所有人带来灭顶之灾。

**Dan Heng:** ...There's no way he could do that.

> **丹恒：**…他不可能做到。

**Yao Guang:** That's the divination's answer. Rather than asking "whether he could," we should ask, "How would he do it?"

> **爻光：**占卜显示如此，比起问「他能不能」，倒不如问「他如何做到」？

**Yao Guang:** My divination shows "Dragons clash in the wilderness, their ichor dark and yellow." While the term "dragon clash" could simply mean "fierce battle," could it be foreshadowing a great calamity from your conflict with Blade?

> **爻光：**我占得「龙战于野，其血玄黄」一爻。「龙战」一词固然能解作「鏖战」，但是否预示着你和刃的相争酿成大难？

**Yao Guang:** Perhaps this prophecy has other interpretations... But with my limited intel right now, I have to prioritize Blade, this most volatile and dangerous factor.

> **爻光：**也可能，这命数另有其他解读…不过眼下我所得的情报有限，只好暂且先将刃这个最容易生变的危险因素摆在最重要的位置考虑。

**Himeko:** I've heard that different interpretations of prophecies can lead to completely opposite outcomes. Until things become clearer, it's better to reserve judgment.

> **姬子：**我听人说，对预言的不同理解会导致截然相反的结果。在一切有眉目前，还是存而不论为好。

**Yao Guang:** Let's put that aside for now. In any case, I've brought along a "creation forged by Blade himself." If things get out of hand, it might temporarily restore his sanity, giving us a brief chance at victory.

> **爻光：**存而不论，好。无论如何，此行我携来了一样「刃亲手所锻的造物」，若事情到了难以收拾的地步，也许可以暂时恢复其心智，争取片刻的胜机。

**Dan Heng:** ...What is it?

> **丹恒：**…是哪一样？

**Yao Guang:** I'm sorry, but fate has shifted once again... I must take my leave.

> **爻光：**抱歉，命数再度生变，我…必须离开了。

**Yao Guang:** To put everyone on the Express at ease, I solemnly swear that Mr. Sunday won't be obligated to do anything for the Xianzhou. On the contrary, our delegation will give him every ounce of aid within our power...

> **爻光：**为了让列车诸位放心，我可以指天信誓，星期日先生不必为仙舟尽任何义务，相反，我将为他提供使团力所能及的一切协助——

**Himeko:** What did you say, general?

> **姬子：**将军，你说什么？

**Dan Heng:** ..."Aid within our power?" She... She's gone.

> **丹恒：**…「力所能及的一切协助」？她消失了。

**Tingyun:** Whoa, that was quick... Madam Yao must be really busy.

> **停云：**呀，这么快就…爻老板看来是忙得很呀。

**Himeko:** Has she vanished into thin air like this before?

> **姬子：**此前她也是这样凭空消失的吗？

**Tingyun:** Yes... I don't know the details myself. With Madam Yao it often happens, and no one knows when she'll return. She doesn't do it on purpose. Allow me to apologize on her behalf.

> **停云：**不错，具体内情我也不太清楚。这在爻老板身上是常事，也不知多久才能回来——她不是有心而为，我代她向各位致歉。

**Himeko:** No worries, at least we know what the Xianzhou is up to.

> **姬子：**无妨，至少我们知道了仙舟的来意。

**Himeko:** Right now, two companions from the Express are still trapped somewhere. (Trailblazer) and I must head to the tavern to uncover some clues.

> **姬子：**眼下，列车由两位同伴仍被困在某处，我和开拓者必须动身去「酒馆」查清一些线索。

**Tingyun:** If Miss Himeko needs my help, please don't hesitate to ask.

> **停云：**需要小女子帮忙的话，姬子小姐请尽管开口。

**Dan Heng:** Are March and Mr. Yang in trouble? I'll go with you.

> **丹恒：**是三月和瓦尔特先生出了事？我同你们一起去。

**Himeko:** No, you and Sunday will act together with the Seer Strategist. We're not at the point where we need to call in reinforcements yet.

> **姬子：**不，你和星期日随那位戎韬将军一起行动——眼下还没到非搬救兵不可的时候。

**Himeko:** The situation with Blade is unusual. That Seer Strategist's concerns aren't just baseless worries.

> **姬子：**刃的事情不同寻常，那位戎韬将军的顾虑绝不是空穴来风的担忧。

**Himeko:** I don't want to see the Phantasmoon Games bring catastrophe to my homeland, so this is also my request. To put it in the general's words: Please do your best to introduce new "variables"!

> **姬子：**这也是我的请求，我不想看到这场幻月游戏为故乡带来灭顶之灾，用那位将军的话说：请你们…尽力带来「变数」吧！

### Talk with Himeko and prepare to head to the tavern

> *Dan Heng and Sunday made some new discoveries too. Himeko asked them to meet up at Dovebrook District. Before heading to the tavern, discuss the next steps with your companions.*

[Recap] Dan Heng understood Himeko's intentions and said he would work with Yao Guang to uncover the Stellaron Hunters' scheme. He hoped you can rescue March 7th and Welt as soon as possible, before the situation worsened.

*(Talk to Dan Heng, optional)*

**Dan Heng:** I understand Miss Himeko's intentions. (Trailblazer), I'll leave March and Mr. Yang in your care.

> **丹恒：**我明白姬子小姐的用心。开拓者，三月和瓦尔特先生就拜托你了。

- ▸ About Blade...

> ▸ 关于刃…

**Dan Heng:** ...Blade's appearance means the Stellaron Hunters are also on the move in this world.

> **丹恒：**…刃的出现，意味着星核猎手在这个世界也有所行动。

**Dan Heng:** From everything we've seen, this mission to Planarcadia is of the utmost importance.

> **丹恒：**以此前的种种迹象看来，这场二相乐园之行也至关重要。

**Dan Heng:** I will accompany General Yao Guang to uncover the Stellaron Hunters' schemes. Whatever happens this time, I hope it won't put any of you in danger... I'll do my best to make sure of that.

> **丹恒：**我会跟随爻光将军一同，查清星核猎手的布局。希望这一次无论发生什么，都不会危及你们…我会尽力确保这一点。

- ▸ About March and Welt...

> ▸ 关于三月和瓦尔特…
