> **丹恒：**什么…他们俩被困在了学校里，被迫「成为」了学生和老师？治安官也对这些事情不闻不问？

**Dan Heng:** Though March and Mr. Yang don't seem in mortal danger for now, who knows what Sparxie is plotting. The longer we delay, the worse it could get... Better to rescue them as soon as possible.

> **丹恒：**虽然三月和瓦尔特先生听起来暂时没有性命之忧，但谁也不知道火花想做什么。为免夜长梦多，还是尽快将他们解救出来吧。

**Dan Heng:** The Phantasmoon Games... What a bizarre ceremony. What does this type of Elation really mean for the people of this place? Why would Aha promote such a game?

> **丹恒：**「幻月游戏」…真是个奇怪的仪式。对此地的居民来说，这样的「欢愉」到底意味着什么？阿哈为什么要推行这样的游戏？

- ▸ Don't worry. I'm here with you.

> ▸ 放心吧，这里还有我呢。

**Dan Heng:** Whatever happens, keep me posted on how things go.

> **丹恒：**无论发生什么，记得告诉我进展。

[Recap] Tingyun was thrilled to reunite with you in Planarcadia. However, acting on Yao Guang's instructions, she had to head to a designated meeting point after Yao Guang's disappearance and took her leave.

*(Talk to Tingyun, optional)*

**Tingyun:** Greetings, benefactor. It's been many days since we met in Penacony, and I've been thinking of you day and night.

> **停云：**见过恩公。匹诺康尼至此，已是一别多日，小女子可是日夜挂念您呐。

- ▸ Tingyun... it's been a while.

> ▸ 停云，好久不见了。

- ▸ How are things these days?

> ▸ 你最近还好吗？

**Tingyun:** Thank you for your concern. I'm doing well.

> **停云：**感谢您的关心，小女子身体无恙。

**Tingyun:** Though my skills are meager, Madam Yao praised my resolve to kill my enemies, saying that was just the "variable" she sought. That's why she assigned me to temporarily serve under her command for this expedition. And look at that... it's brought us back together again!

> **停云：**虽然停云本事低微，但好在这位爻老板夸我有杀贼之心，正是她想要的「变数」。故此，这趟出行她调了我来寄在帐下听令。这不，就和您再续前缘了嘛！

**Tingyun:** What fortune! I wished I could travel with you longer back in Penacony, benefactor. After all, I've yet to reclaim the opportunities Phantylia stole from me.

> **停云：**可巧，匹诺康尼时也曾想过，如能和恩公多同行几日就好了——毕竟，小女子被幻胧夺去的缘分，还没全收回来呢。

- ▸ What kind of person is Madam Yao?

> ▸ 这位爻老板人怎么样？

**Tingyun:** Hard to say. Like the divinations she casts, she's a riddle shrouded in fog. Apart from the ever-absent Patina Justice, she's likely the most mysterious of the Arbiter Generals.

> **停云：**小女子可说不好。就像她卜的卦，如谜如雾，令人捉摸不透。也许除却从不现身的「尘冥将军」外，帝弓天将中就属这位大人最神秘莫测了。

- ▸ How is Madam Yao treating you?

> ▸ 爻老板对你好吗？

**Tingyun:** I'm sure there's no mortal danger, teehee... Please forgive my jest, benefactor. Being around Madam Yao has gotten me into the habit of cracking jokes all the time.

> **停云：**想来应该不至有性命之忧吧~恩公莫怪，跟在这位爻老板的身旁，小女子也习惯起随时开玩笑了。

**Tingyun:** Her actions may seem inscrutable, but she always provides what you need ahead of time. That's why even those who grumble still carry out her orders with diligence. In truth, doesn't she seem more like a shrewd merchant than a diviner?

> **停云：**这位将军虽说行事莫测，但却总能，先一步予你所求，所以她差遣的人纵有腹诽，也会尽心办事。仔细想来，这位比起卜者更像是绝顶的商人？

**Tingyun:** But then, when have any Arbiter Generals ever been ordinary?

> **停云：**唉，帝弓天将嘛，又有哪个是平庸之辈呢？

- ▸ I'll drop by to see you when I get the chance!

> ▸ 得空来看你！

**Tingyun:** Don't go back on your word, benefactor! We can find a local bar and share a drink while we chat.

> **停云：**恩公可不要食言喔！我们可以找一间当地酒馆，举杯闲话。

**Tingyun:** Madam Yao instructed me to meet her at our agreed spot if she disappears. It's growing late, so I'll take my leave now.

> **停云：**爻老板嘱咐，若她遁去身形，要我去约定的地方见面。时候不早了，小女子先行告退了。

[Recap] Sunday showed you his mask, mentioning that it whispered to him and urged him to reveal his "elation" to the god. Before departing to discuss cooperation plans with Yao Guang, he wished you good luck.

*(Talk to Sunday, optional)*

**Sunday:** ...A mask of elation can reflect the shape of one's soul?

> **星期日：**…欢愉的面具，能映照出灵魂的形状？

- ▸ Sunny, can I take a look at your mask?

> ▸ 老日，能看看你的面具吗？

**Sunday:** Sure.

> **星期日：**好啊。

**Sunday:** In Penacony's decorative arts, ravens often symbolize the hidden desires and whispers of humans' dreamscapes.

> **星期日：**在匹诺康尼的装饰艺术里，乌鸦，通常会被用来表现人类梦境中潜在的渴望和低语。

- ▸ Don't get too carried away now.

> ▸ 你可别太投入啊。

**Sunday:** Don't worry, I once tried to ascend to godhood myself... I won't be dazzled by the honor of an audience with a deity.

> **星期日：**放心吧，我曾有过试图登神之举，不会被谒神的殊荣所迷惑。

**Sunday:** Taking part in this grand event is also about gaining a deeper understanding of this... "arcadia." Just as one cannot miss the Charmony Festival when visiting Penacony.

> **星期日：**参与这场盛世，也是为了能更为深入地了解这片…「乐园」。就像人们身处匹诺康尼时，不能错过谐乐大典一样。

- ▸ Will we become enemies?

> ▸ 我们会成为敌人吗？

**Sunday:** Hmm? There's no rule saying that Supplicants of the Phantasmoon Games have to fight each other, right?

> **星期日：**唔？没有哪条规则要求幻月游戏的谒者必须彼此厮杀吧？

**Sunday:** On the contrary, for the Elation, shouldn't laughter be a resonance that comes from within each other's hearts?

> **星期日：**恰恰相反，对「欢愉」来说，笑声不该是从彼此内心发出的共鸣吗？

- ▸ What's the deal with this mask anyway?

> ▸ 这副面具到底有什么作用？

**Sunday:** After putting it on, I seemed to hear... curious voices.

> **星期日：**它扣在我脸上后，我似乎察觉到了一些…有趣的声音。

**Sunday:** The mask whispers to me... urging me to show my "elation" to the god, to create miracles that will make the people of the arcadia laugh with joy.

> **星期日：**面具在向我低语：要我向什么神明展露自己的「欢愉」，尽力创造奇迹让乐园的民众为之欢笑。

- ▸ Now would be the perfect moment!

> ▸ 此刻这正是契机！

- ▸ Sunday will now embark on the Path of Elation!

> ▸ 踏上欢愉命途的星期日！

**Sunday:** Haha, that's a good one!

> **星期日：**哈哈，是个不错的笑话。

**Sunday:** When that General Yao Guang shows up again, I should discuss our collaboration plans with her. Good luck, (Trailblazer).

> **星期日：**等那位爻光将军再度现身，我该去和她谈谈合作的计划了。祝你好运，开拓者。

[Recap] Before setting out to search for the tavern, you recalled Sparkle's riddle: "Whether looking up or down, they all see the same Phantasmoon when gazing at the sky." Himeko figured the answer was Dovebrook.

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**Himeko:** The IPC, Masked Fools, a Xianzhou general, the Stellaron Hunters, and the Nameless...

> **姬子：**星际和平公司、假面愚者、仙舟将军、星核猎手、还有无名客……

**Himeko:** Some came for the alliance, some for the Aeon's game... Whatever their reasons, a storm is brewing in the skies over this world.

> **姬子：**有些为同盟而来，有些为星神的游戏而来，无论为什么，风暴已在这个世界的天空中汇聚成形了。

**Himeko:** But at least we got some good news. Sunday has joined the Phantasmoon Games. He can help us in our showdown against Sparxie.

> **姬子：**不过眼前至少听到了个好消息，星期日加入了幻月游戏，在和火花的对垒中，他可以帮上我们的忙。

**Himeko:** Let's rendezvous with Sparkle at the tavern. We must uncover why Sparxie entered the Phantasmoon Games and what sort of "SparxiCon" she's got planned for Graphia Academy.

> **姬子：**去「酒馆」和花火碰头吧，接下来，我们要弄清火花加入幻月游戏的目标，还有她到底想在绘世学院中策划一场怎样的「火花大会」。

- ▸ So how do we get into the tavern?

> ▸ 所以该怎么才能进入酒馆？

**Himeko:** When I was little, I heard a few urban legends. They say the entrance to the tavern exists behind every single door in this land. But those who aren't invited, or haven't found their destined path, can never step inside.

> **姬子：**我小时候就听过一些都市传说，说「酒馆」的入口存在于这片土地上的每一扇门后。但不经邀请，或是没有机缘寻得路径的人，就无法踏入其中。

**Himeko:** This time, we already have the Fools' invitation in hand. As for the key to open the door... there's a silly rhyme on the back of the business card: "For revelry and drinks galore, no need to search from door to door. Heed the Fool's riddle with care, to find the key that leads you there."

> **姬子：**这一次，我们已经手握愚者的邀请了。至于开门的钥匙…名片的背面还有一首打油诗：「若想痛饮喧闹，不必费心寻找，留神愚者谜题之邀，引你寻得门扉之钥。」

- ▸ Ugh, the Riddlers again?

> ▸ 谜语人又来了？

- ▸ That rhyme's worse than what Rappa comes up with.

> ▸ 这押韵还不如乱破。

**Himeko:** So, (Trailblazer), about the Fool's Riddle... got any leads?

> **姬子：**所以，开拓者，关于「愚者的谜题之邀」…你有什么头绪吗？

**(Trailblazer):** ...

> **开拓者：**……

A long time ago—you scoff to yourself, who even remembers after all this time? But you do remember. When Sparkle first met you, she told you a riddle.

**Sparkle:** Whether they look up or down, the moon that humanity gazes upon in the sky is always the same Phantasmoon.

> **花火：**无论抬头或是低头，人类凝望天空时，看到的都是同一轮幻月。

**Himeko:** Looking up makes sense. But looking down... how exactly do you gaze at the sky?

> **姬子：**抬头还能理解，既然低下了头，又要怎么凝望天空呢？

- ▸ By watching the sky reflected in water.

> ▸ 看着水中的天空。

- ▸ By gazing at the reflected sky.

> ▸ 看着倒影的天空。

**Himeko:** (Trailblazer), it seems fate ties you to the tavern. I get it now. The answer is Dovebrook.

> **姬子：**开拓者，看来你和「酒馆」确实有缘。我明白了，谜底是鸽川。

## 173　Medical Disputes on the Ship of Fools　愚人船上的医患纠纷

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Dovebrook District　|　角色：Ashveil; Sampo; Sparkle; Trailblazer; Himeko; Sparxie; Zhongshan; Giovanni; Paraprince; "Narrator"; Azurewing; Blazeweaver; Magnus; Dawnlance　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Here's Your Fifteen Minutes of Fame'' completed

### Head to Dovebrook and find the entrance to the tavern

> *"Whether looking up or down, they all see the same Phantasmoon when gazing at the sky." Sparkle left behind this riddle as a hint to the tavern's location. Looks like the entrance to the "World's End" won't be so easy to find...*

*(Go to mission location)*

**Himeko:** Many generations ago, the Dovebrook was already a river flowing with countless legends.

> **姬子：**酒馆的门扉藏在其中，倒也不奇怪。

**Himeko:** It's no surprise the door to the tavern lies hidden among them.

> **姬子：**传说大画师绘世曾在鸽川取水洗笔用于作画。

**Himeko:** Legend has it that the master painter Graphia once drew water from the Dovebrook to wash her paintbrushes.

> **姬子：**许多后世的绘师也纷纷效仿，因此这儿也有「绘川」的别名。

**Himeko:** Many later artists followed suit. Hence, its other name, the "Painters' Stream."

> **姬子：**看到鸽川的景色…学生时代的回忆不自觉地涌上心头了。

*(Investigate the Daybreak Squadron, optional)*

**Red-Haired Youth:** Can't believe those Synwish Syndicate folks would stir up such a commotion right when Phantasmoon is at its fullest... What's your take on this, Blue?

**Blue-Haired Youth:** At this very moment, there's barely any dark force that isn't coveting the Phantasmoon Games and the power of Aha...

**Blue-Haired Youth:** That means, out there are countless villains, while the heroes are few and far between.

**Yellow-Haired Girl:** These little thugs are like those swarms of disposable grunts in a movie's big finale. They just go "yee-yah" and make a racket, but can't stir up any real trouble.

**Yellow-Haired Girl:** No need for you guys to transform. I can take care of them all by myself.

**Red-Haired Youth:** Come on, Yellow, did you forget again? We Daybreak Squadron are heroes who protect Planarcadia, not some street gang picking fights!

**Red-Haired Youth:** Tell me, what are we fighting for?

**Yellow-Haired Girl:** For everyone's happiness and smiles!

**Red-Haired Youth:** Good, now's not the time to expose our identity. Let us watch over this land under the Phantasmoon, protecting the peaceful slumber of...

**Gray-Haired Youth:** Boss, aren't we heading back yet? The director's been waiting for us for ages.

**Gray-Haired Youth:** And as I said before, the Synwish Syndicate isn't as bad as you all think. You know, they won't hurt anyone.

**Red-Haired Youth:** Gray, fall back. Let me face off with these evil villains for a bit longer.

[Recap] Himeko recalled her student days, when she was beating up street thugs and sending them packing into Dovebrook to stand up for her juniors. Fifteen years passed in the blink of an eye, and the once reckless Himeko had become the Astral Express's Navigator.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Himeko:** Looking at Dovebrook's scenery... memories from my student days just come flooding back.

> **姬子：**也算不上什么有趣的回忆。那时候鸽川区的帮派众多，混混也满街都是，其中有些人总喜欢来找学生的麻烦。

- ▸ Himeko's student days?
- ▸ Tell me, tell me! I want to hear all about it!

**Himeko:** They're not exactly interesting memories. Back then, Dovebrook District was teeming with gangs. There were hooligans running around everywhere, and some really loved messing with the students.

> **姬子：**我长得比同龄人高大一些，恰好在练习格斗技巧。为了给学弟学妹们讨回公道，我时不时会在街上…和混混们开战。

**Himeko:** I was taller than my peers and happened to be training in martial arts. To stand up for my juniors, I would occasionally... get into scraps with the thugs on the streets.

> **姬子：**落进鸽川的人就像拉面里的豆芽一样铺满了水面…那样的情景恍如昨日。

**Himeko:** The people who fell into the Dovebrook river would cover the surface like bean sprouts in noodle soup... It all feels like it was just yesterday.

> **姬子：**有时候是混混落水，有时候却是我自己掉进水里。

- ▸ Himeko, noodle master!
- ▸ A thug falling into the water... what a scene!

**Himeko:** Sometimes the thugs fell in. Other times, I took the plunge myself.

> **姬子：**那个时候的我活蹦乱跳的，老师总说我像个「上足了发条不停敲锣打鼓的玩具」，走到哪儿都要闹出些大动静。

**Himeko:** Back then I was a bundle of restless energy. The teachers always said I was like "a wind-up toy that never stopped banging its drums and cymbals," stirring up a commotion wherever I went.

> **姬子：**一眨眼，十五年过去了。真是难以想象，这样莽撞任性的我，竟然成了星穹列车的领航员。

**Himeko:** In the blink of an eye, fifteen years have passed. Hard to believe someone as reckless and willful as me became the Navigator of the Astral Express.

> **姬子：**眼下不该是伤春悲秋的时间。我们到鸽川边了，「酒馆」的入口藏在哪儿呢?

**Himeko:** Now's not the time to wax sentimental. We've reached the banks of the Dovebrook, so where's the "tavern" entrance hidden?

> **姬子：**「共愿帮征用中」…看来得找别的路去对岸了。

### Travel through the passage in the painting to the other side of Dovebrook to have a look

*(Investigate the Interplanar Jump! pipe of the Synwish Syndicate, optional)*

**Himeko:** "Under Synwish Syndicate requisition"... Looks like we'll have to find another way across.

> **姬子：**眼下咱们该专注寻找「酒馆」的入口，闲逛的话之后还有机会。

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

**Himeko:** According to the business card, the "tavern" is near the Synwish Syndicate headquarters.

> **姬子：**按照名片所说，「酒馆」在共愿帮总部附近。

**Himeko:** Let's check the other side of the river.

> **姬子：**我们去河对岸看看。

*(Exit Interplanar Jump!)*

### Ask the drunkard by the road

[Recap] A drunk by the roadside told you a joke about Dovebrook: Someone once tried to cross it by stepping on a pedestal of light, only to discover it was merely the moon's reflection on the water — shattering at the slightest touch.

*(Approach the Drunkard)*

**Drunkard:** Hey folks... have y'all heard the joke about the Dovebrook?

> **醉汉：**诸位…诸位听过那个关于鸽川的笑话吗？

- ▸ Come on, tell us!

**Drunkard:** One day, a criminal escaped from prison. He ran for his life, but just when freedom seemed within reach, the Dovebrook blocked his path.

> **醉汉：**有一天，有个罪人逃出了监狱。他没命地跑啊跑，眼看要逃出生天，却被鸽川拦住了去路。

**Drunkard:** With his pursuers closing in, the man prayed to the river: "I am hammered by the law, stained with filth. O pure, flowing waters, please do not swallow me."

> **醉汉：**眼看身后追兵就要到了，那人便对着鸽川祈祷：「我是个满身污秽的罪人，高洁的流水啊，请不要吞没我。」

**Drunkard:** A voice from the Dovebrook replied, "There's a pedestal of light upon the water. Step on it, and you can cross!"

> **醉汉：**鸽川中有个声音这样回应他道：「水上有个光的台座，踩着它就能过河!」

**Drunkard:** The criminal wept: "What pedestal of light? It's only the moon mirrored on the water, an image that shatters at a touch! If I step on it, I'll fall in and drown!"

> **醉汉：**罪人哭了：「哪有什么光的台座？不过是天上的月亮映在水中的影子罢了，这个影子一碰就碎！踩上去我不就掉到河里淹死了吗？」

**Drunkard:** Hahaha, guess how the Dovebrook answered him?

> **醉汉：**哈哈哈，你们猜鸽川怎么回答他？

**Drunkard:** The Dovebrook said, "You're not 'hammered' at all. Otherwise, how could you still tell the moon from a pedestal?" Hahaha... HAHAHA!

> **醉汉：**鸽川这样说道：「你根本没『醉』，不然怎么还能分清月亮与台座？」哈哈哈…哈哈哈哈！

- ▸ What are you even talking about?
- ▸ What are you implying?
- ▸ That's such a dry joke...

**Drunkard:** I-It's fine... You don't have to buy me a drink. As long as I can make you laugh! Hahahahaha!

> **醉汉：**没、没关系…不必非得请我喝一杯的。只要能博各位一笑就好！哈哈哈哈哈！

> **姬子：**你劫持了一整座学校的师生，把他们变成你的傀儡，为你加入「幻月游戏」。前马后效劳——你该不会想狡辩自己是为他们做了这些的吧？

Sparxie's Worries: Of course I did! From the very first day I was created, I've been doing exactly what my creator asked of me: listening to every fan's questions, empathizing with their pain and troubles.

Sparxie's Worries: They feel insecure about being ordinary, cry when ignored by those they like, and strive to stand out because they desperately want others' approval...

Sparxie's Worries: Sparxie hears every wish, and she answers!

Sparxie's Worries: During these Phantasmoon Games, I'll grant their wishes through the Ceremony of the Master of Elation...

- ▸ With a memetic virus?
- ▸ By brainwashing them?

Sparxie's Worries: Because deep down, every one of them longs to become Sparxie. To wear this dazzling mask, capture the internet's attention, and bask in praise.

Sparxie's Worries: You must have heard that saying: "In the future, everyone gets their fifteen minutes of fame."

Sparxie's Worries: Now, that moment has come. SparxiCon will turn every spectator into me.

Sparxie's Worries: Oh? And who gets to define "elation"? The Laughter? The Fools? The IPC? Or... every "me" seeking joy on the arcadian internet?

Sparxie's Worries: Contrary to popular belief, the masters of the Phantasmoon Games were never us, the Supplicants, but the spectators watching us.

Sparxie's Worries: Sadly, the Interastral Peace Network showed people other worlds in the cosmos. Once horizons widen, expectations climb... nothing they hear or see feels fresh, interesting, or thrilling enough.

Sparxie's Worries: And everyone fears that they're outdated, uninteresting, or unexciting in others' eyes.

Sparxie's Worries: So the world's like a derailed train, racing toward thrills and madness... Nameless of the Astral Express, am I wrong?

Sparxie's Madness: I want to see how far you and this outdated model can go. The harder you struggle, the more spectacular it'll be when I livestream the Astral Express crew's "derailment" at SparxiCon!

Sparxie's Madness: I'm looking forward to your resistance, Nameless.

A patient claims to be Ena incarnate, and those who give them a thumbs up are blessed pathstriders of the Order.

The next record is even more absurd. The patient claims that HE is Ena, and that the person who claims to be Ena is actually Yaoshi who has gone mad.

Next page, the patient searches everywhere for the "Space Bar," believing that their system lacks a jump function. Next page...

(Quick read)

Medical Record: Yao Guang

**Name:** Yao Guang Sex: Female Homeland: Yuque Species: Humanoid, Category: Celestial Subspecies Department: Temporal Department Chief Complaint: The patient suffers from an unusual chronological disease. The disease has lasted for 30 years, and causes skips of space-time induced misplacement. The disease has temporarily been dubbed "Temporal Dislocation." Present Illness: Thirty years ago, the patient experienced an accident in an extrapolation array, leading to symptoms of temporal displacement. No similar cases have been reported to date. During episodes, the patient unconsciously deviates from their current spacetime coordinates, leaping through the spacetime continuum and reappearing at incorrect temporal points. According to previous diagnostic records, temporal displacement ranges approximately ±1 to 12 hours, accompanied by spatial displacement within a radius of 15 kilometers. The cause of the episodes remains unknown, and the pattern of occurrence is still unclear, though there is a suspected increase in frequency. History: Infinite lifespan in a stable state, with no risk of becoming mara-struck. Follow-up Report: The patient is in good spirits, with stable breathing, clear speech, and normal physical condition. There is no trend of disease progression. After wearing the "Nexus Sapientiae," the frequency of displacement episodes has decreased. The patient's self-awareness remains uninterrupted during temporal leaps, and brain function remains highly active post-displacement, suggesting a possible maintained connection with the spacetime structure of the Deca-Light Reflection Barrier. Following displacement episodes, the patient exhibits a temporary decrease in blood pressure and blood sugar. Cardiopulmonary function and bone density remain unaffected by spacetime displacement. Prescription: Carry sugar pills and ensure regular intakes of sugar. Attached is a long-term leave permit. (Declaration rejected by the patient) Treatment: Schedule a visit to the clinic upon feeling unwell

**(Trailblazer):** No way... Are these... General Yao Guang's medical records?

You instinctively look away. Some things are better left unknown.

*(Investigate bookshelf)*

**Zhongshan:** The medical records are on the bookshelf, first file on the left.

> **钟珊：**病历就放在书架上，左手第一个文件。

(Quick read)

"Sparxie Virus" Cases and Treatment Plans

Affected by the Supplicant Sparxie, the "Sparxie Virus" is spreading outward from Graphia Academy to surrounding areas. The Department of Aberration Defense has activated the Phantasmoon Games emergency response plan, established quarantine zones, and is providing targeted treatment to infected civilians. Based on infection vectors, several preliminary treatment protocols have been established. All academy memetic treatment facilities are advised to reference these protocols when receiving patients. Details are outlined below. To ensure the safety of medical personnel, this report has been treated with memetic scrubbing procedures and is safe to use. Text-Based Transmission Infection Vector & Symptoms The "Sparxie Virus" possesses the ability to spread through written text. Upon viewing and comprehending virus-laden text, the memetic agent infiltrates the patient's self-awareness, causing them to continuously repeat phrases such as "I love Sparxicle" and "I want to become Sparxicle." The condition worsens with each repetition, gradually transforming their self-awareness into that of Sparxicle. It should be noted that patients infected via text are highly likely to have been exposed to compound infection from both auditory and visual memetic vectors. Soundproofing measures must be strictly implemented during treatment. Treatment Plan Immediately isolate the patient to prevent secondary exposure to the memetic virus. All treatment equipment must be disconnected from the network after memetic decontamination. Then, following standard prescription procedures for textual memes, inject the patient with massive amounts of redundant, harmless information to dilute and overwrite the "Sparxie Virus" in the patient's cognition. Text frequency should be maintained between 22 to 26 characters per second, with each treatment session controlled within 0.5 system hours. Audio Transmission Infection Method and Symptoms The least harmful variant, with distinct phase differentiation. In Phase One, after hearing specific trigger words, "Sparxicle" invades the auditory system, replacing all information the patient hears with "Sparxicle." In Phase Two, all sounds produced by the patient are replaced with "Sparxicle," and they become contagious. The transmission method at this phase is extremely covert. For example, patients will hide "Sparxicle" within fragmented speech, using patterns like "Hey, what should we have for dinner tonight? How about Sparghetti and meatballs with xicle sauce?" Please exercise extreme caution. Treatment Plan Expose the patient to high noise environments. This will continuously disrupt the normal functioning of their auditory organs (No need to worry about damage to the patient. We've imported advanced medical equipment from the Interastral Peace Corporation. There won't be any permanent residual damage.) Then, the correction device will be used to control the patient's vocal organs and force them to read the Intellitron Basic Emotion Test and explain the rules until the patient is free from the memetic influence. Visual Transmission Infection Pathways and Symptoms High-risk infection type: When the patient is exposed to patterns containing the "Sparxie Virus" on the network, their cognition will rapidly become infected. Memes will cover their visual senses, causing the patient to perceive all faces as "Sparxie" and their own face will also shift into being "Sparxie." Additionally, there are also extremely dangerous infected individuals who will sport Sparxie on the surface of their retinas, and anyone they gaze upon will become infected. Treatment Plan Enclose the patient in a dark room and separate them from direct visual contact. Use counter-memetic images provided by the IPC to train patients with visual functions to precisely remove the memetic virus engraved in their visual system. Currently, the "Sparxie Virus" continues to spread, with potential for memetic mutation. If any unusual situations are discovered, report them immediately to the Department of Aberration Defense. Do not attempt to handle it on your own.

**Zhongshan:** Miss Sparkle, I'm going to write up a detailed diagnosis report for you! In the meantime, why don't you chat with your fellow patients who share your affliction?

> **钟珊：**花火小姐，接下来我要为您开具一份详细的诊断报告！就趁这工夫，请您和同病相怜的病友谈谈吧。

### Talk with Sparkle's fellow patients

**Sparkle:** Gray Hair, let's go. I don't want to waste any more time with this quack.

> **花火：**小灰毛，我们走吧没我不想和这个庸医浪费时间了。

**Zhongshan:** Miss Sparkle, please wait in this room until the consultation is complete.

**Weak Mask:** I once suffered from a disease called "Fool."

> **虚弱的面具：**我的身上长出了一个名为「花火」的假面愚者，她慢慢和我融为了一体。她用我来承担扮演时所有的痛苦，她让我遍体鳞伤，自己却狡猾地躲过了一切……

**Weak Mask:** But I was lucky enough to find this tumor called "Sparkle", and I... expelled this malignant growth from my body.

> **虚弱的面具：**不过我的运气够好，我找到了这个名叫「花火」的病灶，我把她…把这颗万恶的毒瘤排出了体外。

> **虚弱的面具：**不，我不要再长出「花火」来——

**Sad Mask:** You used me to win fame, applause, and countless eager gazes... so why would you cruelly cast me aside?

> **微笑的面具：**这个世界不想看到太多悲伤的、残忍的、愤怒的、痛苦的情绪…是你告诉我，任何时候只要微笑就好。

**Sad Mask:** Please, wear me once more...

**Sparkle:** The worst thing an actor should do is be tied down to one type of role.

**Sparkle:** Do yourself a favor and stay down!

**Smiling Mask:** This world doesn't want to see too much sadness, cruelty, rage, or pain... You told me that I just need to smile, no matter what.

### Talk to Zhongshan

[Recap] As a mere mask, Sparxie was the one the world loves, while no one cared for the Sparkle underneath. Zhongshan's countermeasure was to "cremate Sparkle." By burning the person, the Sparxie persona would lose its spark and become a dead thing.

**Zhongshan:** Miss Sparkle, I have your results. Your main components are madness, humor, and a theatrical personality. Unfortunately, the proportions are badly imbalanced. You're beyond saving.

> **钟珊：**花火小姐，诊断有结论了。您的主要成分是疯狂、幽默感和表演型人格。很不幸，您的配比失衡，早已病入膏肓了。

**Zhongshan:** But the silver lining is, your mask has fallen off! She's free at last!

> **钟珊：**但，不幸中的万幸是，你的面具从你身上脱落了！她自由了！

**Sparkle:** And that's where everyone's misfortune began. None of the masks I've cast off ever had such strong opinions or such a temper.

> **花火：**这就是一切大家不幸的开始啊。我抛下的每一张面具里还从没有哪一个主意和脾气都这么大的。

- ▸ She's not just your virtual persona...
- ▸ Is Sparxie also one of your Fool's Masks?

**Zhongshan:** She no longer needs to be corroded by your cancer of rationality. Now, she can do anything that you wanted to do but didn't dare to do.

> **钟珊：**她不用再受你的理性之癌侵蚀，她可以做任何你想做而不敢做的事情。

**Sparkle:** Ha! As if there's anything Miss Sparkle wouldn't dare to do!

> **花火：**笑话，世界上还有什么花火大人不敢做的事情？

**Zhongshan:** For example... you don't dare wear any one mask for too long.

> **钟珊：**举例来说…不敢佩戴一张面具太久。

**Sparkle:** ...

> **花火：**……

**Zhongshan:** Like a butterfly shedding old skin, you keep discarding every mask, just so you won't be defined or bound by them. You're afraid to stop, to be stuck in some stereotype. You're afraid that one day you won't be "elating" enough...

> **钟珊：**你像蝴蝶蜕去旧皮那样不断抛弃着每一张面具，只为了不被它们定义、束缚，你害怕停下来，停留在某个刻板印象里，你害怕自己有一天不够「欢愉」——

**Zhongshan:** ...That's why you drew Miss "Sparxie," and became her online.

> **钟珊：**—一所以你才画出了「火花」小姐，在网络上成为她。

**Zhongshan:** As a dazzling mask, everyone confides in her, everyone wants to be her, everyone feeds her their clever ideas. You thought this would inject new life into your "elation"...

> **钟珊：**作为一张光鲜亮丽的面具，人人都会向她倾诉，人人都想成为她，人人都为她贡献出自己的有趣点子，你本以为这会给你的「欢愉」注入生命力——

**Zhongshan:** But sadly, once the mask is on, you become its accessory. People care only about the image the mask paints. No one cares about the you beneath.

> **钟珊：**但很可惜，一旦戴上了面具，人就成了面具的附属，人们只在乎面具描绘的形象，不会有人关心底下的你。

**Zhongshan:** And that is why she came to life!

> **钟珊：**而这，就是她拥有生命的原因！

**Zhongshan:** Human recognition and desire will flow through the internet, providing nourishment for her to bloom: She'll become a bewitching flower, conforming to everyone's wishes, letting them wear her, become her, and blossom across all of Planarcadia! What a beautiful sight...

> **钟珊：**人类的认同和渴望，会顺着网络的根系为她提供绽放的养料：她会成为一朵妖花，顺应所有人的愿望让人佩戴自己，复制自己，开遍整个二相乐园！多美丽的景象啊……

- ▸ Doctor, do you have a way to stop her?

**Zhongshan:** "Stop"? You mean... "treat," yes?

> **钟珊：**「阻止」？你是说…「治疗」对吧？

**Zhongshan:** Cremate Sparkle. That's the best treatment I can prescribe.

> **钟珊：**「火化花火」，这就是我能开出的最佳治疗方案。

**Sparkle:** Pfft. And here I thought you had something clever.

> **花火：**噗，我还以为你能有什么高招呢？

**Sparkle:** After all, there's only one radical cure for all suffering: make oneself disappear, right?

> **花火：**毕竟，一切痛苦有且只有一种彻底的疗法：让自己消失，对吧？

**Zhongshan:** Miss Sparkle, don't think I'm joking around with you. I only misbehave outside the tavern.

> **钟珊：**花火小姐。可不要觉得我是在对你开玩笑。我只在「酒馆」以外的地方才会没个正形。

**Zhongshan:** But here I am a doctor, without quotation marks. My treatment is real and effective. Generally, when a Fool vanishes, their mask loses its power and becomes a dead thing. You're two sides of the same coin, after all.

> **钟珊：**而在这里，我是个医生，不带引号的那种，所以我的疗法也真实有效——一般来说，愚者消失时，她的面具也会失去力量成为死物，毕竟你们是互为表里的存在。

**Zhongshan:** But as for "Sparxie"... I can't say for sure. During SparxiCon, she'll be worn by millions of ordinary people in Planarcadia, feeding her with sustenance and strength. Your window of opportunity is therefore fleeting...

> **钟珊：**不过，关于「火花」…我说不好，因为在「火花大会」上要佩戴她的，将是二相乐园的亿万普通人，这些人会为她提供滋养和力量。所以,你们的时机稍纵即逝——

**Zhongshan:** Go on, lie down on that bed and say goodbye to your friends. Press that button, and the "nurse" will come wheel you away.

> **z钟珊：**去吧，躺到那张床上去，和你的朋友们告别吧。按下那颗按钮，「护士」会过来把你推走。

**Zhongshan:** There's a door in the tavern that absolutely, positively, leads to a still-burning star in Sedounirama. The "nurse" will send you there, and that'll be the end of it.

> **钟珊：**酒馆里有扇门，确定、一定以及肯定能通往赛杜尼拉默一颗还没熄灭的恒星。「护士」会把你推到那儿去，一了百了。

**Sparkle:** And if I say no?

> **花火：**我要是说不呢？

**Zhongshan:** Then I'll have to... be impolite.

> **钟珊：**那就恕我…不客气啦。

**Sparkle:** ...?

> **花火：**……？

**Zhongshan:** Miss Sparkle, (Trailblazer), the choice is yours. As for me... By The Laughter above, I've got to catch the next ship out of this dump.

> **钟珊：**花火小姐，无名客，选择已经交给你们了。至于我…乐子神在上，我得赶快搭下一班飞船离开这破地方了。

### Talk to Sparkle

*(Idle dialogue from Sparkle)*

**Sparkle:** Hey! What's going on! Why am I pinned here!?

> **花火：**喂！什么情况！怎么就把我摁在这儿了！花火在病床上剧烈挣扎……

**Sparkle:** Gray Hair? Ma'am Navigator? A little help?

*(Talk to Sparkle)*

**Himeko:** The mask of the Masked Fool represents what you wish to become. You use it to focus your power, but the mask will gradually bind you as well.

> **姬子：**假面愚者的面具，代表着你们想要成为的样子。你们用它来聚焦自己的力量，但同样面具也会渐渐束缚住你们。

**Himeko:** That's why you've been avoiding Sparxie all this time. She didn't just steal a vial of memetic virus... She stole your strength.

> **姬子：**这就是你一直在逃避火花的原因。她从你这边偷走的不只是一筒模因病毒…还有你的力量。

**Himeko:** No wonder the Fool at the bar joked: when a Masked Fool loses their mask, what remains is just a fool.

> **姬子：**也难怪，吧台的愚者说了这样的笑话：「假面愚者」失去了假面，剩下的不过是个愚者而已。

**Sparkle:** ...

**Sparkle:** Yes, every Fool has at least one such mask. Not mere decoration, but a part of our destiny, crafted by our own hands...

> **花火：**是啊，每个愚者都拥有这样一张假面，至少一张。它们不是装饰，而是我们命运的一部分，是我们亲手造就了它们一—

**Sparkle:** It might be a Mourning Actor mask you took a liking to and stole from them. It might be a head accessory you personally sketched in a moment of inspiration. Or it might be something that one day fell from the sky and latched onto your face...

> **花火：**从悲悼伶人那儿偷走最顺眼的假面；灵感降临时自己亲手绘制的头饰；又或者某一天从天而降扣在自己脸上的东西……

**Sparkle:** To become a Fool, one must experience at least one such instance of destiny: the moment of masking one's face.

> **花火：**能成为愚者的人，至少会经历一次这样的命运时刻：覆面的时刻。

**Sparkle:** Through the mask's eyeholes, we gain a different perspective, finally seeing how the universe is simply churning with endless jokes and the absurd nature of our existence as living beings.

> **花火：**透过面具的眼洞，我们获得了不同的视角，瞧见了宇宙中翻涌着无穷无尽的玩笑，还有我们作为生命的可笑本质。

**Sparkle:** The mask is what we want to be. And now, this impostor is more "me" than I am.

> **花火：**面具就是我们想成为的样子。如今，这个冒牌货比我更像我了。

**Sparkle:** Do it! Press the button, call the nurse, and cremate Miss Sparkle!

> **花火：**动手吧！按下按钮叫来护士，把花火大人火化了！

**Sparkle:** At least then the impostor will lose her power for a little while. You should be able to take her down easily, right?

**Sparkle:** Uh... you're not actually going to press it, right?

> **花火：**呜，你们……不会按铃的，对吧？

**Himeko:** Give us a moment... to hesitate, to think.

> **姬子：**给一点时间，让我们犹豫犹豫、考虑考虑。

**Sparkle:** Waiting is torture, you know. You're not doing this on purpose to punish me, are you? Hey, Gray Hair! Say something!

> **花火：**等待可是很折磨人的。你俩不会是故意要这么惩罚我吧？喂，小灰毛？你好歹说句话呀！

- ▸ (Don't press the button)

**Sparkle:** Psst, let me tell you something. While no one was looking, I already popped the battery out of the button. This was a test, and you passed!

> **花火：**悄悄告诉你个事，我刚才趁着大家不注意已经抠掉了按钮的电池。这个考验，你通过了！

- ▸ (Press the button)

**Sparkle:** Unbelievable! Gray Hair, is your heart colder than Belobog's Ice Out of Space!?

> **花火：**难以置信！小灰毛，你的心比贝洛伯格的外宇宙之冰还冷吗？！

**Sparkle:** Hehe, I took the battery out of the button earlier while no one watched. It was a test, and you failed!

> **花火：**嘿嘿，我一早趁着大家不注意抠掉了按钮的电池。这是个考验，你没通过！

- ▸ (Frantically mashing buttons!)

**Sparkle:** Hey hey hey! Gray Hair, have you forgotten our friendship? You'll make the button smoke at this rate!

> **花火：**喂喂喂喂！小灰毛，咱们的交情你都忘光了吗？按钮都快被按冒烟啦！

**Sparkle:** Too bad. I took the battery out of the button earlier while no one watched. It was a test, and you failed!

> **花火：**可惜，我一早趁着大家不注意抠掉了按钮的电池。这是个考验，你没通过！

**Himeko:** Since the Fool made her choice, she must have a plan to reclaim the Mask, right?

> **姬子：**既然「愚者」自己做出了选择，她应该也想好了回收「假面」的办法，对吧？

> **姬子：**如果你敢说个「不」字，我可要按下按钮，让天基炮来「火化」你咯？你那不干净的小手应该没能快到偷走这块电池吧？

**Sparkle:** There's a way! There's always a way!

> **花火：**有办法有办法！办法总是有的！

**Sparkle:** Miss Sparkle will definitely take responsibility and work together with everyone to stop SparxiCon!

> **花火：**花火大人一定会负起责任，和大家齐心协力阻止「火花大会」的！

**Himeko:** What's with this applause?

> **姬子：**这掌声又是怎么回事？

**Sparkle:** Have we... been watched the whole time?

> **花火：**我们…是不是一直都在被谁看着？

### Step forward and smash the glass!

Sparkle finds herself bound to the bed by an invisible force. This irresistible power you've experienced yourself on mornings when you just can't get up. No one can defeat it!

**Himeko:** That's life for you. While there's life, there's judgment. In every unseen corner, countless judging eyes are watching, diagnosing, and writing their own "prescriptions."

> **姬子：**这是人生，生命不息，评判不止。在你看不到的角落里，总有数不尽的评判的目光，为你诊断，为你开出「药方」。

**Sparkle:** I've always had this premonition that one day Miss Sparkle would become an unstoppable terror in this universe. Someone has to find a way to stop me before that day comes!

**Sparkle:** On a trip, I met a girl who shared my tastes. She had quite the knack to be a Fool.

> **花火：**一次旅行，我遇见了某个和我志趣相投，颇有愚者天分的小姑娘。薇塔…？

> **花火：**「当欢愉之主攀上存在之树的高枝，祂窥见真空冰冷可憎，星辰机械运转，万物意义让位寸虚无。」

> **花火：**「祂继续望去，直到目睹一名婴儿落地啼哭犹如受尽委屈，便忍不住纵声大笑，这清澈的笑声撕裂了冰冷死寂的宇宙，回荡诸界至今。」

> **花火：**很多人都以为这撕裂宇宙的笑声只是某种比喻…但，很遗憾，它是真的。

> **花火：**「酒馆」里的人称这东西为「太初笑声的回响」，它撕开了一个空间口子。

### Check out the "Gift of The Laughter"

Angular and blocky, it hurts to hold, with a slight stinging sensation, like stepping on a small toy part hidden in the carpet.

> **姬子：**被「火花病毒」感染的观众当然没法反抗火花…我们闯进学校要不了多久就会变成那样——但如果在此之前，我们已经被另一种模因病毒感染了，那就很难说了。

> **姬子：**而这只「香蕉」的意义…正是给我们争取一点抵抗的时间。

> **姬子：**还真有「欢愉」独一份的幽默感啊，现在，我们走吧！

**Sparkle:** Who are you two? Haven't you heard the rule? Only one Fool's allowed in here at a time.

> **不死途：**我是谁？一个路过的名侦探，还有他的助手。

**Ashveil:** Have I been asleep too long? Or has humanity sunk so low they don't even care if they turn into monkeys?

> **不死途：**是我睡太久了吗？还是这个世代的人类已经堕落到了变成猴子也满不在乎的地步了？

> **「旁白」：**与此同时，无知的罪犯们自以为发现了能改变他们命运的「宝藏」，但他们浑然不觉，自己手里握着的东西足以摧毁整个世界的理智。

## 174　The Discreet Charm of a Streamer Girl　主播女孩的审慎魅力

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Duomension City　|　角色：Trailblazer; Himeko; Ryusuke; Asat Pramad; Sparxie; Sparkle; Yao Guang; Sunday; Welt; March 7th; Paraprince; Poofy; Snowie; Belle　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Medical Disputes on the Ship of Fools'' completed

### Go to Duomension City and continue the adventure

> *Everyone was fighting over the mask during the Phantasmoon Games, but General Yao Guang just gave it away. Was that just a whim, or is she planning something?*

[Recap] Sunday met with Yao Guang, wanting to know why she refused to don the mask and enter the fray herself. Yao Guang asked Sunday to use the mask-enhanced "tuning" ability and experience her answer firsthand.

Meanwhile, somewhere in Duomension City...

**Yao Guang:** Your thoughts tell me that you still have doubts about me.

> **爻光：**你的心声告诉我，对于我，你仍有疑虑。

**Yao Guang:** Did you ask to meet here so I could clear up some of your doubts?

> **爻光：**约在此地碰头，是想让我为你解惑一二？

**Sunday:** ...Greetings, Lady Yao Guang.

> **星期日：**…爻光大人。

**Yao Guang:** Being able to hear others' thoughts anytime... this "tuning" ability sure comes in handy.

> **爻光：**能随时倾听对方的心思，「调律」这个能力还真是方便啊。

**Sunday:** Compared to the Seer Strategist's ability to come and go as she pleases, this is nothing but a parlor trick.

> **星期日：**和戎韬将军来去无踪的本事一比，不过是些微末小道罢了。

**Sunday:** I've heard that Xianzhou folks are big on etiquette. Yet here I am, and you're still playing hide-and-seek. General, isn't that a bit discourteous?

> **星期日：**久闻仙舟人看重礼数，我已到了，将军却还是真人不露相，未免有些失礼了吧？

**Yao Guang:** Sorry... I have this old problem of suddenly disappearing. It's really something beyond my control.

> **爻光：**抱歉…我总会突然消失这个老毛病，实在是身不由己。

**Yao Guang:** As for the reason, I'm afraid I can't disclose it. Please forgive my rudeness.

> **爻光：**至于原因，恕我无法透露。失礼之处，还请多多包涵。

**Yao Guang:** Or, if you're truly that curious, why not use your "tuning" and find out?

> **爻光：**又或者，你要是真的好奇，也可以用「调律」自己来寻找答案啊？

**Sunday:** You know I won't do that.

> **星期日：**你知道我不会这么做的。

**Sunday:** Besides, I couldn't even if I wanted to right now.

> **星期日：**何况，眼下我也办不到。

**Yao Guang:** Couldn't?

> **爻光：**办不到？

**Sunday:** At this moment, the crowd's thoughts are crashing over me like a tidal wave.

> **星期日：**此时，四周人群的思想就像海潮一样涌向我。

**Sunday:** I can't block out all this mental noise. Just keeping my own thoughts from getting tangled up in everyone else's is hard enough.

> **星期日：**我没法隔绝这些杂讯，光是束缚自己的念头不让它们卷入别人的意识，就已精疲力竭了。

**Yao Guang:** I see. So that's why, when you were speaking with Miss Himeko earlier, I accidentally intruded on your "private conversation."

> **爻光：**原来如此，所以刚才和姬子小姐会谈时，我闯入了你们俩的「私人通信」，也是这么回事吧。

**Sunday:** My "tuning" is spiraling out of control. This mask... is distorting my abilities.

> **星期日：**我的「调律」正在失控。这副面具…扭曲了我的能力。

**Yao Guang:** ...I see it differently. The loss of control is temporary. That mask is amplifying your power.

> **爻光：**…我却不这么看，失控是暂时的。那副面具在增强你的力量。

**Sunday:** How is this possible? This "tuning" power is bestowed by the Harmony. How could the Elation meddle with THEIR authority?

> **星期日：**这怎么可能？「同谐」赐下的调律之力，「欢愉」如何能擅动祂的权能？

**Yao Guang:** Perhaps the Elation simply poured more "fuel" into this divine authority?

> **爻光：**也许，「欢愉」只是为这份权能注入了更多「燃料」也说不定呢？

**Yao Guang:** In my humble opinion, this mask binds the Supplicant to all beings of Planarcadia, drawing strength from those who share the same Path here for your use.

> **爻光：**依我愚见，这张面具将谒者和二相乐园的众生联为一体，抽汲此间相同命途的力量，供你们使用。

**Yao Guang:** That's why you're hearing others' thoughts surge toward you like a tide... Your "tuning" is steadily expanding, and you've become the center of this melody.

> **爻光：**这就是你能听到别人的心声如海潮般涌向你的原因…你的「调律」正在不断扩张，你已成了这曲旋律的中心。

**Sunday:** You sure gave me quite the "gift", general.

> **星期日：**将军还真是送了我一份「重礼」啊。

**Sunday:** But why would such a precious gift be placed on my face...? I need an honest answer.

> **星期日：**只是如此贵重的礼物，为何却偏要扣在我的脸上…我需要一个坦诚回答。

**Yao Guang:** You want to know the reason? Didn't I explain it already?

> **爻光：**理由？我之前不是解释了吗？

**Sunday:** Your reasons about me make sense, but what I really want is your answer... about yourself.

> **星期日：**与我有关的理由，言之成理。但我更想听的是，与你自己有关的答案。

**Sunday:** General of the Yuque who sees into the future... why not wear the mask yourself and personally step into the fray with its power?

> **星期日：**洞察未来的玉阙将军，为何不把这副面具扣在自己脸上，靠着它给予的力量，亲身入局？

**Yao Guang:** Words can deceive, but only "tuning" lays the truth bare.

> **爻光：**语可以虚饰伪装，唯有「调律」才能揭开真相。

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Sunday, why not use your enhanced "tuning" to feel the weight of that answer for yourself?

> **爻光：**星期日先生，不妨用你被增强过的「调律」力量，亲自体会这个答案的重量吧。

[Recap] Yao Guang's bestowed Spiritus was Kālacakra Skyplume, granting her the Eye of Īśvara to read the flow of fortune. Since arriving in Planarcadia, she had seen every passerby marked by ominous signs, and knew a world-ending catastrophe is approaching. If the mask was worn, Yao Guang's divinations would permeate into this realm, sealing the fate of all living beings in the arcadia within a death trap. Fortunately, for now, there still remained a glimmer of hope.

**Sunday:** Well then... pardon my intrusion, Lady Yao Guang.

> **星期日：**那就冒犯了，爻光大人。

**Yao Guang:** Ah... so this bird on my shoulder is the manifestation of the "tuning's" resonance?

> **爻光：**啊…这只停在我肩上的鸟儿，就是「调律」感应之力的具现？

**Sunday:** Exactly. And these symbols I see floating above people's heads...

> **星期日：**正是。而我能看到的这些人头顶的符号……

**Sunday:** ...Would be the "Weal and Woe Signs" you mentioned, general?

> **星期日：**也就是将军所说的「吉凶签」？

**Yao Guang:** That's right. To become an Arbiter-General, one must undergo three ceremonies: the "Rainbow Ordainment," the "Reignbow's Gaze," and the "Bestowal of Spiritus." Only then can one truly take up the mantle and live up to the title.

> **爻光：**没错。成为帝弓天将，须得经历「天虹封典」、「帝弓垂眸」，以及「受赐威灵」三道仪式，如此方能算得上接掌符节，名实相称。

**Yao Guang:** When I was appointed as the Yuque's general, I was bestowed with the Reignbow Spiritus called "Kālacakra Skyplume," which represents the vision of the Hunt among the seven aspects of the Reignbow: a symbol of foresight, verdict, and self-discipline.

> **爻光：**我接任玉阙将军时受赐的帝弓威灵，名唤「时轮天雉明王」于帝弓七相中代表「巡猎」的眼界，是预视、决断与自制的象征。

**Yao Guang:** Thanks to that, I gained the "Eye of Īśvara," which allows me to read the flow of destiny.

> **爻光：**正是靠着它，我才拥有了判读气运的「观自在眼」。

**Yao Guang:** Since the day I arrived on this world, every person I've seen bore a crimson "Woe Sign" above their head without exception. Within days at most, a catastrophic disaster will befall this place.

> **爻光：**自抵达这个世界之日起，我举目所见，往来行人皆头顶赤红的「凶签」，无一例外。最迟不过数日，将有一场灭顶灾变到来。

**Sunday:** So, that red glow rising from the edge of the city is...?

> **星期日：**所以，我看到城市远方升起的这蓬红光是……？

**Yao Guang:** The blazing light formed by the convergence of all those "Woe Signs" in Planarcadia.

> **爻光：**那是二相乐园所有人的「凶签」汇聚而成的光焰。

**Sunday:** In that case, general, it's all the more reason for you to wear the mask. Once your sight into the future is strengthened, you won't need to fear the "deathly omen" in your readings.

> **星期日：**这么说来，将军更应佩戴面具才对——您洞知未来的能力一经增强，便不必畏惧占下结果中的「死兆」了。

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Sunday, have you heard the diviners' iron law? "Never divine the same matter twice"?

> **爻光：**星期日先生，卜者有一条「事不二卜」的铁律，不知你是否听过？

**Sunday:** In Penacony, diviners also warn clients beforehand: You can't ask for another spread just because you dislike the result. There's a reason for that, isn't there?

> **星期日：**匹诺康尼的占卜者用卡牌占卜前也会告诫顾客，不能因为占卜结果不顺心意，就要再来一次。这其中难道有什么讲究？

**Yao Guang:** Divining the future is, in essence, a form of observation and calculation. It's an interference with the future. Each reading drives the murky future toward a definite outcome, a collapsed quantum state, even if that outcome isn't to one's liking.

> **爻光：**占卜未来，本质上是一种观测与计算，是对未来的干涉。而每一次测算，都会将混沌不清的未来推向某个确定的坍缩结果，即便这结果并不为人所乐见。

**Yao Guang:** To be honest, when I set out for Planarcadia, I had fully intended to personally participate in the Phantasmoon Games.

> **爻光：**不瞒你说，动身前往这二相乐园时，本座确实做好了亲自踏入「幻月游戏」的打算。

**Yao Guang:** But the moment I laid hands on the mask, I understood the consequences of wearing it. Once this miraculous item is donned, the "Eye of Īśvara" would transcend mere sight and permeate throughout this realm. The Kālacakra Skyplume would predict the future of all beings at tens of billions of instances per second.

> **爻光：**但在面具入手的瞬间，我已明了佩戴它的后果一一这件奇物一旦加身，「观自在眼」将超脱目光所限，遍入此界，时轮天雉明王会以每秒百亿次计判读众生的未来。

**Sunday:** ...You're saying that, simply by your observation, our future would become fixed?

> **星期日：**…你是说，只是靠你双眼观察，我们的未来就会被决定？

**Yao Guang:** Indeed, just like how the Wisdomwalker expounded the way of destiny, anchoring the fated moments.

> **爻光：**不错，就像遍智天君阐演天道，锚下命定的时刻。

**Yao Guang:** If I wore the mask, I would become the Nous of Planarcadia, and I would "personally anchor" a destiny that drives us all into a bottomless darkness. We will become locked in a dead end with not a shred of hope for salvation.

> **爻光：**如果戴上面具，我就成了这二相乐园之上的博识尊，我会「亲眼」将诸位推入黑暗的命运，囚锁在命定的死局里，再无一点拯救的希望。

**Yao Guang:** Close one. I passed the trial. There's still a sliver of hope in this round.

> **爻光：**好险，我通过了考验。这一局，尚有一线生机。

[Recap] Having foreseen the fall of an Arbiter-General, Sunday was curious as to what still motivates Yao Guang to save Planarcadia. Yao Guang believed fortune and misfortune were intertwined, that this was the perfect place to gamble against the gods.

**Sunday:** ...Now that makes me even more curious.

> **星期日：**…这就更让我感到好奇了。

**Sunday:** Pardon my bluntness, but the fate of billions in Planarcadia never had much to do with you, General. Your encounter with the Express and the IPC was merely a chance meeting.

> **星期日：**恕我失礼，二相乐园亿万生灵的吉凶命运，和将军本来也没什么关系。列车、公司与你只是一面之缘。

**Sunday:** You are a messenger of the Xianzhou. Since you foresaw the omen — that "an Arbiter General will fall here" — you could have boarded your ship and left at any time...

> **星期日：**你是仙舟的使者，既然预见了「一位帝弓天将在此殒落」的征兆，你就该随时登船撤离……

**Sunday:** Why choose to stay and help us? To help this world break free of this deathtrap?

> **星期日：**为什么要选择留下来帮助我们，帮助这个世界挣脱眼前的死局？

**Yao Guang:** Because... I am guided by great compassion. Every life is precious beyond measure...

> **爻光：**那是因为…本座心怀大爱，慈悲为怀，只因每一条生命都弥足珍贵——

**Yao Guang:** Such diplomatic pleasantries won't fool the former master of Penacony like yourself, would they?

> **爻光：**这些个场面话，想来骗不了你这位匹诺康尼前主公吧？

**Yao Guang:** Then, how about this reason? The Astral Express has distinguished itself time and again against the Destruction, and bears an irreplaceable duty for the future. I can't just sit idly and watch you face annihilation...

> **爻光：**那你看看这个理由怎么样？星穹列车抗击「毁灭」，屡建奇功，于未来肩负不可或缺的重任，我绝不能坐看诸位陷入灭顶之灾。

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Yao Guang:** *sigh* Give me a break. I really am terrible at all this diplomatic talk.

> **爻光：**唉，饶了我吧，本座真的不擅长外交辞令啊。

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Sunday, what makes you think that everything I've done is just to save Planarcadia?

> **爻光：**星期日先生，是什么让你觉得，我所做的一切，只是为了拯救二相乐园？

**Sunday:** What?

> **星期日：**什么？

**Yao Guang:** "Weal is where Woe lurks. Woe is where Weal leans." The good fortune of some often births the ill fate of others, and a dead end for someone may hide an opportunity for another.

> **爻光：**「福兮，祸之所伏；祸兮，福之所倚」。一部分人的吉运，往往导致另一些人的凶命；而一部分人面临的死局，也许潜藏着另一群人的生机。

**Yao Guang:** This treacherous land where life and death turn again and again, where fortune flips to misfortune, is, in my eyes, the perfect board for a game of wits.

> **爻光：**这个生死百转、否泰错变的凶危之地，在我眼中恰恰也是绝佳的博弈之地。

**Sunday:** A game with whom?

> **星期日：**与谁博弈？

**Yao Guang:** ...With the gods.

> **爻光：**…与神。

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Sunday, have you heard of the "Battle of the Fanghu" thirty years ago?

> **爻光：**不知星期日先生是否听过三十年前的「方壶血战」？

**Sunday:** I've heard rumors of that war, but not the details. General, are you suggesting Planarcadia is now facing dangers just as perilous as the Xianzhou Fanghu was back then?

> **星期日：**我确实听过一些战争的传闻，但对细节所知不多。将军是想说，此刻的二相乐园，就和当年的方壶仙舟一样凶险？

**Yao Guang:** You got that right.

> **爻光：**你没猜错。

[Recap] During the Battle of the Fanghu, Yao Guang witnessed countless soldiers and a living planet struck down by The Hunt's Lux Arrow. She realized that even something as mighty as an Aeon could be swayed by human will.

**Yao Guang:** Thirty years ago, the Abundance Axis broke our lines and invaded the Fanghu homeland. The abominations awakened a living demonic star, intending to sink the Xianzhou Fanghu.

> **爻光：**三十年前，丰饶孽物的联军撕开阵线，杀入了方壶本土。孽物们唤醒了一颗活化的妖星，意欲击沉方壶仙舟。

**Yao Guang:** With no other options, the Alliance staked everything on my junior soror's plan... to operate the astronomical observation device, the Cloudpeer Telescope, to signal the Reignbow for aid, using THEIR Lux Arrow to strike down the demon star.

> **爻光：**联盟无计可施，遂兵行险着，采纳了我师妹提出的计划，操纵观星器具「瞰云镜」向帝弓发射信号求援，俟其光矢击落妖星。

**Yao Guang:** Whoever operated the Cloudpeer Telescope would not return. It was to be a one-way trip.

> **爻光：**而负责操作「瞰云镜」的那人将有去无回，十死无生。

**Sunday:** So your junior soror...?

> **星期日：**莫非将军的师妹……？

**Yao Guang:** You guessed wrong. The one who offered his life was my master, who taught me divination. It was Master Jingtian, then the Yuque's Master Diviner, and the only high official authorized to operate that device. He accepted his fate and ventured to the Fanghu.

> **爻光：**您猜错了。那位自愿成仁者，是教我卜筮之道的师父。时任玉阙太卜的竟天先生，是唯一有权限操作那件器物的重臣。他接受天命，前往「方壶」.

**Yao Guang:** Going with him into certain death were Yueyu, the previous Yaoqing General who guarded the Cloudpeer Telescope, and the Verdant Knights under her command.

> **爻光：**与他一同踏入死地的，还有彼时护卫「瞰云镜」的前代曜青将军月御，以及她所率领的青丘军士卒。

**Yao Guang:** I was responsible for assessing the entire plan's feasibility and mending its gaps. I activated the Deca-Light Reflection Barrier, calculating and casting lots again and again.

> **爻光：**而我负责勘定整个计划的可行性，补足其缺漏。我启用「十方光映法界」，一次次计算，一次次推占。

**Yao Guang:** I watched as the "starlight" representing countless possibilities flickered within the reflections — faint, wavering — until the vast shadow of a pondering god swallowed them whole, and they vanished into the abyssal dark at the edges of the universe.

> **爻光：**我目睹着代表众多可能性的「星光」于法界中闪烁微弱明灭，终至被一位神明沉思的巨影吞没，消失在天渊深邃的黑暗里。

**Yao Guang:** In that moment, I understood that I had cemented my master and countless soldiers into an inescapable doom. When the Lux Arrow fell, they would perish along with the enemy and that demonic star... all reduced to ashes.

> **爻光：**至此，我明白，我已将师父和万千军士的性命砌进了无从逃脱的死路。光矢坠下时，他们将与敌人…还有那颗妖星一同灰飞烟灭。

**Sunday:** You sacrificed so many comrades just to ensure the enemy's fall?

> **星期日：**你们牺牲了许多战友，只为确保敌人的死亡？

**Yao Guang:** To Penaconians, that must seem incredibly cruel, right?

> **爻光：**在匹诺康尼人听来，这样的事情一定很残忍吧？

**Sunday:** ...As someone born in an era of peace, I have no right to judge. Perhaps the founders of Penacony could understand the necessity.

> **星期日：**诞生在和平年代的我无权置评。也许匹诺康尼的创立者们能理解其中的无奈。

**Yao Guang:** Cruel or not, it was my duty. And so I had to witness, and remember their sacrifice.

> **爻光：**无论残忍与否，这都是我的职责。因此，我也必须见证并铭记他们的牺牲。

**Yao Guang:** When I saw the Lux Arrow descend, I realized the Xianzhou Alliance had actually directed an Aeon's actions.

> **爻光：**在目睹光矢坠下的那一刻，我意识到，仙舟联盟竟然牵引了一位星神的行动轨迹。

**Yao Guang:** This realization illuminated the mission of my remaining years. In this cold, merciless cosmos, when you're trapped in despair, an Aeon's intervention is often the only hope.

> **爻光：**这个念头照亮了我余生的使命。在这冰冷无情的银河里，要想从绝望死局中争得一线生机，星神的介入往往是唯一的希望。

**Yao Guang:** We are not ants, helpless beneath the celestial spheres as we simply watch the stars turn. Instead, THEIR trails were forged by us, who offered up a portion of our insignificant destiny.

> **爻光：**我们并非目视天体运行却无能为力的蝼蚁。是我们献出自己微不足道的命运的一部分，铸就了祂们的轨迹。

**Yao Guang:** Even beings as mighty as the Aeons can be pulled by our will!

> **爻光：**伟大如星神，也可以被我们的意志所牵引！

[Recap] Yao Guang wanted to draw the Elation into her hexagram and shake out a Great Weal for the cosmos's future: an answer beyond the Finality. And at Graphia Academy, the arcadia's first catastrophe was about to erupt.

**Sunday:** ...Is this what you want to do in Planarcadia?

> **星期日：**…这就是你想在二相乐园做的事？

**Yao Guang:** Indeed. I will seize those "starlights of possibility" I once buried with my own hands. Just like the Battle of the Fanghu thirty years ago: an Arbiter-General will fall, and an Aeon will descend.

> **爻光：**不错。我要握住那些曾被我亲手埋葬的「可能性的星光」，一如三十年前的方壶之战，一位天将陨落，一尊星神垂迹。

**Yao Guang:** I will forge the destined death into wagers and game pieces, and invite the god who delights in meddling with destiny to sit at my table.

> **爻光：**我要将命定的死兆铸为算筹，我要邀请那位钟情拨弄命运的神明入局。

**Yao Guang:** This time, I will invite THEM into my calculations... to draw a Great Weal for the cosmos's future!

> **爻光：**这一次，我要请祂…入我卦中，为银河的未来摇出大吉之签！

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Sunday, surely you can understand this mad idea of mine, having once reached the divine throne of Order yourself?

> **爻光：**星期日先生，这一念狂想，曾登临「秩序」神座的你，想必也能够理解吧？

**Sunday:** Our common ground goes far beyond "mad ideas."

> **星期日：**…我们的共同点，绝不止「狂想」这一处。

**Sunday:** But I was reviving a god who already lay dead and buried in the infinite dark. General... how can you be sure that the most capricious Aeon would be led by your fancy?

> **星期日：**只是，我复苏的是一位在深渊中瞑目已死的神明。将军又怎么能确定，那位最善变的星神愿意被你的狂想牵引？

**Yao Guang:** Have you heard this myth about the Elation?

> **爻光：**你听说过这样一则「欢愉」的神话吗？

**Yao Guang:** They say Aha once climbed to the highest bough of the Tree of Existence. Gazing out, THEY saw stars turning coldly like machines, and the meaning of everything yielded to nihility... Only the cry of a newborn made THEM burst into laughter.

> **爻光：**传闻阿哈曾攀上存在之树的高枝，他放眼四望，只见星辰冰冷运转如机器，一切的意义让位于虚无…唯独一名婴儿降生的啼哭却引来祂的哈哈大笑。

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Sunday, answer me: why did Aha laugh?

> **爻光：**星期日先生，请回答我：阿哈因何而欢笑？

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** I think it's because THEY finally saw an audience arrive at this long-empty stage we call the universe.

> **星期日：**我想是因为，祂终于看到一位观众来到了宇宙这个空旷许久的舞台前。

**Yao Guang:** Well said! You're way funnier than you look!

> **爻光：**答得好啊！你比外表看起来要幽默多了嘛！

**Yao Guang:** As divination teaches: "Heaven creates forty-nine paths. Humans find the one unseen." WE are the greatest variable this blind, mindless universe can produce.

> **爻光：**卜筮之道有云「天衍四九，人遁其一」。我们是这恒盲无智的宇宙所能创造出的最大的变数。

**Yao Guang:** The universe and the gods set riddles for us, and we shall give THEM a surprise... An answer beyond the Finality.

> **爻光：**宇宙与众神为我们设谜，而我们将会给他们意外…一个「终末」以外的答案。

**Yao Guang:** Ah, looks like it's about time...

> **爻光：**啊，时间差不多了……

**Sunday:** Don't tell me you're disappearing without a goodbye again, general?

> **星期日：**莫非，将军又要不告而别了？

**Yao Guang:** No. This time, we will set our eyes on the same place and carve a sliver of possibility out of destiny with our own "sight."

> **爻光：**不，这一次，我们俩要将目光投向同一个地方，「亲眼」从命运中凿开一线可能。

**Sunday:** Could you be talking about... the school where Himeko and the others went?

> **星期日：**您说的该不会是.姬子她们前往的那所学校？

**Yao Guang:** Yes. The crimson light there pierces the sky today, marking it as a locus of Woe. The arcadia's first calamity will erupt there.

> **爻光：**嗯，今日那儿赤光烛天，正是一处「凶位」。乐园的第一场灾变，将会从那儿爆发。

**Sunday:** I should've asked you to give them a few "secret strategies" before they left...

> **星期日：**早知道该在他们出发前，求将军给他们个「锦囊妙计」啊……

**Yao Guang:** Forget it. The stuff in those pouches is all harmless banter anyway... We should let them off the hook.

> **爻光：**算了，反正写的都是些无关痛痒的说笑打趣…就饶了他们吧。

### Go to Graphia Academy and continue the adventure

> *Himeko says we need to stop SparxiCon by using water from Dovebrook in the "Worldbreaker Waves" painting to clear Sparxie's memetic infection. Better find that imagenated landscape mural fast before we all turn into monkeys... or worse, Sparxie!*

[Recap] According to news reports, two more Supplicants had appeared, their masks shaped like a raccoon and a crow. At Graphia Academy, the SparxiCon announced by Sparxie was about to begin.

**Poofy:** Hello everyone, welcome back to Fulwish TV's special news segment, Arcadian Morning Report! I'm your host, Poofy!

> **噗噗：**大家好，欢迎回到满愿电视台《乐园早知道》特别新闻栏目！我是主持人噗噗！

**Snowie:** Hi everyone, I'm your host, Snowie!

> **雪雪：**大家好，我是主持人雪雪！

**Poofy:** It's been three hours since our first Supplicant, Miss Sparxie, announced her entry into the Phantasmoon Games. The performance she announced to all her fans, SparxiCon, is also about to begin!

> **噗噗：**距离第一位谒者火花小姐宣布加入幻月游戏已经过去了三小时。她向所有粉丝们宣告的演出——「火花大会」也即将到来！

**Snowie:** How curious! Planarcadia's day–night cycle was completely scrambled in ancient times! Graphia Academy's location just happens to be bathed in light...

> **雪雪：**真让人好奇啊，二相乐园的昼夜节律早在古老的时代里就被完全打乱了！绘世学院所处的区域恰好是个光照充沛的地方……

**Snowie:** So, in broad daylight, what kind of fireworks show does Miss Sparxie have in store for us?

> **雪雪：**所以，在明晃晃的白昼里，火花小姐到底要为我们呈现一出怎样的烟花表演呢？

**Poofy:** Who knows? Maybe she'll blow the school sky-high in a blaze of fireworks? After all, at the Phantasmoon Games fifteen years ago, Graphia Academy also...

> **噗噗：**谁知道呢？该不会是要把学校炸个烟花吧？毕竟十五年前的幻游戏，也是在绘世学院发生了——

**Snowie:** Hey, senior, you can't joke about that! Dear viewers, our apologies! Let's move on to news about other Supplicants.

> **雪雪：**哎，前辈，这样的玩笑可开不得啊！各位观众，非常抱歉！下面让我们来播报有关其他谒者的消息。

**Poofy:** According to the Department of Aberration Defense's staff, during a routine inspection of the Phantasmoon Courtyard, two additional mask shapes manifested on the altar.

> **噗噗：**根据异常防御部的工作人员提供的消息，在例行检查幻月秘庭时，他们发现神坛上显现出另外两张面具的形状。

**Snowie:** In other words, two more Supplicants have appeared!

> **雪雪：**也就是说，又有两位谒者出现了！

**Poofy:** Although none of them have publicly announced entry into the games yet, the altar's masks mean someone, somewhere, has quietly donned the "Masks of Elation."

> **噗噗：**虽然截至目前为止，尚未有任何谒者公开宣布加入游戏，但神坛上的面具代表有人在某处悄然佩戴上了「欢愉假面」。

**Poofy:** As for the two masks, Department of Aberration Defense staff describe one as a raccoon and the other as some kind of bird...

> **噗噗：**关于这两副面具，按照异常防御部工作人员的描述，其中之一是浣熊的形象，另一副似乎是某种鸟类…

**Snowie:** A raccoon mask, huh... Hard not to think of the Astral Express that just arrived in Planarcadia.

> **雪雪：**浣熊面具啊…不免让人想起最近抵达二相乐园的星穹列车。

**Snowie:** Will the Nameless from the Astral Express join the Phantasmoon Games? I hear they've even invited them to SparxiCon as special guests!

> **雪雪：**星穹列车的无名客会加入这次的幻月游戏吗？听说「火花大会」也邀请了他们作为嘉宾呢！

**Poofy:** Hmm! Will it be a miraculous show formed out of the collaboration between Supplicants, or a head-to-head showcase between two famed contenders?

> **噗噗：**嗯！这到底会是一场谒者间联手创造的奇迹秀，还是两位名动一方的谒者大展身手，彼此对抗呢？

**Snowie:** Countless streamers are already live, broadcasting the scene at the venue, let's cut to the feed now...

> **雪雪：**此时已经有不计其数的主播正在直播现场盛况，让我们把画面转到现场的直播中！

[Recap] You, Himeko, and Sparkle arrived at Graphia Academy and use the Banana memetic virus to slow down the Sparxification. You needed to find the imagenated landscape mural hidden by Sparxie and to use the "waves" to cleanse the entire academy.

**"Sparxified" Lady:** Ah! Look, it's the Nameless from the Astral Express!

> **「火花化」的女生：**啊！快看，是星穹列车的无名客来了！

**Broadcast:** One girl, a thousand faces! A thousand faces, one girl! Welcome to the SparxiCon live broadcast!

> **广播：**一人千面，千面一人~欢迎来到「火花大会」直播现场！

**Broadcast:** Dear lovely fans, please be patient ♥! SparxiCon is about to begin!

> **广播：**请各位可爱的粉丝耐心等待~♥，「火花大会」即将正式开场！

**Sparkle:** This horror stream is about to end. Time for you all to log off.

> **花火：**这场恐怖直播马上就要结束了。你们也该下课了。

**"Sparxified" Lady:** That woman has Miss Sparxie's face! What a pathetic imitation!

> **「火花化」的女生：**这个女人竟然长着火花大人的脸！好拙劣的模仿！

### Go to the old classroom

> *Now that the SparxiCon livestream is over, the chaos Sparxie caused is finally settling down. The Supplicants' first battle ends in a win for the Express Crew. But now... I've got more questions than answers. Who is Sparxie, and what really happened in that tragedy fifteen years ago?*

[Recap] Himeko once participated in the previous Phantasmoon Games as a Supplicant. During that time, a Supplicant harboring immense malice brutally murdered many students and citizens, leaving bloody handprints at each crime scene. The media dubbed them the "Herald of Death." Graphia Academy's Dean Ryusuke entered the classroom, saying his daughter "Himeko" was already killed in that year's "Bloodstained Games."

You retrace your steps back to that eerie twilight classroom.

**Himeko:** This place... It's like nothing ever happened. The ghosts are gone.

> **姬子：**这儿…好像什么都没发生过。那些幽灵消失了。

- ▸ What exactly happened back then?

**Himeko:** Fifteen years ago, during the last Phantasmoon Games, I participated as a Supplicant.

> **姬子：**十五年前，上一次「幻月游戏」期间，我作为谒者参与了这场游戏。

**Himeko:** I once believed that all it took for a Supplicant to win was to show the people a magnificent "miracle," and then I'd have an audience with the Aeon of Elation...

> **姬子：**我本以为，谒者们只要向人们展示足够壮丽的「奇迹」，就能赢得最终的胜利，觐见「欢愉」星神……

**Himeko:** How foolish... how naive I was. Not everyone wants to obtain everyone's Wishpower in a positive way.

> **姬子：**那时候的我，多么天真可笑。并非所有人都想以展示美好的方式激起人们的愿力。

**Himeko:** On July 22nd, year 1984 of the Arcadian Calendar, a Supplicant harboring immense malice stepped into Graphia Academy...

> **姬子：**乐园历1984年7月22日，一名怀有巨大恶意的谒者踏入绘世学院……

**Himeko:** He slaughtered twenty-two students who had stayed behind after school, then went on to Dovebrook District, where one hundred and forty-six innocent civilians were either killed or wounded.

> **姬子：**他杀害了二十二名放学后留校的学生，之后又一路来到鸽川区，造成了一百四十六名无辜市民死伤。

**Himeko:** That killer moved like a deranged artist, leaving corpses imprinting his signature mark... the Bloody Bloom. The media dubbed him... the Herald of Death.

> **姬子：**这个杀人魔就像一名恐怖的作画者，留下一路受害者的遗骸和签名般的杀人痕迹「绽放血手」。媒体称他为…「告死魔」。

**???:** It was a tragedy that the world could not forget. The Phantasmoon Games of that year came to be remembered as the "Bloodstained Games"...

> **？？？：**这是一桩无法遗忘的悲剧。十五年前的「幻月游戏」，因此被人们称为「血涂游戏」……

**???:** So many parents lost their children. So many people lost those dearest to them.

> **？？？：**许多人失去了自己的孩子，许多人失去了自己的至亲。

**???:** One of the victims... was my very own daughter. Upon my return, I stood before the grave, where she was laid to rest.

> **？？？：**其中一名受害者，是我的女儿。我在归来后，亲眼目睹了埋葬她的墓冢。

**???:** ...Her name was Himeko.

> **？？？：**她叫姬子。

- ▸ What!?
- ▸ Who are you?

**???:** Forgive me. Allow me to introduce myself. I am Ryusuke, the current dean of Graphia Academy.

> **？？？：**抱歉，请允许我自我介绍。我是隆介，绘世学院的现任校长。

**Ryusuke:** So, this lady who bears her name...

> **隆介：**所以，眼前这位顶着她名字的女士……

**Ryusuke:** ...Who exactly are you?

> **隆介：**…你到底是谁？

**Himeko:** Fifteen years ago, when I made up my mind to join this Phantasmoon Games, my father wasn't with me.

> **姬子：**十五年前，当我决心加入这场幻月游戏时，我的父亲不在我身边。

**Himeko:** Even as I fought for my life against the Herald of Death, my father was still off somewhere, wandering the cosmos.

> **姬子：**在我与告死魔对抗，命悬一线的时刻，我的父亲依旧不知道在银河哪儿游荡。

**Himeko:** Strictly speaking, ever since I can remember, his presence in my life has been as thin as the air in space...

> **姬子：**严格来说，打从记事起，他在我成长过程中的存在感就像太空里的空气一样稀薄……

**Himeko:** So, I would ask you who bears his name, who exactly are you?

> **姬子：**所以，眼前这位顶着他名字的先生，你又是谁？

## 175　The Indifference of Those Who Yet Live　生者对死者无动于衷

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Graphia Academy　|　角色：Skott; Ashveil; Kuchiba; Silver Wolf; Pearl; Evanescia; Caustide; Poofy; Snowie; "Narrator"; Azurewing; Blazeweaver; Dawnlance　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Discreet Charm of a Streamer Girl'' completed

### Go to Duomension City and continue the adventure

> *The highly anticipated SparxiCon is all over the front page. At the same time, more news is trickling in.*

[Recap] According to news reports, members of the Astral Express engaged Sparxie in an epic battle. A large number of casualties were discovered at Synwish Syndicate headquarters, including an IPC employee: Lyndon Skott.

**Poofy:** Welcome back to the Arcadian Morning Report special edition! We continue to bring you updates on the Phantasmoon Games...

> **噗噗：**欢迎回到《乐园早知道》特别新闻栏目！我们将持续为您跟踪幻月游戏的进展——

**Snowie:** The "SparxiCon" hosted by Supplicant Sparxie came to an abrupt end less than an hour after it began.

> **雪雪：**谒者火花宣称举行的「火花大会」在开启不到短短一小时后，匆匆落下了帷幕。

**Poofy:** Under the watchful eyes of the entire network, the Nameless from the Astral Express went head-to-head with streamer Sparxie in a spectacular showdown!

> **噗噗：**在全网注视下，星穹列车的无名客与网络主播火花展开一场精彩的战斗！

**Poofy:** Unbelievable! I used to think all of (Trailblazer)'s fight scenes in Director Reca's latest film Holy Grail: Stay Night were just special effects... Turns out he/she's got real moves!

> **噗噗：**我的天！我本以为在芮克导演的新作《圣杯/今晚留下来》里，开拓者的战斗场面全是特效——现在看来，她/他的实力没掺半点假！

**Snowie:** We'll continue following the Astral Express's activities and bring you...

> **雪雪：**接下来本台将持续聚焦星穹列车的行动，并安排——

**Poofy:** Breaking news!

> **噗噗：**插播一条紧急新闻！

**Poofy:** Around 4:00 PM today, the Security Bureau uncovered multiple corpses at the Synwish Syndicate's headquarters in Dovebrook District! Initial reports suggest violent infighting among gang members as the cause.

> **噗噗：**今天下午四时许，治安局在鸽川区帮派「共愿帮」的总部中发现大量尸体！死因疑似是帮派内讧导致的火并。

**Poofy:** Even more shocking: an IPC employee was found dead at the scene! According to the investigation, the victim was identified as...

> **噗噗：**更让人震惊的是：现场还有一名公司人员遇害！根据调查确认，死者名为——

**Poofy:** Lyndon Skott...

> **噗噗：**林登·斯科特……

### Go to Dovebrook District and continue the adventure

> *A sudden tragedy has gripped Planarcadia in fear and set the factions on edge again. Can the Phantasmoon Games go on as planned, or is everything about to spiral out of control?*

[Recap] Kuchiba asked Ashveil to investigate the Synwish Syndicate massacre. Based on the bloody handprints at the scene, Ashveil deduced that the "Herald of Death" was back at it again. But Kuchiba insisted the culprit was executed five years ago.

**Kuchiba:** Lyndon Skott, age 34. Field operative in the Strategic Investment Department. Ring any bells, Mr. Ashveil?

> **朽叶：**林登·斯科特，34岁，战略投资部的外派专员。你认识他吗，不死途先生？

**Ashveil:** Never heard of him. What was someone from the IPC doing here?

> **不死途：**没见过。公司的人为什么会死在这儿？

**Kuchiba:** Word is, the Synwish Syndicate recently got hold of a mask. Maybe Mr. Skott came to negotiate for the Phantasmoon Games, and got caught in the fallout.

> **朽叶：**听说共愿帮最近得到了一张面具。也许这位斯科特先生是为了幻月游戏的事上门商谈，遭到了殃及。

**Kuchiba:** Apologies for interrupting your work, Mr. Ashveil.

> **朽叶：**很抱歉，打断了你手头的活儿，不死途先生。

**Ashveil:** Wouldn't call it an "interruption"... the mess at Graphia Academy cleared itself up. But this...

> **不死途：**算不上「打断」，绘世学院的麻烦已经自行解决了。但这里……

**Kuchiba:** Initial assessment: thirty-three Synwish Syndicate members — leader Polwave included, along with second-in-command Caustide — were murdered in the same time frame.

> **朽叶：**初步判断，头目池波、二号人物恶水在内的三十三名「共愿帮」成员，在同一时间段内遭人杀害。

**Ashveil:** ...Caustide.

> **不死途：**…恶水。

**Kuchiba:** The victims show clear signs of trauma and fractures, along with massive blood loss. Exact cause of death still pending further autopsy.

> **朽叶：**死者身上有明显的外伤和骨折，还伴有大量失血。具体死因还要进一步鉴定才有结论。

**Ashveil:** These stylized handprints... We both know what they mean.

> **不死途：**这些作画般的手印，我们俩应该都不陌生。

**Ashveil:** "Bloody Bloom." Only one person ever used that as their calling card...

> **不死途：**「绽放血手」，会使用这签名般的杀人手法的，只有那个家伙……

**Ashveil:** The Herald of Death is back.

> **不死途：**「告死魔」又回来了。

**Kuchiba:** Your suspicion isn't without merit, but that guy was executed five years ago.

> **朽叶：**你的怀疑并非没有道理，但那家伙已在五年前被处决了。

**Ashveil:** Executed? Who witnessed it? Was the execution broadcast to the public?

> **不死途：**处决？谁见到了？有没有向全民直播他的死刑过程？

**Ashveil:** Who can prove he's dead? Who's to say you didn't let him out, to rig this entire game!?

> **不死途：**谁能保证他死了？谁又能保证他不是被你们放了出来，只为了掌控这场狗屁游戏？！

**Kuchiba:** You've heard the rule, haven't you? "No one can enter the Phantasmoon Games twice."

> **朽叶：**你应该听说过这条规则，「一个人无法两次加入幻月游戏」。

**Kuchiba:** So let me give you a piece of advice: Don't let rage gnaw away at your reason. It's bad for your health.

> **朽叶：**所以，我想给你一个忠告：不要让愤怒啃噬理智，那样对身体不好。

**Kuchiba:** The case's info is in the dossier, get to work. The Department of Aberration Defense isn't paying us to bum around.

> **朽叶：**案件信息都在卷宗里，干活吧。异常防御部的顾问费可不是白给的。

[Recap] Ashveil's friend Caustide was killed in a copycat crime imitating the "Herald of Death." To bring justice to the dead, Ashveil accepted Pearl's proposal and joined the Phantasmoon Games to prevent things from spiraling out of control.

**"Narrator":** Rage sparked quietly in the detective's chest, and not even the wind from Dovebrook could dispel the metallic tang of blood clinging to his nose.

> **「旁白」：**愤怒悄然在侦探心中燃起，鸽川的风也没法吹散萦绕在他鼻尖的血腥气味。

**"Narrator":** When he first heard the Synwish Syndicate had obtained the mask, a grim premonition stirred within him. But he never expected it to be the omen of a friend's death.

> **「旁白」：**在听闻「共愿帮」得到面具时，侦探心中曾升起一丝不祥的预感。但他从未能料到，这竟然会是自己和一位朋友死别的征兆。

**Ashveil:** Mister N... He's no longer one of us.

> **不死途：**老白，他不再是我们的朋友了。

**Ashveil:** Now he's... just a body, another nameless casualty buried beneath a headline.

> **不死途：**他现在…只是一具尸体，一个被埋在报纸新闻里不起眼的名字。

**Ashveil:** People only care about the stars twinkling above, about the epic tales of the eternal conflicts among the cosmos's great figures.

> **不死途：**人们在意的只有天上星辰的明灭，在意的永远是银河里大人物们生死相搏的壮绝故事。

**Ashveil:** Compared to all that, who would mourn a small-time thug from a petty gang?

> **不死途：**和这些相比，一个小小帮派分子的死，又算得了什么呢？

**"Narrator":** What should we do next?

> **「旁白」：**接下来该怎么办？

**Ashveil:** ...I've been slacking off for far too long. It's time to make a choice.

> **不死途：**…懒散了如此之久，我也该做个决定了。

**Pearl:** Mr. Ashveil? If you're calling now, may I assume you've accepted my offer?

> **真珠：**不死途先生？这次拨来通化，我是否可以假定：你会接受我的提议。

**Ashveil:** You are right, Miss Pearl.

> **不死途：**你说的没错，真珠小姐。

**Ashveil:** I'll join the games, assuming you still need me, of course.

> **不死途：**我会加入「游戏」——当然，是在你仍然需要我的前提下。

**Pearl:** My offer still stands.

> **真珠：**对你，我的提议始终有效。

**Pearl:** Just like fifteen years ago, the same omen returns. There are signs the Phantasmoon Games are spiraling out of control again. Mr. Ashveil, I ask for your strength to steer these Aeonic games back on course.

> **真珠：**与十五年前一样，幻月游戏再次出现了失控的征兆。不死途先生，还请你为导正这场星神游戏献出一份力量。

**Ashveil:** All I have to offer... is a heart set on revenge.

> **不死途：**我能献出的，只有一颗复仇的心。

**Pearl:** Today, thirty-three citizens of Planarcadia and one IPC colleague perished under mysterious circumstances... Someone is imitating the Herald of Death's methods, attempting to interfere with the games.

> **真珠：**今天，三十三位二相乐园的居民和一名公司同仁死于非命…有人模仿「告死魔」的手段，试图染指这场游戏。

**Pearl:** Only victory can bring solace to the fallen.

> **真珠：**能告慰这些死者的，只有胜利。

**Ashveil:** No, what consoles the dead...

> **不死途：**不，能告慰死者的……

**Ashveil:** ...is only justice.

> **不死途：**…唯有公义。

### Go to Duomension City and continue the adventure

[Recap] Silver Wolf said aether editing and imagenesis were arts that share the same origin, both drawing their power from the Elation. She offered to trade the secret of how top-tier hacker "Zero" created an independent plane in exchange for one of Pearl's masks. The deal was struck, and Pearl candidly admitted she had been deliberately guiding Silver Wolf all along.

**Silver Wolf:** What's the matter? Found a better candidate? Don't I even make the shortlist?

> **银狼：**怎么，找到了更合适的代理人选？不考虑一下我吗？

**Pearl:** This is a game shaped by the most capricious Aeon, surprises and twists are its very nature. It's only right I choose the strongest players.

> **真珠：**这是最善变的星神所划定的游戏，变化和意外是它的本质。我理应挑选最有能力的人加入这场游戏。

**Pearl:** Even so, I have to ask: what makes you so sure...

> **真珠：**即便如此，我仍感到好奇：是什么让你十足确信——

**Pearl:** That I'd hire a wanted criminal to fight in the Phantasmoon Games... instead of tossing you into an IPC cell?

> **真珠：**我会雇佣一名通缉重犯，作为代理人出站幻月游戏…而不是把你丢进公司的监狱里？

**Silver Wolf:** Throw me in prison?

> **银狼：**把我丢进监狱里？

**Silver Wolf:** But, the Strategic Investment Department in Penacony clearly extended an olive branch to us Stellaron Hunters, no?

> **银狼：**可是，战略投资部在匹诺康尼，明明冲着我们星核猎手抛出过橄榄枝啊？

**Silver Wolf:** Changed your minds already? Tsk. You suits sure turn faster than a page in a paperback.

> **银狼：**你们改变主意了？唉，生意人，翻脸真是比翻书还快。

**Silver Wolf:** So let's cut the fluff: Give me a mask. Call it your first strategic investment in us. What do you say?

> **银狼：**直说了吧，给我一张面具。把它当作对我们的第一笔战略投资，如何？

**Pearl:** Understanding comes first. Then the investment. Tell me, how do you plan to earn my trust?

> **真珠：**先有理解，再有投资。回答问题：你想如何取信于我？

**Silver Wolf:** Apologies in advance. Before I came to meet you, I broke into your collection room. I know what you've been working on... "Imagenate"...

> **银狼：**真是对不住。在来见你之前，我潜入过你的藏品室，知道你在研究什么…「幻造」——

**Silver Wolf:** Tell me, have you heard of Zero? Greatest hacker and most feared player in all of Punklorde?

> **银狼：**对了，你听说过朋克洛德最伟大的骇客，最可怕的玩家「零号」吗？

**Pearl:** Some say she's an Emanator of Elation, while others say she enters the "Dark Zone" created by Mythus as if it were her own backyard.

> **真珠：**有人说她是「欢愉」的令使；有人说她进入迷思创造的「黑域」就跟回自家的后花园一样。

**Pearl:** She caused Punklorde's data meltdown and infected IPC branches in twenty-eight segmenta, isolating them from each other...

> **真珠：**她导致了朋克洛德的数据大雪崩，还感染了星际和平公司在二十八个星域的分部，导致彼此失联……

**Pearl:** Most information about Zero is half true, half false, much like the rumors surrounding you Stellaron Hunters. But one thing is certain: Zero suffered a major downfall and is now a prisoner of the IPC.

> **真珠：**关于「零号」的消息大都半真半假，和你们星核猎手何其相似。不过，有一条消息确凿无疑：「零号」栽了个大跟头，现在已经是公司的阶下囚了。

**Silver Wolf:** It's obvious she left a deep impression on you... traumatic, even. In that case, you should also know that aether editing and imagenesis stem from the same technological source, or... art.

> **银狼：**看得出来，她给你们留下的心理阴影还挺重的。那你应该知道，「以太编辑」和「幻造」是系出同源的技术，或者…艺术。

**Pearl:** I know. I've studied it.

> **真珠：**我知道，也研究过。

**Silver Wolf:** Ha, how could I forget? You stole Zero's tech to make that lousy cash-grab game Aetherium Wars, of course you'd know all this...

> **银狼：**哈，我怎么把这个茬给忘了呢？你们偷走了「零号」的技术，做出了那个蹩脚游戏《以太战线》捞钱，又怎么会不知道这些呢……

**Silver Wolf:** But you corporate moneybags will never comprehend it, the fleeting, ever-changing power of the Elation.

> **银狼：**不过你们这些公司的钱袋子永远也无法理解它——「欢愉」瞬息万变的力量。

**Silver Wolf:** Zero once tampered with a parameter of reality, creating an independent realm that belonged solely to her... just like that other one...

> **银狼：**「零号」曾更动过一个现实参数，创造出属于她的独立位面，就像那个……

**Pearl:** ...Just like Graphia.

> **真珠：**…和绘世一样。

**Pearl:** Are you willing to trade the secrets of imagenesis?

> **真珠：**你愿意交易「幻造」的秘密？

**Silver Wolf:** I can think about it.

> **银狼：**我可以考虑。

**Pearl:** Deduction: you may be lying. I've heard that those who join the Stellaron Hunters have long since abandoned the Path they once walked.

> **真珠：**推演：也许你在说谎。我听说：加入星核猎手的人早已放弃了旧日所行的命途。

**Silver Wolf:** Who knows? You're in the arcadia of elation, wouldn't it be a waste not to test your luck?

> **银狼：**谁知道呢？踏上这座欢愉乐园，不碰一把运气，像话吗？

**Pearl:** ...

> **真珠：**……

**Pearl:** Deal, aether hacker. And to seal it with confidence, let me show you a little trick of the trade.

> **真珠：**成交，以太骇客。为了给这笔生意增添一些信心的保障，请允许我向你展示一点小小的骇客技巧。

**Silver Wolf:** ...A hacking trick?

> **银狼：**…骇客技巧？

**Pearl:** Not exactly professional, negotiating while soaked in a bathtub.

> **真珠：**泡在浴缸里和对手谈生意，可说不上尊重啊。

**Pearl:** I took one of your data slices. This will help us have a more honest conversation.

> **真珠：**我取走了你的一个数据切片。这样有利于我们之间更真诚的沟通。

**Silver Wolf:** H-How did you do that...?

> **银狼：**你，怎么做到的……？

**Pearl:** Because I've studied you, including that cyber battle between you and that genius Screwllum. I even logged into all seventy-six of those banned accounts.

> **真珠：**因为我研究过你，包括那场与天才螺丝咕姆之间的网络攻防战，我还登录过那被封禁的七十六个账号。

**Pearl:** I scattered just enough data breadcrumbs online for you to sniff out, leading you to the Ashen Detective. I even let you stroll through my collection room, to explore as you pleased.

> **真珠：**是我在网络上布设了足够多的数据和档案任你翻看，引导你找到了那位不死神探。也是我允许你进入我的藏品室，随意游览。

**Pearl:** Miss Silver Wolf, a word of advice: Don't treat that mask like some Ten Stonehearts investment in a Stellaron Hunter.

> **真珠：**银狼小姐，我有个建议：不要把这副面具当成石心十人对星核猎手的骰子——

**Pearl:** Just think of this deal as something private... between you and me.

> **真珠：**把这笔生意当成是我和你的私人交易…就好。

### Go to Phantasmoon Courtyard and continue the adventure

[Recap] Eight masks manifested within the Phantasmoon Courtyard. Evanescia remarked that far too many Supplicants with unclear intentions had come this time. Still, you fought well in your first battle. Your promise had been fulfilled, and the calamity foretold in the oath was wiped clean. A crow swooped in, catching Evanescia's attention.

Meanwhile, in the Phantasmoon Courtyard...

**Evanescia:** What the heck? I just blinked and suddenly half the Supplicants are wearing masks.

> **绯英：**怎么回事？只不过一眨眼的功夫，突然就有那么多谒者戴上了面具。

**Evanescia:** I was hoping this show would get better. Streamers clashing with cosmic heroes, a flashy brawl, without anyone getting hurt. Wouldn't that have been nice?

> **绯英：**一直期待这出戏能往好的方向发展。网络主播和银河英雄的碰撞，一番大战热热闹闹，论结果无人受伤，这不是很好吗？

**Evanescia:** But now we've got a crow, a shell, a cat, a wolf... how many more mystery guests are crashing this stage?

> **绯英：**但是现在，乌鸦、贝壳、猫咪、恶狼…来意不明的角色未免有些太多了吧？

**Evanescia:** And this thing... when did it sneak in?

> **绯英：**还有，这个东西…又是什么时候混进来的？

**Evanescia:** In the end, everything always spirals into the abyss.

> **绯英：**到最后，事情总会不可避免地滑向深渊啊。

**Evanescia:** But you're the rare exception. Your first battle wasn't bad for a greenhorn protagonist.

> **绯英：**不过，你倒是个例外，作为「主角」，初出茅庐的第一仗打的不赖嘛。

**Evanescia:** Since you came out on top, let's wipe the slate clean on that doom-filled oath.

> **绯英：**既然胜出了，你誓言中的劫数就这么一笔勾销吧。

**Evanescia:** Who knows if the owners of those masks will become your friends or opponents?

> **绯英：**也不知道这些面具的主人，会成为你的对手还是盟友呢？

**Evanescia:** Or maybe, one of them will bring about that terrifying future I saw in my dreams...

> **绯英：**还是说，他们中的某一个会带来我梦见的那个可怕的未来——

**Evanescia:** Little one, how'd you find your way here?

> **绯英：**小家伙，你是怎么找到这里的？

**Evanescia:** The Supplicants in this Phantasmoon Games really caught me by surprise!

> **绯英：**这次「幻月游戏」的谒者，还真让我感到意外啊！

*(Visitor Verification)*

(Sparxie has become a visitor to the Express. You might come across Sparxie when you return to the Express)

(Yao Guang has become a visitor to the Express. You might come across Yao Guang when you return to the Express)

### Post-Mission

*(Investigate the Daybreak Squadron in Dovebrook District, optional)*

**Red-Haired Youth:** Who would've thought that the Phantasmoon Games has barely begun, and those villains plotting against Planarcadia are already getting restless.

**Red-Haired Youth:** Time for the Daybreak Squadron to save the world.

**Yellow-Haired Girl:** Just got the new mechatron, and my Smiling Fist is itching for action.

**Blue-Haired Youth:** Hmph, a violent girl will always be a violent girl. To defeat powerful enemies, what truly matters is having a cool head and a sharp mind.

**Blue-Haired Youth:** Under precise analysis, any evil can be shattered with a snap of a finger.

**Red-Haired Youth:** Oh yeah? Well, I think the greatest power comes from friendship! Courage! And happiness!

**Red-Haired Youth:** With the four of us working together, Daybreak Squadron will be unstoppable!

**Yellow-Haired Girl:** Ah, speaking of which... Boss, Gray just messaged saying the director's pushing us to get back on set again. If we don't hurry back, it's instant noodles for dinner tonight.
